Conclusion: 领域专科化基础模型结合统计风险控制为准确乳腺CNB诊断和可靠报告生成提供了有前景的路径。

Abstract: Breast core needle biopsy (CNB) is central to breast cancer diagnosis yet remains challenging because limited tissue sampling, lesion heterogeneity, and subtle morphologic overlap can obscure subtype distinctions. We developed CorePath, a breast-specialized multimodal pathology foundation model fine-tuned from PRISM using 7901 paired CNB whole-slide images and diagnostic reports from two centers. Evaluated across six CNB cohorts and two public breast pathology benchmarks without task-specific retraining, CorePath consistently outperformed PRISM across cancer detection, invasion assessment, and histological subtyping. It achieved weighted area under the receiver operating characteristic curves (AUCs) of 0.9526-0.9735 for five-class CNB histological subtyping across private centers. On public benchmarks, CorePath outperformed leading pathology foundation models, achieving the highest weighted AUCs of 0.7780 for BCNB invasive carcinoma subtyping, 0.8178 for BRACS lesion stratification, and 0.8252 for BRACS fine-grained classification. In report generation, CorePath reduced the overall non-breast hallucinations from 30.1% to 2.8%, demonstrating improved domain fidelity after breast-specific adaptation. CorePath-CRG further combined conformal subtype-confidence gating with Learn-Then-Test risk control to enable selective report release, subtype-level fallback, and deferral. CorePath-CRG achieved zero non-breast hallucinations among released outputs and showed the strongest overall performance in pathologist-validated LLM-based Evaluation Scores and quantitative report-generation metrics across most centers. These results demonstrate that domain-specialized foundation models with statistical risk control offer a promising approach for accurate breast CNB diagnosis and reliable report generation.

</details>


### [29] [DiverseDiT++: Quantifying, Analyzing, and Promoting Representation Diversity in Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03082)
*Binglei Li,Mengping Yang,Zhiyu Tan,Xiaomeng Yang,Zhizhong Huang,Junping Zhang,Hao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了Diffusion Transformers（DiTs）中块级表示多样性的动态，提出加权多样性评分（WDS）这一新指标来衡量各block间表征差异，并发现表示多样性与生成质量强相关（Pearson r=-0.869）。基于此发现，提出DiverseDiT++框架，通过长残差连接和表示多样性损失显式促进多样化表示学习，在ImageNet 256×256和512×512上取得一致性能提升和收敛加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DiTs在视觉合成中取得了显著进展，且REPA等外部预训练编码器被引入以进行表示对齐，但DiTs内部表示学习的底层机制仍缺乏理解。作者旨在揭开DiTs内部表征演化的规律，寻找影响合成质量的关键因素。

Method: (1) 提出加权多样性评分（WDS）指标，量化不同block之间表征差异；(2) 通过大规模实验系统分析不同设置下DiTs内部表示的演化与影响；(3) 基于WDS强相关性发现，提出DiverseDiT++框架，包含长残差连接（diversify输入表示）和表示多样性损失（鼓励各block学习不同特征）。

Result: WDS与log(FID)的Pearson相关系数达到-0.869，在不同设置、模型规模和训练阶段下均展现强相关性。DiverseDiT++应用于不同骨干网络和多种模型尺寸时，在ImageNet 256×256和512×512上均取得一致的性能提升并加速收敛。

Conclusion: 块间表示多样性是DiTs中有效表示学习的关键因素；WDS可作为反映模型性能的指标和模型优化的原则性指导；DiverseDiT++通过显式促进多样化表示学习，为DiTs提供了普适的性能增强框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have enabled remarkable progress in visual synthesis, benefiting from their superior scalability. To facilitate DiTs' capability of capturing meaningful internal representations, recent works such as REPA incorporate external pretrained encoders for representation alignment. However, the underlying mechanisms governing representation learning within DiTs remain poorly understood in the community. To this end, this paper first presents a systematic analysis of the representation dynamics of DiTs via quantifying the diversity of block-wise representations. Specifically, we introduce a novel metric, termed the Weighted Diversity Score (WDS), to measure the representational discrepancies across different blocks. Through extensive investigations on the evolution and influence of internal representations under various settings, we reveal that representation diversity across blocks is a critical factor for effective representation learning in DiTs. More importantly, WDS exhibits a strong correlation with synthesis quality across diverse settings, model scales, and training stages (Pearson's $r=-0.869$ with $\log(\text{FID})$), suggesting its potential as an indicator to reflect model performance and a principled guide for model optimization. Based on this key finding, we propose DiverseDiT++, a novel framework that explicitly promotes diverse representation learning. Concretely, our method incorporates long residual connections to diversify input representations across blocks and a representation diversity loss to encourage blocks to learn distinct features. Extensive experiments on ImageNet $256\times256$ and $512\times512$ demonstrate that our DiverseDiT++ yields consistent performance gains and convergence acceleration when applied to different backbones with various sizes,...

</details>


### [30] [GSTEP: Global Spatio-Temporal Density-Driven Visual Token Pruning for Efficient Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03083)
*Mengjie Zhang,Qihui Zhu,Tao Zhang,Shuangwu Chen,Huihuang Qin,Yu Guo,Shenghao Ye,Zijian Wen,Yunpeng Hou,Dong Jin,Xiaobin Tan,Huasen He,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GSTEP（全局时空密度剪枝）框架，将视频建模为连续的时空信息流，通过全局token采样在保持视频理解性能的同时大幅降低VideoLLM的推理成本。该框架在剪枝75%视觉token的情况下保持了100.2%的平均性能，并实现1.17倍端到端加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VideoLLMs在长视频推理中因大量冗余的时空视觉token导致推理成本高昂。现有token剪枝方法多为段级局部剪枝方式，将视频分割为孤立片段并在各段内独立选择token，这种方式可能低估短但语义密集的片段，同时会丢弃在局部看似不显著、但全局视角下关键的token。因此需要一种全局视角的token剪枝方案。

Method: GSTEP将视频建模为连续的时空信息流，构建token级时空密度——该密度由两部分组成：一是通过平滑的中心帧级变化信号获得的连续时间密度，二是帧内空间密度。随后通过联合平衡信息密度与覆盖率的方式进行全局token采样，实现即插即用的剪枝框架。

Result: 在多个VideoLLM和公共基准上，GSTEP一致地实现了强准确率-效率权衡，并具有良好的跨模型架构和评估设置的泛化能力。在LLaVA-OneVision-7B上，剪枝75%的视觉token后，平均性能保持为原始的100.2%，并实现1.17倍端到端加速。

Conclusion: GSTEP通过全局时空密度建模和全局token采样，有效解决了现有段级局部剪枝方法在长视频理解中的局限性，在显著降低推理成本的同时保持了甚至略微提升了模型性能，为VideoLLM的高效推理提供了通用且可迁移的解决方案。

Abstract: Video large language models (VideoLLMs) achieve strong video understanding performance, but their inference remains expensive due to the large number of redundant spatio-temporal visual tokens in long videos. Existing token pruning methods alleviate this cost by reducing redundant tokens, yet most of them rely on segment-level local pruning, where videos are partitioned into isolated segments and tokens are selected independently within each segment. Such designs may under-preserve short but semantically dense segments and discard tokens that appear non-salient locally but remain critical from a global perspective. To address this issue, we propose GSTEP (Global Spatio-Temporal Density Pruning), a plug-and-play pruning framework that models video as a continuous spatio-temporal information flow. GSTEP constructs a token-level spatio-temporal density by combining a continuous temporal density, obtained from a smoothed centered frame-level change signal, with intra-frame spatial density, and then performs global token sampling by jointly balancing information density and coverage. Extensive experiments on multiple VideoLLMs and public benchmarks demonstrate that GSTEP consistently achieves strong accuracy-efficiency trade-offs and generalizes well across model architectures and evaluation settings. On LLaVA-OneVision-7B, GSTEP prunes 75% of visual tokens, preserves up to 100.2% of the original average performance across benchmarks, and achieves a 1.17 end-to-end speedup.

</details>


### [31] [SUV: Future Scene Understanding as Video Generation for End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03084)
*Yibo Yuan,Jiacheng Fu,Jiangtong Zhu,Yi Li,Jianhua Han,Meng Tian,Zhuohan Liu,Zhiwei Xiong,Hang Xu,Jianwu Fang,Jianru Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SUV，一个统一的端到端驾驶框架，将未来场景理解构建为视频生成任务，利用预训练视频基础模型同时预测未来外观、语义、相对深度和实例级动态，并通过联合视频-动作注意力生成自车轨迹。在NAVSIM-v2和WOD-E2E基准上取得领先或具有竞争力的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端驾驶方法通过任务特定的头部和输出格式建模未来场景，缺乏可扩展性。作者希望探究视频生成能否作为统一的未来场景预测器，替代多个任务专用分支，从而提升模型的统一性和泛化能力。

Method: SUV基于预训练视频基础模型，将未来外观、语义、相对深度和实例级动态统一编码为视频流，由共享的视频专家处理，无需针对每种流配备专门的视觉预测头。通过联合视频-动作注意力机制，动作专家关注所有未来流的潜在表示以生成自车轨迹。仅使用单个前置摄像头，无需候选轨迹选择。

Result: SUV直接预测全部四种未来流，结构化未来监督和直接未来流访问可提升轨迹规划分数。在NAVSIM-v2两套分割上均超越多种最新方法，navtest上EPDMS达91.0，navhard上达36.9；在长尾WOD-E2E基准上RFS达7.94，具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 实验证明视频生成可以作为端到端驾驶中统一的未来场景理解预测器，结构化的未来监督和直接的未来流访问对轨迹规划有积极贡献，展示了该框架在不同难度基准上的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: End-to-end driving requires a coherent understanding of future scenes, yet existing methods model these scenes using task-specific heads and output formats, with limited scalability. Can video generation instead provide a shared predictor? We introduce SUV, a unified end-to-end driving framework that casts future Scene Understanding as Video generation using a pretrained video foundation model. SUV models future appearance, semantics, relative depth, and instance-level dynamics as video streams with a shared video expert, without stream-specific visual prediction heads. Through joint video-action attention, the action expert attends to the latent representations of all future streams and generates the ego trajectory. Experiments show that SUV directly predicts all four future streams, while controlled ablations show that structured future supervision and direct future-stream access yield higher trajectory planning scores. With only a single front camera and no candidate-trajectory selection, SUV outperforms a broad set of recent state-of-the-art methods on both NAVSIM-v2 splits, achieving 91.0 EPDMS on navtest and 36.9 on navhard. On the long-tail WOD-E2E benchmark, SUV achieves a competitive RFS of 7.94.

</details>


### [32] [Channel-wise Dynamic Knowledge Distillation via Adaptive Sample Generation for Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03100)
*Ping Li,Chenhao Ping,Jie Song,Mingli Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应样本感知的通道动态知识蒸馏（ASCD KD）方法，通过两阶段设计（自适应样本生成+通道动态蒸馏）解决视频/图像分类中KD的两个关键限制，在5个基准数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏（KD）方法在压缩大动作识别模型时存在两个关键局限：1）依赖固定输入样本导致冻结教师与学生模型之间的特征对齐次优；2）对所有通道施加统一蒸馏强度，未考虑不同通道在捕获不同知识（如运动速度或幅度）方面的重要性差异及其随训练epoch的变化。

Method: 提出ASCD KD方法，包含两个阶段：1）自适应样本生成模块：通过最小化以各层通道质心频率差异加权的特征损失得出样本梯度，利用其语义生成更新样本，并对频率特征施加高斯掩码保留关键运动信息；2）通道动态蒸馏模块：基于样本梯度和特征频率指导学生在生成样本上训练。样本按周期更新而非每epoch更新以保证效率。

Result: 在三个视频基准（UCF101、Kinetics-400、Something-Something-v2）和两个图像数据集（CIFAR-100、ImageNet）上的大量实验表明该方法取得了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: ASCD KD通过自适应生成样本和通道级动态蒸馏强度，有效解决了固定样本和均匀蒸馏强度带来的局限性，在动作识别及图像分类任务上均实现了优于现有KD方法的性能。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation (KD) offers a promising yet underexplored path for compressing large action recognition models. However, existing KD methods suffer from two key limitations: 1) reliance on fixed input samples leads to suboptimal feature alignment between the frozen teacher (larger model) and the learnable student (smaller model), and 2) applying a uniform distillation strength for all channels fails to account for their varying importance in capturing distinct knowledge (e.g., motion tempo or magnitude) across training epochs. This motivates us to develop an Adaptive Sample-aware Channel-wise Dynamic (ASCD) KD approach, which operates in two stages. First, we use an adaptive sample generation module to create updated samples by incorporating semantics from sample gradients, which are derived by minimizing a feature loss weighted by channel centroid frequency differences at each layer. Meanwhile, crucial motion-related details are preserved by applying a Gaussian mask to frequency features. Second, we employ a channel-wise dynamic distillation module to train student on these generated samples, guided by sample gradients and feature frequencies. For efficiency, samples are updated periodically rather than per epoch. Extensive experiments on three video benchmarks (UCF101, Kinetics-400, Something-Something-v2) and two image datasets (CIFAR-100, ImageNet) demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance of our method. Code is available at https://github.com/mlvccn/ASCD_KD_Action.

</details>


### [33] [Double Down on Defense: Strengthening Deep Perceptual Hashes against Evasion Attacks without Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03101)
*Bangjie Sun,Nayoung Kim,Mun Choon Chan,Jun Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DualShield是一个即插即用的防御框架，通过结合匹配时的随机平滑和发布时的输入加固，在不重新训练或修改底层模型的情况下，提升现有深度感知哈希的健壮性，使其能够抵抗对抗性扰动和图像变换攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度感知哈希被广泛用于近似重复图像匹配、版权保护和视觉搜索等领域，但对抗性扰动可以使攻击者绕过匹配机制。现有防御手段需要重新训练或修改底层模型，成本高昂且不灵活。

Method: DualShield结合两个机制：（1）匹配时的随机平滑，对扰动的参考-查询图像对聚合决策，提供认证鲁棒性；（2）发布时的加固，在参考图像发布前添加经过优化的不可感知扰动。两个机制协同提供认证和实验层面的鲁棒性保证。

Result: DualShield实现了约0.3的认证ℓ2半径，保证该半径内的查询扰动无法逃逸匹配。在8种深度感知哈希和3个数据集上，针对自适应白盒、黑盒和图像变换攻击，显著降低了攻击成功率，同时保持了较低的碰撞率。

Conclusion: 通过改进匹配过程和发布前的参考图像加固，可以在无需昂贵重训练的前提下增强深度感知哈希的鲁棒性，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Near-duplicate image matching is crucial for trust and safety, provenance verification, copyright enforcement, and large-scale visual search. Modern platforms increasingly rely on deep perceptual hashes, which map visually similar images to nearby representations despite common image transformations. However, adversarial perturbations can cause near-duplicates to evade matching. We present DualShield, a plug-in defense that improves the robustness of existing deep perceptual hashes without retraining or modifying their underlying models. DualShield combines matching-time randomized smoothing, which aggregates decisions over perturbed reference-query pairs, with publication-time hardening, which adds an optimized imperceptible perturbation to each reference image before publication. Together, these mechanisms provide certified and empirical robustness. DualShield achieves a certified $\ell_2$ radius of approximately 0.3, guaranteeing that query perturbations within this radius cannot evade matching. We further evaluate it against adaptive white-box, black-box, and image-transformation attacks. Across eight deep perceptual hashes and three datasets, DualShield substantially reduces attack success rates while preserving low collision rates. These results show that deep perceptual hashes can be strengthened without costly retraining by improving the matching procedure and hardening reference images before publication.

</details>


### [34] [FaithIR: Rethinking Infrared Image Super-Resolution from Perceptual Sharpness to Task Relevant Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03106)
*Axi Niu,Zhenguo Wu,Kang Zhang,Qingsen Yan,Jinqiu Sun,Yanning Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FaithIR，一个忠实红外超分辨率框架，通过patch级条件分支捕获全局热结构和结构信息，像素级恢复分支在结构引导下进行密集局部重建，提升机器感知任务（目标检测和语义分割）的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红外图像超分辨率（IISR）方法常产生人为纹理、过度锐化边缘和虚假高频细节，扭曲了真实热结构和语义信息，限制了下游机器感知任务的可靠性。

Method: FaithIR包含两个分支：patch-level条件分支捕获全局热结构和结构信息，pixel-level恢复分支在结构引导下进行密集局部重建。整个恢复过程直接在像素域中进行以保留红外特有的结构和任务相关信息。

Result: 在FLIR-IISR、M3FD和FMB数据集上的大量实验表明，FaithIR在重建保真度、跨数据集泛化能力以及目标检测和语义分割性能方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 保留忠实的红外结构比单纯追求感知锐度对可靠的机器感知更重要。

Abstract: Infrared image super-resolution (IISR) is important for downstream tasks such as object detection and semantic segmentation. Existing IISR methods often produce artificial textures, over-sharpened edges, and spurious high-frequency details that distort authentic thermal structures and semantic information. To address this issue, we propose FaithIR, a faithful infrared super-resolution framework for reliable machine perception. FaithIR consists of a patch-level conditioning branch that captures global thermal and structural information and a pixel-level restoration branch that performs dense local reconstruction under structural guidance. The entire restoration process is performed directly in the pixel domain to preserve infrared-specific structures and task-relevant information. Extensive experiments on FLIR-IISR, M3FD, and FMB demonstrate strong reconstruction fidelity, cross-dataset generalization, and superior performance in object detection and semantic segmentation. These results show that demonstrate that preserving faithful infrared structure preservations is more important for reliable machine perception than merely pursuing perceptual sharpness alone.

</details>


### [35] [A Unified Resolution-Conditioned Framework for Orthogonal Line-Scanning Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03107)
*Yiming Gong,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于秩增强线性注意力(RELA)的统一分辨率条件融合框架，通过特征级线性调制(FiLM)使单一模型适应不同狭缝宽度的激光线扫描显微镜各向异性分辨率恢复任务，在多种光学配置下达到34-40 dB PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激光线扫描显微镜能实现快速体积成像，但产生各向异性的横向分辨率。虽然正交线扫描提供互补方向信息可恢复近各向同性分辨率，但现有深度学习方法需要为每种光学配置分别训练单独模型，缺乏统一且可泛化的解决方案。

Method: 提出了基于秩增强线性注意力(RELA)的统一分辨率条件融合框架。具体包括：(1) FiLM条件化网络连续适应分辨力比；(2) 自适应RELA模块，用比率条件的多尺度深度卷积替代固定核秩增强，并采用可学习的注意力温度来调节选择性；(3) 基于物理可分离点扩散函数模型生成跨多种狭缝配置的训练数据，在48.3 dB准确度下验证。

Result: 模型在多种配置下达到34-40 dB PSNR；无条件多狭缝训练仅24.3 dB；单狭缝专家模型在其训练配置外损失4-9 dB；模型可平滑泛化到未见过的中间配置；消融实验显示FiLM解决配置歧义、全局线性注意力捕获长距离方向对应、自适应温度在近各向同性困境下额外提升2 dB。

Conclusion: 该统一框架能通过单一模型自适应多种光学配置的各向异性分辨率恢复，并具有良好的泛化能力，为线扫描显微成像的深度学习重建提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Laser line-scanning microscopy enables fast volumetric imaging but produces anisotropic lateral resolution. Orthogonal line scans provide complementary directional information that can recover near-isotropic resolution, yet existing deep-learning methods require a separate model for each optical configuration. We present a unified, resolution-conditioned fusion framework based on Rank Enhanced Linear Attention (RELA). Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) conditions the network continuously on the resolving-power ratio, enabling one model to adapt across slit widths. We further introduce Adaptive RELA, which replaces fixed-kernel rank enhancement with ratio-conditioned multi-scale depthwise convolutions and uses a learnable attention temperature to adjust selectivity with degradation severity. Training data spanning multiple slit configurations are generated using a physics-grounded separable point-spread-function model verified against measured optical data at 48.3 dB accuracy. The resulting model achieves 34-40 dB PSNR across configurations, whereas unconditioned multi-slit training collapses to 24.3 dB and per-slit specialists lose 4-9 dB outside their training setting. It also generalizes smoothly to unseen intermediate configurations without interpolation artifacts. Ablations show that FiLM resolves configuration ambiguity, global linear attention captures long-range directional correspondences, and adaptive temperature yields an additional 2 dB in the challenging near-isotropic regime, where complementary signals are weak.

</details>

*Jiakai Lin,Zijun Li,Guoyu Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态机器人AI框架，将基于加权拉普拉斯收缩（W-LBC）的无监督3D骨架提取与基于证据优先的语言模型微调相结合，实现对植物根系的可解释、数据高效的自动化表型分析。该框架从点云中提取骨架结构并计算定量形态学描述符，再通过监督微调GPT，使其在提供可测量证据的基础上进行自然语言推理，跨越12种植物物种验证了其在根系表型分析中的鲁棒性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植物根系表型分析对于理解地下结构、优化作物管理和提升农业可持续性至关重要，但传统方法在自动化、数据效率和可解释性方面存在不足。现有方法难以将定量的几何形态特征与语义理解无缝桥接，缺乏一个既能提供可测量证据又能进行自然语言推理解释的统一框架。

Method: 该框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 基于加权拉普拉斯收缩（W-LBC）的无监督骨架提取网络，从机器人3D传感平台获取的密集点云中生成高保真结构表示；(2) 从骨架图计算定量形态学描述符（根数、长度、分支角度、密度）以捕捉几何和拓扑特征；(3) 基于证据优先的GPT微调框架，利用自动生成的指令-响应对（每个样本先提供可测量证据再进行自然语言推理）进行监督微调，使模型将数值结构与语义意义关联。

Result: 实验表明该结构引导框架在12种具有多样化根系构型的植物物种上实现了稳健、可解释的推理，验证了无监督3D几何感知与大规模语言理解相结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过整合无监督3D几何感知与大语言模型理解，建立了可解释的机器人植物根系表型分析统一范式，桥接了定量分析与语义解释之间的鸿沟，为农业自动化与植物科学研究提供了新的方法论路径。

Abstract: Plant root phenotyping is fundamental to understanding below-ground structures, optimizing crop management, and improving agricultural sustainability. This paper presents a multimodal robotic AI framework that integrates 3D skeleton extraction with language-guided reasoning for interpretable and data-efficient root analysis. We develop an unsupervised skeleton extraction network based on Weighted Laplacian Contraction (W-LBC) to generate high-fidelity structural representations from dense point clouds captured by robotic 3D sensing platforms. Quantitative morphological descriptors, including root count, length, branching angle, and density, are computed from the reconstructed skeleton graph to capture geometric and topological characteristics. Building on these features, we introduce an Evidence-First language modeling framework that fine-tunes GPT as an interactive analytical chatbot using automatically generated instruction--response pairs. Each training sample provides measurable evidence before natural-language reasoning, enabling the model to ground interpretation in quantitative morphology. Through supervised fine-tuning, GPT associates numerical structure with semantic meaning, producing biologically consistent explanations of growth patterns and adaptive traits. Experiments show that the structure-guided framework achieves robust, interpretable reasoning across 12 plant species with diverse root architectures. By integrating unsupervised 3D geometric perception with large-scale language understanding, our approach bridges quantitative analysis and semantic interpretation, establishing a unified paradigm for explainable robotic plant root phenotyping.

</details>


### [37] [Adaptive Two-Stage Visual Token Pruning for Efficient Inference in Video-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03112)
*Paribesh Regmi,Qingshuang Chen,Chi Zhang,Heba Aly,Yelin Kim,Hongda Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对视频处理的两阶段自适应token剪枝策略：首先剪除冗余帧，然后在保留帧内根据视频内容自适应性确定token级剪枝比例。该方法是即插即用（post-hoc）的，无需额外训练，在10% token保留率下将视频字幕基准的准确率提升+7%，同时计算量TFLOPs降低95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在图像和视频理解中表现出色，但由于每张图像需要处理数千个token导致推理延迟高，限制了其在资源受限的边缘设备和实时监控应用中的部署。现有token压缩技术主要针对单张图像设计，无法利用视频的时间与帧间冗余；且通常使用固定的统一剪枝比例，忽略了不同视频内容中冗余程度的差异，导致关键信息可能被丢失。

Method: 提出两阶段自适应token剪枝策略：(1) 第一阶段剪除视频中冗余的帧；(2) 第二阶段在保留帧内进行token级剪枝。关键创新是第二阶段的剪枝比例并非固定值，而是通过分析token嵌入的相关性结构来量化冗余程度，据此自适应地确定每个视频的剪枝比例。该方法完全post-hoc，无需额外训练。

Result: 在视频字幕基准上，以10%的token保留率实现+7%的准确率提升，同时将计算量TFLOPs减少95%。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过在帧级和token级两个层面进行内容自适应的冗余消除，可以在不进行模型训练的情况下显著提升视频推理效率并保持甚至提升任务性能，为视频理解模型在边缘设备上的高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models excel at image and video understanding but suffer from high inference latency due to the need to process thousands of tokens per image, limiting their deployment on resource-constrained edge devices and in real-time surveillance applications. This challenge is further amplified in video processing, where multiple frames must be analyzed simultaneously. Existing token reduction techniques are largely developed for single-image inputs and therefore fail to account for the temporal and inter-frame redundancies present in video sequences. In addition, these methods generally rely on a fixed, uniform pruning ratio applied across all inputs, which is suboptimal because the degree of redundancy can vary significantly between different videos, necessitating content-dependent pruning levels to preserve critical information. To address these limitations, we propose a two-stage adaptive token pruning strategy specifically designed for video processing. In the first stage, we prune out the redundant frames, and in the second stage, token-level pruning is applied within the retained frames. Crucially, the pruning ratio in the second stage is determined adaptively based on the content of each video. This is achieved by analyzing the correlation structure of token embeddings to quantify redundancy, which is used to determine the ratio. Importantly, our method is entirely post-hoc and requires no additional training or fine-tuning, while achieving strong empirical gains; notably, it improves accuracy by +7\% on a video captioning benchmark at 10\% token retention, while reducing computation TFLOPs by 95\%.

</details>


### [38] [Non-Destructive Quantification of Urea Adulteration in Bovine Milk Using Transmittance Multispectral Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03113)
*Sharukshan Niranjan,Iresha Ranaweera,Tharindu Chandrarathne,Kalana Dissanayaka,Roshan Godaliyadda,Vijitha Herath,Parakrama Ekanayake,Janak Vidanarachchi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于多光谱成像（MSI）与回归框架相结合的定量检测牛奶中尿素掺假的方法。在受控密度条件下，使用自建的多光谱成像系统（12个光谱波段，365-940 nm）采集图像，通过多元线性回归和前馈神经网络建模，分别获得0.9599和0.9773的验证R²，证明了透射式多光谱成像用于快速、无损、准确定量尿素检测的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛奶中尿素掺假是重要的食品安全和健康问题，现有检测方法（实验室分析和光谱技术）多依赖专业设备、样品前处理或化学试剂，难以满足快速、低成本、常规筛查的需求，因此需要开发一种简便、经济且准确的定量检测手段。

Method: 采用自建的12波段（365-940 nm）多光谱成像系统，采集添加尿素并通过加水调节密度（比重1.032, 20°C）的牛奶样品的透射多光谱图像。使用多元线性回归（MLR）建立初始回归映射，再以前馈神经网络（FFNN）优化预测性能。

Result: 多元线性回归的验证R²为0.9599，前馈神经网络进一步将验证R²提升至0.9773，表现出较高的预测精度。

Conclusion: 透射式多光谱成像结合回归模型能够在受控密度平衡条件下实现牛奶尿素的准确、无损定量检测，具备作为牛奶质量快速筛查工具的应用潜力。

Abstract: Adulteration of bovine milk using urea remains a major food quality and health concern, motivating the development of rapid and quantitative screening tools. Conventional approaches, including laboratory-based analytical methods and spectroscopic techniques, have been used for urea detection; however, many remain less suitable for rapid, low-cost routine screening due to requirements such as specialized instrumentation, sample preparation, chemical reagents, or laboratory operation. This study introduces a pragmatic, cost-effective, accurate, and laboratory-validated MSI-based method for quantitative urea estimation under controlled density conditions using a multispectral-imaging-based regression framework. An in-house-built multispectral imaging system operating in twelve discrete spectral bands (365--940~nm) was used to acquire multispectral images of milk samples prepared with controlled urea addition and water for density balancing. Fresh milk was obtained on the day of image acquisition, and the specific gravity of the milk was verified to be 1.032 at 20°C using a hydrometer. Multiple linear regression provided an initial mapping with a high validation $R^2$ of 0.9599, while a feed-forward neural network further improved predictive performance with a validation $R^2$ of 0.9773. These results demonstrate the feasibility of transmittance multispectral imaging for accurate, non-destructive urea quantification under controlled density-balanced conditions, supporting its potential as a rapid screening approach for milk-quality assessment.

</details>


### [39] [SeCo-SBIR: Semantically Consistent Prompt Learning for Zero-Shot Sketch-Based Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03120)
*Long Hoang Dang,Tuan Nguyen Huu,Nguyen Minh Hieu,Tu Minh Phuong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SeCo-SBIR框架，通过文本引导的多模态提示学习和扰动一致性约束解决零样本素描图像检索中任务适配与零样本泛化之间的张力，在三个标准基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于CLIP的零样本素描图像检索（ZS-SBIR）通过提示学习进行任务适配时面临根本性矛盾：模型既要通过任务特定适配弥合素描-照片领域差距，又面临过拟合已见训练类别、削弱CLIP零样本泛化能力的风险。

Method: 提出了语义一致的提示学习框架（SeCo-SBIR），包含两方面核心机制：(1) 文本引导的多模态提示策略：学习可学习的提示向量经过CLIP文本编码器，通过可学习的耦合函数将中间表示逐层注入视觉编码器，借助文本编码器从大规模语言监督中学到的抽象类别级语义知识，将可迁移的语义知识直接注入视觉通路；(2) 基于扰动的语义一致性约束：通过非对称InfoNCE目标函数将适配模型与冻结的CLIP参考分支对齐（扰动输入进入冻结分支，干净输入进入可训练分支），锚定学习表示到CLIP的泛化特征空间。此外结合轻量适配器和包含三元组损失、NT-Xent和分类项的多目标损失。

Result: 在三个标准ZS-SBIR基准的类别级、泛化级和跨数据集设置下均达到了最先进的结果（SOTA）。

Conclusion: SeCo-SBIR通过从文本编码器注入可迁移的语义知识并利用冻结CLIP参考分支进行一致性约束，有效解决了任务适配与零样本泛化之间的根本矛盾，验证了语义一致性约束在保留CLIP泛化能力方面的有效性。

Abstract: Adapting CLIP for zero-shot sketch-based image retrieval (ZS-SBIR) via prompt learning faces a fundamental tension: the model must bridge the sketch-photo domain gap through task-specific adaptation, yet the added flexibility risks overfitting to seen training categories and eroding CLIP's zero-shot generalization. We present SeCo-SBIR, a semantically consistent prompt learning framework that resolves this tension from both sides. First, a text-guided multi-modal prompting strategy routes learnable prompt vectors through CLIP's text encoder and projects the resulting intermediate representations into the visual encoder at every layer via learnable coupling functions. Because the text encoder has already learned robust, abstract category-level semantics from large-scale language supervision, this mechanism injects transferable semantic knowledge directly into the visual pathway - adapting the model to the sketch-photo domain while inherently favoring generalization to unseen classes. Second, a perturbation-based consistency constraint addresses the residual overfitting risk from the learnable coupling functions by aligning the adapted model with a frozen CLIP reference branch using an asymmetric InfoNCE objective - augmented inputs feed the frozen branch while clean inputs feed the trainable branch - anchoring the learned representations to CLIP's generalizable feature space. Together with lightweight adapters and a multi-objective loss combining triplet, NT-Xent, and classification terms, SeCo-SBIR achieves state-of-the-art results on all three standard ZS-SBIR benchmarks across categorical, generalized, and across-dataset settings.

</details>


### [40] [Rectify Then Diffuse: Disentangling Concepts Before Denoising Trajectory Unfolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03135)
*Ning Zhu,An Chen,Mengfei Zhao,Juntao Xu,Jingze Liang,Boyuan Gu,Liang-Jian Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Rectify-then-Diffuse (RTD)，一种无需训练的文本到图像组合生成框架。该方法通过Soft-Overlap Disentanglement (SOD)和Isotropic Gradient Rectification (IGR)在去噪前一次性修正初始潜在表征，解决多概念生成中概念被遗漏或错误合并的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型在生成单一概念时表现良好，但在多概念组合生成时常常遗漏或错误合并概念。作者将这一失败追溯到去噪前的早期协调瓶颈：提示条件注意力可能在去噪开始前将不同概念分配到高度重叠的空间区域，导致后续去噪过程中注意力持续耦合。因此，作者主张将组合生成视为一个边界条件问题，而非反复控制演化轨迹。

Method: RTD包含两个核心组件：1) Soft-Overlap Disentanglement (SOD)：将归一化的概念图之间的重叠转换成一个可微分的、与布局无关的分离目标函数；2) Isotropic Gradient Rectification (IGR)：对SOD梯度进行归一化，并施加跨提示和初始条件一致尺度的有界隐空间位移。整个框架在标准去噪开始前一次性修正初始分配，无需训练。

Result: 在AE-Bench物体对子集上，RTD相比CO3将BLIP-VQA提升了45.8%，ImageReward提升了19.6%，同时运行速度快2.3倍，实现了最先进的组合保真度和稳健增益。

Conclusion: RTD通过将组合生成视为边界条件问题，在去噪前一次性修正初始潜在表征，有效解决了多概念扩散生成中的概念遗漏和错误合并问题，且无需训练、计算开销更低，验证了"去噪前修正"策略优于"去噪中反复控制"的思路。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models can generate individual concepts well, but they often omit or merge concepts incorrectly with multiple concepts. We trace these failures to an early coordination bottleneck: before denoising begins, prompt-conditioned attention may allocate different concepts to strongly overlapping spatial support, which can keep their attention coupled as denoising proceeds. This observation motivates treating compositional generation as a boundary-condition problem rather than repeatedly controlling the evolving trajectory. To this end, we propose Rectify-then-Diffuse (RTD), a training-free framework that rectifies the initial allocation once before standard denoising. Firstly, we propose Soft-Overlap Disentanglement (SOD), which converts normalized overlap between pilot concept maps into a differentiable and layout-agnostic separation objective. Secondly, we introduce Isotropic Gradient Rectification (IGR), which normalizes the SOD gradient and applies a bounded latent displacement with a consistent scale across prompts and initializations. Extensive experiments show that RTD achieves state-of-the-art compositional fidelity and robust gains. On the AE-Bench object pair subset, RTD improves BLIP-VQA by 45.8% and ImageReward by 19.6% over CO3 while running 2.3$\times$ faster. Code will be released at https://github.com/Z-yiwei/rectify-then-diffuse

</details>


### [41] [Frozen High-Resolution Inference for Cross-City Object Detection: An AI City Challenge 2026 Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03136)
*Jaeuk Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在AI City Challenge 2026 Track 6中，如何在气隙式Training-as-a-Service平台上仅通过聚合的COCO-style AP反馈进行跨城市目标检测。结果表明，在不更新任何参数的情况下，将704×704训练的检查点直接以1120×1120分辨率推理，聚合AP从0.3272提升至0.3654（+0.0382），超过所有微调配置，其中小目标相对增益最大、中目标绝对增益最大。论文还揭示了域内验证在聚合反馈下是不可靠的模型选择信号，并披露了所有配置的审计细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨城市目标检测要求在一个城市训练的检测器泛化到未标注的目标城市，但现实中常面临黑盒式训练服务、仅返回聚合指标、无法访问数据或日志等约束。作者希望在这种受限的Training-as-a-Service平台上，系统性地审计不同配置并找到提升聚合AP的有效手段，同时为"仅通过聚合反馈选择模型"这一缺乏验证信号的问题提供实证经验。

Method: 在气隙平台上对单一RF-DETR-Large检测器进行多组存档配置的审计分析。核心实验包括：(1) 冻结的1120×1120推理（对比704×704训练分辨率，输入像素增至2.53倍）；(2) 基于1120px输入的热启动微调（与推理运行使用了不同置信度阈值0.05 vs 0.01，因此被视为描述性结果）；(3) 灰世界归一化消融；(4) 矩形输入运行审计（发现意外使用了竖屏方向）。所有配置各提交一次，最优结果通过隐藏服务器上的聚合AP选出。

Result: 冻结的1120×1120推理取得了最高的聚合AP（0.3272→0.3654，+0.0382），无任何参数更新；小目标相对增益最大，中目标绝对增益最大。熵启动微调在域内验证AP从0.767升至0.789，但聚合AP仅达0.3470，表明域内验证无法可靠预测聚合目标。灰世界归一化对冻结-1120结果无明显影响；矩形配置因方向错误被审计排除。

Conclusion: 在聚合反馈约束下，更高分辨率推理（无需参数更新）是简单且有效的性能提升手段，其效果甚至优于微调。域内验证AP在聚合反馈场景下是误导性的模型选择信号。所有发现均为探索性、可审计的结果，仅针对聚合混合目标，不能断言目标城市的独立改进。

Abstract: Cross-city object detection requires a detector trained in one city to generalize to an unlabeled target city. In AI City Challenge 2026 Track 6, we analyze archived configurations of a single RF-DETR-Large detector inside an air-gapped Training-as-a-Service platform whose server returns only an aggregate COCO-style AP over a hidden mixture of source- and target-city images. Frozen 1120 x 1120 inference of a checkpoint trained at 704 x 704 achieved the highest aggregate AP among the evaluated configurations (0.3272 -> 0.3654, +0.0382) without any parameter update, with the largest relative gain on small objects and the largest absolute gain on medium objects, at 2.53x the input pixels. A warm-start 1120px fine-tuning recipe reached 0.3470 while its in-domain validation AP rose (0.767 -> 0.789), a caution that in-domain validation is an unreliable model-selection signal under aggregate-only cross-city feedback. Because that run's evaluation used a higher confidence threshold than the inference-only runs (0.05 vs. 0.01), we treat its score as a descriptive archived outcome rather than a controlled verdict on fine-tuning. Gray-world normalization did not meaningfully change the frozen-1120 result, and a rectangular run was found by audit to have used an unintended portrait orientation. We release verbatim platform commands, configuration snapshots, and an explicit evidence boundary for every claim. Each configuration was submitted once and the best was selected on the hidden server, so these are exploratory, audited findings about the aggregate mixture; they do not establish target-city-specific improvement.

</details>


### [42] [From Routes to Steps: Separating Semantic Progress from Local Execution in Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03143)
*Xiangyun Huang,Xiangchen Wang,Runfeng Lin,Yihao Xu,Kangyu Huang,Jiang Hengchen,Xiwang Dong,Lin Jiarong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Route2Step提出了一种将语义进度跟踪与动作生成解耦的VLN框架，通过显式的步骤级接口和E-SPA步骤对齐程序来监督进度状态，在R2R-CE上将SR从48.1%提升到55.3%，SPL从43.3%提升到48.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLM导航器通常仅通过下一步动作预测来同时监督进度跟踪和动作执行两个能力，导致当智能体偏离路线时，无法区分是选择了错误的子指令还是未能执行正确的子指令。这种歧义会使智能体在错误的进度状态下继续做决策，因此需要一个解耦的框架来分别监督这两个能力。

Method: Route2Step包含两个核心模块：(1) 指令分析模块(M_IA)从全局指令和视觉历史中预测语义进度状态；(2) 动作生成模块(M_AG)基于预测的状态和最近的观察生成局部动作块。为监督进度状态而无需要手动时间标签，提出了E-SPA步骤对齐程序，将子指令与路线的演示片段关联起来，从而对错误的进度估计提供状态监督。对在正确活动子指令下反复失败的rollout组保留直接的动作监督。

Result: 在R2R-CE基准上，Route2Step将成功率(SR)从48.1%提升到55.3%，将SPL从43.3%提升到48.2%，仅使用190K状态级矫正样本和11.5K直接动作监督状态。在真实世界室内和室外环境中的实验也验证了其实用性。

Conclusion: Route2Step通过解耦语义进度跟踪与动作生成，有效解决了VLN中进度跟踪错误与执行错误难以区分的歧义问题，显著提升了导航性能，并在真实环境中展示了实际应用价值。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) requires an agent to follow a route-level instruction by executing its constituent steps from egocentric visual observations. Existing VLM-based navigators typically supervise both capabilities through next-action prediction alone, making progress-tracking errors difficult to distinguish from execution errors. When an agent deviates from the route, a corrective action label may recover the next movement but does not indicate whether the agent selected the wrong sub-instruction or failed to execute the correct one. Consequently, the agent may continue making decisions from an erroneous progress state. To resolve this ambiguity, we propose \textbf{Route2Step}, a framework that decouples semantic progress tracking from action generation through an explicit step-level interface. The Instruction Analysis Module ($\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{IA}}$) predicts this state from the global instruction and visual history. Conditioned on the predicted state and recent observations, the Action Generation Module ($\mathcal{M}_{\mathrm{AG}}$) generates local action chunks. To supervise the progress state without manual temporal labels, E-SPA, a step-alignment procedure, associates sub-instructions with their corresponding portions of route-level demonstrations. These alignments enable state supervision for incorrect progress estimates, while direct action supervision is reserved for rollout groups that repeatedly fail under the correct active sub-instruction. On R2R-CE, Route2Step improves SR from 48.1\% to 55.3\% and SPL from 43.3\% to 48.2\%, using 190K state-level corrective samples while requiring only 11.5K directly action-supervised states. Experiments in real-world indoor and outdoor environments further demonstrate the practical applicability of Route2Step. The project page is: https://sisyphus-hxy.github.io/Route2Step/.

</details>


### [43] [CROSS: Cascaded Distillation and Dual-Constraint Grounding for Remote Sensing Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03147)
*Tingzhang Luo,Ruizhong Liu,Yichao Liu,Cheng Fan,Yu Liu,Jianyuan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CROSS是一个面向指代遥感图像分割任务的紧密集成范式，通过语言引导的级联蒸馏（LGCD）将SAM的几何亲和力蒸馏到VLM中间层以细化定位，并通过视角-空间对比学习（PSCL）构造硬负样本打破语义捷径，在RRSIS基准上取得了最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RRSIS方法依赖VLM与SAM的强预训练能力，但存在两个根本缺陷：(1) 架构弱耦合——单向信息流导致仅依赖粗粒度VLM提示、浪费SAM像素级结构引导，造成定位漂移；(2) 物体中心语义偏差——模型过度强调主导物体语义而忽视空间推理，这对空间描述敏感的RRSIS任务尤为不利。

Method: 提出CROSS紧密集成范式，包含两大核心模块：(1) 语言引导级联蒸馏（LGCD）——将SAM的几何亲和力作为软正则化器蒸馏到VLM中间层，注入稠密结构先验以细化定位；(2) 视角-空间对比学习（PSCL）——通过挖掘掩码过滤的欺骗性干扰项和空间-语言反事实作为硬负样本，施加跨锚定约束，打破语义捷径以强制真实逻辑一致性。

Result: 在RRSIS基准上的大量实验表明，CROSS取得了最先进的性能，并且在严重空间描述扰动下仍能维持精确的定位能力。

Conclusion: CROSS作为RRSIS的稳健新范式，通过架构级紧耦合和语义偏差校正，有效解决了定位漂移和空间推理不足的问题，为指代遥感图像分割提供了更可靠的技术路径。

Abstract: Referring Remote Sensing Image Segmentation (RRSIS) has achieved significant progress through the integration of VLMs and the Segment Anything Model (SAM). However, this progress largely relies on strong pre-trained capabilities, while leaving two fundamental limitations insufficiently addressed: (1) Architectural Weak-Coupling, where the unidirectional flow forces reliance on coarse VLM prompts and wastes SAM's pixel-level structural guidance, causing localization drift; and (2) Object-Centric Semantic Bias, where models overemphasize dominant object semantics while remaining insensitive to spatial reasoning crucial for RRSIS. Motivated by these observations, we propose CROSS, a tightly integrated paradigm for RRSIS. First, we introduce Linguistic-Guided Cascaded Distillation (LGCD) to bridge the architectural gap, which distills SAM's geometric affinities as soft regularizers into VLM intermediate layers, injecting dense structural priors to refine localization. Second, Perspective-Spatial Contrastive Learning (PSCL) imposes cross-anchored constraints by mining mask-filtered deceptive distractors and spatial-linguistic counterfactuals as hard negatives, explicitly shattering semantic shortcuts to enforce genuine logical consistency. Extensive experiments on RRSIS benchmarks demonstrate that CROSS achieves state-of-the-art performance and maintains precise localization even under severe spatial description perturbations, standing as a robust new paradigm for RRSIS.

</details>


### [44] [Surface Keypoint Representation for Multi-Object and Articulated Human-Object Interaction Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03158)
*Xiaogang Peng,Zeyu Han,Zichong Meng,Yiming Xie,Jihua Zhu,Gang Hua,Huaizu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于表面关键点轨迹表示的人-物交互生成方法，通过跟踪每个刚性部件的表面关键点来统一处理单物体、多物体和关节物体的交互，并引入时空接触距离场指导全身运动合成，在多个基准上取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人-物交互（HOI）生成方法大多限定于单一刚体物体的交互，无法有效扩展到多物体或具有多样关节机制的关节物体场景，亟需一种统一且通用的表示方法来处理上述复杂交互场景。

Method: 1) 提出表面关键点轨迹作为物体运动表示，对每个刚性部件（独立物体或关节装配中的部件）跟踪少量非共线表面点在时间上的运动，直接从点动力学处理多物体协调和多样关节机制，无需显式指定关节类型；2) 引入时空接触距离场，将基于距离的接触建模扩展到全身、多物体和关节设置；3) 将HOI生成分解为三阶段：从文本或路径点生成物体运动、预测接触距离场、通过接触引导优化合成全身运动。

Result: 在ParaHome、HIMO、ARCTIC和OMOMO四个基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在单物体、多物体和关节交互场景中均达到优于或与现有方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: 通过表面关键点轨迹表示和时空接触距离场，该方法为通用的、可扩展的复杂人-物交互生成提供了有效解决方案，统一处理了多物体协调和多样化关节机制。

Abstract: Daily activities require humans to coordinate whole-body motion with the motion of surrounding objects. Despite recent progress in human-object interaction (HOI) generation, most existing methods assume interactions with a single rigid object and do not extend well to scenarios involving a variable number of objects or articulated objects with diverse joint mechanisms. We propose surface keypoint trajectories as an object motion representation: for each rigid component, whether a standalone object or one part of an articulated assembly, we track a small set of non-collinear surface points over time. This representation handles multi-object coordination and diverse articulation mechanisms directly from point dynamics without requiring explicit joint-type specification. To model when and where each body region contacts each object, we introduce a spatio-temporal contact distance field that extends distance-based contact modeling to whole-body, multi-object, and articulated settings. We factorize HOI generation into three stages: generating object motions from text or waypoints, predicting the contact distance field, and synthesizing whole-body motion with contact-guided optimization. Experiments on ParaHome, HIMO, ARCTIC, and OMOMO demonstrate better or comparable performance to existing methods across single-object, multi-object, and articulated interaction settings.

</details>


### [45] [Frequency-Decorrelated Temporal Ensembles for EEG--fNIRS Imagined-Handwriting Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03176)
*Xiao Fan,Hongbin Guo,Yubo Han,Yi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FRED系统，一种针对想象手写轨迹分类的任务自适应方法，利用多尺度时间卷积网络从三个互补EEG频率视图学习特征，结合跨频带集成、转导伪标签训练、EEG-Conformer成员、后验聚合及基于匈牙利算法的块配额解码，在MM挑战赛私密集上达到0.7718的总体准确率，排名第四，并验证了fNIRS贡献可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 想象手写为无创神经解码提供了时间信息丰富的范式，但跨受试者（不可见被试）的可靠识别仍然困难，原因是头皮EEG噪声大且内部生成的笔画序列因人而异。需要一个跨受试者、任务自适应的分类系统来解决这一挑战。

Method: FRED将想象手写建模为多秒运动序列，训练紧凑的多尺度时间网络处理三种互补的EEG频率视图。每个视图使用三个随机种子产生跨频带成员，误差相关性低于同频带副本，形成九成员集成。流水线还结合转导伪标签训练、三个EEG-Conformer成员、后验聚合和范式感知解码器，利用每个12次试验随机块中含四类各三实例的配额约束，通过匈牙利分配获得最终预测。

Result: 九成员集成在公开/私密/总体测试分区上的准确率为0.8076/0.7242/0.7492（无需测试集适配或输出约束）。完整系统在固定后验池上，独立、会话约束和块约束解码分别达到0.7600、0.7758和0.7952总体准确率；完整系统达到0.8498/0.7718/0.7952，在私密集排名第四。模态审计显示fNIRS单独解码接近随机（0.2511），而加入fNIRS仅提升+0.0025。

Conclusion: 频率多样的时间EEG建模和协议匹配的结构化推理是该稀疏电极EEG-fNIRS设置中性能的主要来源；fNIRS模态在此任务中贡献极小。源代码已公开。

Abstract: Imagined handwriting offers a temporally rich paradigm for non-invasive neural decoding, yet reliable recognition across unseen participants remains difficult because scalp EEG is noisy and internally generated stroke sequences vary across individuals. The Multimodal Brain-Computer Interface Grand Challenge provides synchronized EEG and fNIRS for four-class subject-independent handwriting-trajectory classification. We propose FRED, a task-adapted system that models imagined handwriting as a multi-second motor sequence and trains a compact multi-scale temporal network on three complementary EEG frequency views. With three seeds per view, cross-band members produce substantially less-correlated errors than same-band replicas, yielding a clean nine-member ensemble accuracy of 0.8076/0.7242/0.7492 on the public/private/overall test partitions without test-set adaptation or output constraints. The submitted pipeline further incorporates transductive pseudo-label training, three EEG-Conformer members, posterior aggregation, and a paradigm-aware decoder. Because every 12-trial randomization block contains three instances of each class, the final predictions are obtained by Hungarian assignment under the known block quota. On one fixed posterior pool, independent, session-constrained, and block-constrained decoding achieve 0.7600, 0.7758, and 0.7952 overall accuracy, respectively. The complete system reaches 0.8498/0.7718/0.7952, ranking fourth on the private split. A modality audit finds fNIRS-only decoding at chance (0.2511 overall), while adding fNIRS to EEG changes accuracy by only +0.0025. These results identify frequency-diverse temporal EEG modeling and protocol-matched structured inference as the principal sources of performance in this sparse-montage EEG--fNIRS setting. The source code is available at https://github.com/XiuFan719/EEG-fNIRS-fuse-method-for-MM-challenge.

</details>


### [46] [EditFlow3D: Automated Local Editing of 3D Assets with Trajectory Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03179)
*Rui Nie,Chuang Wang,Haitao Zhou,Jiahe Song,Buyu Li,Sheng Wang,Qian Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditFlow3D是一个无需训练的3D局部编辑框架，利用VLM驱动的流程自动构建视觉引导图像和3D编辑掩码，通过掩码引导的差分流和逐步轨迹保持实现精确的局部编辑，同时保持非目标区域的一致性。在提出的更大规模基准EditFlow-Bench和已有Edit3D-Bench上的实验表明其优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D局部编辑方法缺乏简单而精确的3D掩码获取方式，难以在实现目标编辑的同时忠实保持非目标区域的结构和外观。具体挑战包括：1）目标定位困难；2）视觉引导不准确；3）非目标区域保真度不足。

Method: EditFlow3D采用训练-free框架，核心包含：1）VLM驱动的流程解释编辑意图并自动生成视觉引导图像和精细的3D编辑掩码；2）掩码引导的差分流将编辑聚焦于目标区域；3）逐步轨迹保持通过维持非目标区域与源资产之间的一致性，而不直接替换中间特征，从而实现局部编辑。

Result: 编辑在EditFlow3D在Edit3D-Bench和新增的EditFlow-Bench两个基准上的定量结果、定性比较和用户研究均表明，EditFlow3D在目标区域编辑的精确性和非目标区域保真度方面均优于现有3D编辑方法。

Conclusion: EditFlow3D通过VLM驱动自动生成掩码和引导图像，结合差分流与轨迹保持机制，实现了无需训练的精确3D局部编辑，同时引入EditFlow-Bench扩充了局部编辑基准的覆盖范围，为3D局部编辑任务提供了更全面的评估平台。

Abstract: Controllable local editing of 3D assets requires precise target localization and appropriate visual guidance. However, existing methods lack a simple yet accurate way to obtain 3D masks and struggle to achieve the desired edit while faithfully preserving the structure and appearance of non-target regions. To address these challenges, we present EditFlow3D, a training-free framework for local 3D editing. Given a source asset and an edit instruction, a VLM-driven workflow interprets the editing intent and automatically constructs a visual guidance image and a refined 3D editing mask, enabling localized editing in the native representation space of a pretrained 3D generative model. Specifically, mask-guided differential flow focuses the edit on the target region, while step-wise trajectory preservation maintains consistency between non-target regions and the source asset without directly replacing intermediate features. Since the existing Edit3D-Bench covers only a limited range of local editing categories, we further introduce EditFlow-Bench as a complementary benchmark encompassing a broader variety of structural and appearance edits, and evaluate EditFlow3D on both benchmarks. Quantitative results, qualitative comparisons, and a user study demonstrate that EditFlow3D achieves more accurate target-region editing and better preserves non-target regions than existing 3D editing methods.

</details>


### [47] [CRIL-U-Net: Compact Ratio-Interaction Learning for Focal Cortical Dysplasia Segmentation from T1w and FLAIR MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03185)
*Soumen Ghosh,Amit Soni Arya,Tilottama Goswami,Subhojit Mandal,John Phamnguyen,Rajat Vashistha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CRIL-U-Net，一种结合紧凑比率交互学习模块的3D U-Net，用于局灶性皮层发育不良（FCD II型）的自动分割。该模块融合局部空间特征、体素级跨模态混合和双向比率启发交互。在85例FCD患者和25例健康对照的5折交叉验证中，CRIL-U-Net配合Focal Tversky-Focal损失达到最高平均Dice分数（0.196±0.262），显著优于传统U-Net和注意力U-Net，但48.2%的零重叠率仍凸显了方法改进和进一步验证的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FCD II型是药物难治性局灶性癫痫的重要结构性病因，但其体积小、外观异质且MRI特征细微，使得自动分割极具挑战。传统多模态网络通常简单拼接T1w和FLAIR图像，需要后续层隐式学习跨模态关系，效率较低，因此需要更紧凑、显式的跨模态表示学习方法。

Method: 提出CRIL-U-Net，在3D U-Net架构中引入紧凑比率交互学习（Compact Ratio-Interaction Learning）模块，该模块包含三个关键组件：局部空间特征提取、体素级跨模态混合以及双向比率启发的交互机制，旨在显式地建模T1w与FLAIR模态间的互补关系。实验采用5折交叉验证，在85例FCD受试者和25例健康对照上，与常规3D U-Net及输入自注意力U-Net进行对比，每种架构分别使用Dice二值交叉熵（Dice-BCE）和Focal Tversky-Focal（FTF）两种损失函数独立训练。

Result: 在FTF损失下，CRIL-U-Net取得了最高平均Dice分数（0.196±0.262），优于U-Net（0.136±0.224）和注意力比较模型（0.135±0.214）。CRIL-U-Net在85例中有44例产生非零病灶重叠，而U-Net为36例。经错误发现率（FDR）校正后，CRIL-U-Net在FTF下显著优于两种比较架构。

Conclusion: 紧凑的跨模态表示学习结合不平衡感知损失函数，在受控的U-Net设置下能够改善FCD分割性能。然而，48.2%的零重叠率表明该任务仍极具挑战性，需要进一步的方法学发展和更大规模的验证。

Abstract: Focal cortical dysplasia (FCD) type II is an important structural cause of drug-resistant focal epilepsy, but its small size, heterogeneous appearance, and subtle MRI characteristics make automated segmentation challenging. Conventional multimodal networks commonly concatenate T1-weighted (T1w) and fluid-attenuated inversion recovery (FLAIR) images, requiring subsequent layers to learn useful cross-modal relationships implicitly. We propose CRIL-U-Net, a 3D U-Net incorporating a Compact Ratio-Interaction Learning module that combines local spatial features, voxel-wise cross-modal mixing, and bidirectional ratio-inspired interactions. CRIL-U-Net was compared with a conventional 3D U-Net and an input self-attention U-Net using five-fold cross-validation on 85 FCD subjects and 25 healthy controls. Each architecture was trained independently using Dice-binary cross-entropy (Dice-BCE) and Focal Tversky-Focal (FTF) losses. With FTF, CRIL-U-Net achieved the highest mean Dice score (0.196 +/- 0.262), compared with 0.136 +/- 0.224 for the U-Net and 0.135 +/- 0.214 for the attention comparator. It produced nonzero lesion overlap in 44 of 85 cases, compared with 36 for the U-Net. Under FTF, CRIL-U-Net significantly outperformed both comparison architectures after false-discovery-rate correction. These findings suggest that compact cross-modal representation learning can improve FCD segmentation within a controlled U-Net setting when combined with an imbalance-aware objective, although the remaining zero-overlap rate of 48.2% highlights the need for further validation and methodological development.

</details>


### [48] [Bridging Online and Offline Handwriting via Differentiable Physical Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03198)
*Seonmi Park,Seunghyun Shin,Vihaan Misra,Dongmin Shin,Ukcheol Shin,Jean Oh,Hae-Gon Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的在线-离线手写体生成框架，通过紧凑的物理笔刷模型和可微渲染模块，将笔画运动轨迹与像素级外观联系起来，实现从文本和风格图像到手写体图像的端到端生成，并在真实机器人书法演示中验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线方法能捕捉笔画的结构和时序动态但缺乏细粒度纹理，离线方法能生成逼真外观但丢弃笔顺信息。两类方法相互独立，难以统一，主要面临的挑战是：(1)缺少连接笔画运动学和像素级外观的显式物理模型；(2)缺少成对的轨迹-图像数据集；(3)端到端学习需要跨运动和外观域的可微渲染过程。

Method: 提出紧凑的物理笔刷模型连接笔画动力学和视觉外观，配合可微渲染模块将笔画轨迹转换为风格化图像。框架由四个核心模块组成：1)文本到笔画生成器，根据给定文本和风格图像预测目标笔画；2)笔刷参数观测器，从风格参考图像中提取笔刷模型参数；3)可微笔刷渲染器，将笔画序列和物理笔刷参数映射为手写体图像；4)零样本图像精化器，通过扩散模型对渲染图像进行精化。

Result: 通过大量实验和真实机器人书法演示验证了方法有效性，在结构保真和视觉保真两个方面均取得了良好效果。

Conclusion: 该方法成功统一了在线和离线手写体生成范式，不仅实现了结构与时序信息以及真实外观的兼顾，还为手写生成领域提供了可微的物理渲染框架，并为机器人书法等下游应用提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Realistic handwritten text generation plays an important role in numerous applications, such as font design, biometric authentication, and robotic calligraphy. Existing methods are typically divided into two independent paradigms: online approaches that estimate handwriting trajectories and offline approaches that synthesize realistic handwriting images. While online models capture structural and temporal dynamics, they often lack fine-grained textures, whereas offline models reproduce realistic appearance but discard stroke order. However, unifying online and offline models remains challenging due to (1) the lack of an explicit physical model linking stroke kinematics to pixel-level appearance and (2) the absence of paired trajectory-image datasets. Moreover, enabling end-to-end learning requires a differentiable rendering process across motion and appearance domains. To address these challenges, we propose a compact physical brush model that bridges stroke dynamics and visual appearance, together with a differentiable rendering module that converts stroke trajectories into stylized images. By integrating these components, we propose a unified online-offline handwriting generation framework via differentiable brush rendering. The proposed framework consists of four core modules: 1) a text-to-stroke generator that predicts the target stroke conditioned on the given text and style image, 2) a brush parameter observer that extracts brush model parameters from style references, 3) a differentiable brush renderer that maps a stroke sequence and physical brush parameters into a handwritten image, and 4) a zero-shot image refiner that refines rendered images via diffusion models. Extensive experiments and real-world robotic calligraphy demonstrations validate our approach, achieving both structural and visual fidelity.

</details>


### [49] [DRIFT: Derailing Denoising Trajectories of Flow-Matching VLAs with Adversarial Patch Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03207)
*Hoseong Tae,Jong-Seok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DRIFT攻击方法，揭示flow-matching VLA模型（如pi0）对对抗扰动的鲁棒性是虚假的。通过仅攻击去噪ODE的第一步，在机器人夹爪上放置通用对抗补丁即可破坏几乎所有原本可解的任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究声称flow-matching VLA模型能抵抗对抗扰动，但这种鲁棒性可能源于先前攻击方法忽略了多步去噪ODE这一关键机制。作者旨在揭示这种"虚假鲁棒性"并构建真正的攻击方法。

Method: 提出DRIFT攻击，通过在机器人的夹爪上放置测试时通用对抗补丁，攻击flow-matching策略的去噪速度场。关键发现是仅攻击第一步去噪比攻击更宽的步骤窗口更强且更便宜，这源于输入空间优化中特有的梯度冲突，与训练时后门机制恰恰相反。

Result: 在pi0和pi0.5上的四个LIBERO套件中，DRIFT用单个小补丁破坏了几乎所有原本可解的任务，效果大幅超越基于动作空间和嵌入空间的攻击基线。

Conclusion: flow-matching VLA模型的对抗鲁棒性在很大程度上是虚假的，攻击仅需集中在去噪过程的第一步即可实现高效且强力的对抗攻击。

Abstract: Flow-matching vision-language-action (VLA) models such as pi0 generate robot actions by integrating a learned denoising velocity field, and have been reported to resist adversarial perturbations that readily fool autoregressive VLAs. We show that this robustness is largely illusory: it stems from prior attacks ignoring the multi-step denoising ODE. We introduce DRIFT (Denoising Redirection via Input perturbation of the Flow-matching Trajectory), a test-time universal adversarial patch placed on the robot's gripper that attacks the denoising velocity field of an off-the-shelf policy. Our central finding is counterintuitive: attacking only the first denoising step is both stronger and cheaper than attacking a wider window of steps, which we explain through a gradient conflict unique to input-space optimization and which is exactly opposite to the training-time backdoor regime. On pi0 and pi0.5 across four LIBERO suites, DRIFT breaks essentially all originally-solvable tasks with a small single patch, far exceeding action- and embedding-space attack baselines.

</details>


### [50] [CrossScope: A Role-Asymmetric World Model for Joint Dual-Scope Surgical Video Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03211)
*Wanhao Liu,Jinsong Lin,Rulin Zhou,Chi Kit Ng,Wenbin Pan,Zhiqing Tang,Dongyue Li,Liwei Luo,Yanshen Wu,Panshuo Li,Zhiyong Xiong,Huxin Gao,Tamas Haidegger,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CrossScope，一个针对母-子双胆道镜（ERCP）场景的角色不对称双流手术世界模型，通过几何引导的残差交互实现跨视角选择性证据路由，在双视角未来预测中优于现有外科视频生成基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉世界模型通常从单一观察流学习未来动态，无法建模多个独立运动观察者的协作系统。在ERCP手术中，母镜和子镜两个柔性内窥镜提供互补但角色依赖的视图，且没有标定的立体关系。传统的多视角融合假设对称信息交换，忽视了不同视角在预测目标中的不对称角色，因此需要一种角色感知的跨视角信息路由方法。

Method: CrossScope采用双流架构，保留每个视角的专属特征专家，并通过几何引导的残差交互实现目标特定的证据路由。其核心是两个互补的通信方向：(1) 母视图的几何运动线索引导子视图未来动态预测；(2) 仅当建立有效空间对应关系时，姿态对齐的子视图外观才支持母视图预测。这种设计允许每个内窥镜贡献任务相关证据而不损害其视角专属表征。

Result: 作者建立了包含同步体模和真实ERCP片段的成对双视角基准，从视觉保真度、结构保持、目标定位和运动一致性四个维度进行评估。实验表明CrossScope在多个强外科视频生成基线上持续取得最优性能，验证了角色感知证据路由对多观察者视觉世界建模的重要性。

Conclusion: CrossScope证明了在无标定立体关系的多内窥镜场景中，角色不对称的跨视角信息路由（而非对称融合）是更有效的视觉世界建模策略。这一范式有望推广到其他具有异构视角角色的协作感知系统。

Abstract: Visual world models typically learn future dynamics from a single observation stream, limiting their ability to model cooperative systems with multiple independently moving observers. We investigate this challenge in Mother--Child endoscopic retrograde cholangiopancreatography (ERCP), where two flexible scopes provide complementary yet role-dependent views without a calibrated stereo relationship. Unlike conventional multi-view fusion that assumes symmetric information exchange, we formulate \textbf{role-asymmetric dual-scope future prediction}, where cross-view evidence is selectively transferred according to the prediction target and its underlying spatial requirements. We propose \textbf{CrossScope}, a dual-stream surgical world model that preserves view-specific experts while enabling target-specific evidence routing through geometry-guided residual interactions. CrossScope learns two complementary communication directions: geometric motion cues from the Mother view guide Child-view future dynamics, while pose-aligned Child appearance supports Mother-view prediction only when valid spatial correspondence is established. This design allows each scope to contribute task-relevant evidence without compromising its view-specific representation. To evaluate this problem, we establish a paired dual-scope benchmark comprising synchronized phantom and real-world ERCP episodes, with evaluations assessing visual fidelity, structural preservation, target localization, and motion consistency. Experiments demonstrate that CrossScope consistently outperforms strong surgical video generation baselines, validating the importance of role-aware evidence routing for multi-observer visual world modeling.

</details>


### [51] [iFAN: Inference-Aware Learning for Plain Mask Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03216)
*Fang Li,Yu He,Haoyang Tong,Lichen Ma,Jingling Fu,Wenxiao Fan,Tongxuan Liu,Luohang Liu,Ke Zhang,Junshi Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: iFAN是一个面向掩码变换器的通用训练框架，通过调整概率掩码排名（APMR）和跨层自蒸馏（CLSD）来对齐训练与推理过程中的查询竞争机制，在多种分割任务和架构上平均提升1.20 PQ、1.30 AP和0.63 mIoU，且不增加推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于查询的掩码变换器在推理时通过最终层查询预测之间的像素级竞争来组装分割输出，但这种推理过程在训练时并未被显式优化，导致两个关键不匹配：1）具有最高概率掩码得分的查询不一定产生最准确的掩码；2）最终层解码可能丢弃中间层更优的预测。

Method: 提出Inference-Aware Learning (iFAN)训练框架，包含两个核心组件：1）Adjusted Probability-Mask Ranking (APMR)，将查询竞争与预测掩码质量对齐，并抑制高置信度但不准确的竞争者；2）Cross-Layer Self-Distillation (CLSD)，将中间层的更强预测迁移至最终层。两个目标均为训练专用，推理仍保持高效的最终层解码。

Result: 在COCO、ADE20K和Cityscapes数据集上，覆盖全景、实例和语义分割任务，以及不同架构、骨干网络规模和输入分辨率下均获得一致改进，平均提升1.20 PQ、1.30 AP和0.63 mIoU，额外参数、FLOPs和推理延迟可忽略不计。

Conclusion: iFAN通过训练阶段的推理感知优化，有效弥合了掩码变换器训练与推理之间的差距，在不增加推理成本的前提下显著提升多种分割任务的性能，是一种通用且高效的训练框架。

Abstract: Query-based mask transformers assemble segmentation outputs through pixel-wise competition among query predictions of the final layer, yet this inference process is not explicitly optimized during training. We identify two key mismatches: the query with the highest probability-mask score does not necessarily produce the most accurate mask, and final-layer decoding may discard superior predictions from intermediate layers. To address these issues, we propose Inference-Aware Learning (iFAN), a general training framework for plain mask transformers. iFAN introduces Adjusted Probability-Mask Ranking (APMR), which aligns query competition with predicted mask quality and suppresses high-confidence but inaccurate competitors. We further employ Cross-Layer Self-Distillation (CLSD) to transfer stronger intermediate predictions to the final layer. The ranking and distillation objectives are training-only, while inference retains efficient final-layer decoding. Experiments on COCO, ADE20K, and Cityscapes demonstrate consistent improvements across panoptic, instance, and semantic segmentation, as well as across different architectures, backbone scales, and input resolutions. Overall, iFAN improves performance by an average of 1.20 PQ, 1.30 AP, and 0.63 mIoU, with negligible additional parameters, FLOPs and inference latency.

</details>


### [52] [Self-Supervised Representation-Guided Generative Dataset Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03218)
*Mingzhuo Li,Guang Li,Linfeng Ye,Jiafeng Mao,Takahiro Ogawa,Konstantinos N. Plataniotis,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SRG框架，一种自监督表示引导的生成式数据集蒸馏方法。SRG将SSL表示空间的几何结构转化为扩散引导信号，通过类原型对齐、类间判别和类内分配三类目标在扩散采样阶段进行分阶段引导（早期锚定到真实图像潜变量保持视觉真实感，后期由SSL目标引导），从而蒸馏出保留预训练表示空间判别几何的紧凑合成数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集蒸馏方法主要面向随机初始化网络，而现代视觉系统通常采用冻结的预训练编码器加轻量模块进行适配。蒸馏样本应当保留预训练表示空间中的判别性几何结构，但现有生成式蒸馏目标并未显式考虑这一点。

Method: SRG框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 从真实图像的SSL表示中构造类级原型；(2) 通过三个SSL空间目标（原型对齐、类间判别、类内分配）进行扩散引导；(3) 采用分阶段引导策略——早期去噪步骤锚定到SSL表示最接近指定原型的真实图像潜变量，后期去噪步骤由SSL空间目标引导。该策略在保持生成先验提供的视觉真实感的同时，逐步将样本推向SSL表示空间中具有代表性且类判别性的区域。

Result: SRG在多个数据集和多种IPC（每类图像数）设置下持续优于所评估的生成式基线方法。跨编码器评估进一步表明其具有跨预训练表示空间的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了表示引导生成在基于预训练SSL模型的数据集蒸馏中的有效性，说明将SSL几何结构融入扩散引导可以显著提升蒸馏样本的质量。

Abstract: Dataset distillation compresses a large training set into a compact synthetic set while retaining its downstream utility. Most existing methods target randomly initialized networks, whereas modern vision systems often adapt frozen pretrained encoders with lightweight modules. Distilled samples should therefore preserve the discriminative geometry of the pretrained representation space, which existing generative objectives do not explicitly consider. We propose self-supervised representation-guided generative dataset distillation (SRG), a framework that translates the SSL geometry into diffusion guidance. Specifically, SRG constructs class-wise prototypes from real-image SSL representations and performs guidance through three SSL-space objectives for prototype alignment, inter-class discrimination, and intra-class assignment. During diffusion sampling, it adopts a stage-wise guidance strategy: early denoising is anchored to the latent of the real image whose SSL representation is nearest to the assigned prototype, whereas later denoising is guided by the SSL-space objectives. This division preserves the visual realism provided by the generative prior while progressively steering samples toward representative and class-discriminative regions of the SSL representation space. SRG consistently outperforms the evaluated generative baselines across multiple datasets and IPC settings. A cross-encoder evaluation further indicates transfer across pretrained representation spaces. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of representation-guided generation for dataset distillation with pretrained SSL models.

</details>


### [53] [Open-Linguistic Concept Unified Learning for Cross-Site Interpretable Dermatology Image Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03225)
*Chengyu Wu,Junpeng Tan,Wanxiang Luo,Yaqi Wang,Yandong Wen,Yefeng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniCon是一个开放语言统一概念学习框架，通过共享语义原型码本、开放语言多面语义规范和可靠性门控瓶颈聚合，实现跨模态（皮肤镜与临床照片）、跨站点的可解释视觉语言皮肤病学诊断与临床干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概念模型在可解释医学诊断中表现优异，但其僵化的数据集特定适配限制了跨站泛化，难以在异构模态（如皮肤镜和临床照片）和应用不同概念分类体系的队列间迁移。现有基础视觉语言模型（FVLM）的适配需要昂贵的标签工程和重复后训练，干预机制也固着于预定义概念，阻碍了可扩展的皮肤病学CAD部署。

Method: UniCon通过三个核心贡献解决上述问题：(1) 构建统一概念原型码本作为共享语义表示空间，无缝协调跨模态的异构概念体系，无需数据集特定重训练；(2) 采用开放语言的多面语义规范，克服稀疏文本标签限制，提升不确定临床场景下的边界敏感性；(3) 设计基于可靠性门控瓶颈聚合的鲁棒跨站点可调干预接口，实现一致推理和可迁移的临床医生修正。

Result: 大量实验表明UniCon不仅获得了顶级的诊断准确率，还成功弥合了不同临床分类体系之间的鸿沟，解锁了前所未有的跨站点干预能力。

Conclusion: UniCon提供了一个开放、统一且可干预的概念学习框架，为多模态可解释皮肤病学诊断的可扩展部署提供了有效方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Human-interpretable computer-aided diagnosis is crucial for clinical decision making. Concept-based models excel by providing transparent reasoning and enabling post-hoc, clinician-in-the-loop interventions. However, their rigid dataset-specific adaptation inherently restricts cross-site generalization. Applying them across diverse modalities, such as dermoscopic and clinical photographs, is challenging due to heterogeneous concept taxonomies varying in availability, granularity, and semantics across cohorts. Consequently, adapting Foundation Vision-Language Models (FVLMs) demands costly label engineering and repeated post-training. Existing intervention mechanisms remain rigidly tied to predefined concepts, lacking adaptability and hindering scalable dermatology CAD deployment. To address these bottlenecks, we propose UniCon, an open-linguistic unified concept learning framework for multimodal interpretable vision-language diagnosis. UniCon resolves these challenges through three contributions: (1) A shared semantic representation space via a unified concept prototype codebook, seamlessly coordinating heterogeneous concept systems across modalities without dataset-specific retraining. (2) Open-linguistic based multi-faceted semantic specifications to overcome sparse textual label limitations, improving boundary sensitivity in uncertain clinical contexts. (3) A robust, cross-site adjustable intervention interface powered by reliability-gated bottleneck aggregation, enabling consistent reasoning and transferable clinician corrections. Extensive experiments demonstrate that beyond securing top-tier diagnostic accuracy, UniCon successfully bridges disparate clinical taxonomies, unlocking unprecedented cross-site intervention capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/wuchengyu123/UniCon.

</details>


### [54] [CIGTSurv: Clinical Information Guided Tri-modal Survival Prediction with Local Prototype Association and Global Feature Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03247)
*Jing Dai,Qibin Zhang,Weiwei Zhou,Mingde Xu,Jingsong Liu,Jingdong Zhang,Hongming Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CIGTSurv，一个以临床信息为锚点的三模态（病理图像、基因组数据、临床数据）生存预测框架。通过文本模板和预训练基础模型将临床表格数据转化为高维token嵌入，并设计了基于交叉注意力的局部原型关联模块和基于最大均值差异的全局特征对齐损失，实现跨模态交互建模，在五个TCGA癌症队列上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态生存预测方法主要整合病理图像与基因组数据，但临床信息因其离散、稀疏和低维特性而被忽视。同时，多模态间的固有异质性给跨模态交互建模带来显著挑战。

Method: 1) 设计整体文本模板，利用预训练基础模型将临床表格数据转换为高维token化嵌入；2) 以临床信息为锚点，提出双层次交互机制：局部原型关联（LPA）模块基于交叉注意力显式学习不同模态间的token级对应关系；全局特征对齐（GFA）损失基于最大均值差异（MMD）隐式增强跨模态分布一致性。

Result: 在五个TCGA癌症队列上的广泛实验表明，CIGTSurv达到了最先进的生存预测性能。

Conclusion: CIGTSurv有效利用了临床信息并通过双层次交互机制解决了多模态异质性挑战，为多模态生存预测提供了新框架。

Abstract: Multimodal learning has significantly advanced survival prediction by integrating pathology images with genomic data. However, clinical information, despite its critical role in reflecting a patient' s overall health, remains underutilized due to its discrete, sparse, and low-dimensional nature. Furthermore, the inherent heterogeneity across these modalities pose significant challenges in modeling cross-modal interactions. In this paper, we propose CIGTSurv, a Clinical Information Guided Tri-modal framework for Survival prediction. Specifically, we first design a holistic text template and use pretrained foundation models to transform clinical tabular data into high-dimensional tokenized embeddings. Using clinical information as an anchor, we then introduce a dual-level interaction mechanism: 1) a local prototype association (LPA) module based on cross-attention to explicitly learn token-level correspondences between different modalities, and 2) a global feature alignment (GFA) loss based on Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD) to implicitly enhance cross-modal distribution consistency. Extensive experiments on five TCGA cancer cohorts demonstrate that CIGTSurv achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) survival prediction performance. Our source code is publicly available at https://github.com/Daijing-ai/CIGT-Surv.git.

</details>


### [55] [Clarity Contrast and Similarity Selection for Multi-Focus Image Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03252)
*Yicheng Zhang,Haoyou Deng,Zhiqiang Li,Wenti Yin,Nong Sang,Changxin Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为CSNet的多聚焦图像融合网络，通过清晰的对比注意力模块（CCAM）和相似性选择策略，在源图像之间建立直接信息交互，以保留聚焦区域并恢复自然边界，达到最先进的融合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的多聚焦图像融合方法缺乏源图像之间的显式交互，导致融合性能受限且可解释性不足。特别是聚焦与失焦边界附近的像素因散焦扩散效应（DSE）在所有源图像中均退化，需要更精细的处理策略。

Method: CSNet包含两个关键组件：（1）Clarity Contrast Attention Module (CCAM)：通过对比源图像间的清晰度差异，相互增强锐利特征并抑制模糊特征，从而识别精确的聚焦区域和聚焦-失焦边界；（2）Similarity Selection Strategy：从源图像重建初始清晰图像，通过比较像素间的相似性来选择最优像素，以细化边界附近受DSE影响的模糊区域。两者构成交互式融合框架。

Result: 大量实验表明，CSNet在定量（客观指标）和定性（主观视觉）两方面均达到了最先进的融合性能，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过源图像间的显式信息交互（清晰度对比和相似性选择），CSNet能够有效保留聚焦区域并恢复自然边界，为多聚焦图像融合提供了一种具备可解释性的新方案。

Abstract: Multi-focus image fusion (MFIF) aims to generate an all-in-focus image from multiple images of the same scene focused at different regions. Most existing deep learning-based methods lack explicit interaction between the source images, which limits their performance and interpretability. This paper presents a novel Clarity Contrast and Similarity Selection Network (CSNet), to bridge direct information exchange for MFIF. Specifically, by contrasting the clarity differences between source images within our proposed Clarity Contrast Attention Module (CCAM), we mutually enhance sharp features while suppressing blurry ones. This allows us to identify the exactly focused regions in each source and locate the focused-defocused boundaries. Moreover, the Defocus Spread Effect (DSE) degrades pixels in all source images around the boundaries. To further refine these ambiguous areas, we introduce a Similarity Selection Strategy, which reconstructs an initial clear image from source images and selects optimal pixels by comparing the similarity among them. Through this interactive approach, CSNet effectively preserves focused regions as well as recovering natural boundaries to fuse an all-in-focus output. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance both quantitatively and qualitatively. Our code is available on Github: https://github.com/ZYC-HUST/CSNet.

</details>


### [56] [NanoMorph-3D: An End-to-End Physics-Driven Unrolling Framework for Nanomaterial Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03257)
*Beiyuan Zhang,Hesong Li,Ziqi Wu,Ruiwen Shao,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NanoMorph-3D，一个基于纳米形态学分类法的统一端到端框架，用于解决电子断层扫描中的缺失楔问题。该方法通过大规模合成数据集、物理驱动的展开网络（将近端梯度下降映射为可学习架构）、层级注意力机制、双域正弦注意力策略以及无监督双流机制，实现了对复杂3D纳米材料拓扑的高保真重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的3D纳米材料表征对揭示结构-性能关系至关重要，但标准电子断层扫描受缺失楔问题根本性限制，传统算法存在严重几何畸变且受噪声干扰；现有学习方法要么依赖物理盲目的后处理，要么采用感受野受限的端到端架构，无法捕捉复杂3D拓扑结构。

Method: 1) 构建纳米形态学分类法（Nanomorphological Taxonomy）作为理论基础；2) 构建大规模合成数据集，显式建模非线性电子衰减；3) 设计物理驱动的展开网络（Physics-Driven Unrolled Network），将近端梯度下降映射为可学习架构；4) 提出带物理归一化的层级注意力机制，捕捉长程3D依赖与尺度不变性；5) 采用双域策略，利用正弦注意力显式建模物理投影轨迹，强制执行正弦图一致性以缓解缺失楔伪影；6) 设计无监督双流机制弥合仿真到现实的差距。

Result: 实验表明NanoMorph-3D能以优越的保真度和速度重建多样化的3D纳米材料拓扑结构。

Conclusion: NanoMorph-3D通过将物理先验与深度学习架构深度融合，在电子断层扫描重建任务中同时实现了高保真度和高效率，验证了物理驱动的展开网络结合双域注意力机制在解决缺失楔问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Precise 3D characterization of nanomaterials is essential for unlocking structure-property relationships. However, standard electron tomography is fundamentally limited by the missing wedge problem. Consequently, conventional algorithms suffer from severe geometric distortions, a challenge further complicated by pervasive noise interference. Current learning-based methods either rely on physics-blind post-processing or employ end-to-end architectures constrained by local receptive fields, failing to capture complex 3D topologies. We propose NanoMorph-3D, a unified end-to-end framework grounded in a comprehensive Nanomorphological Taxonomy. Powered by a large-scale synthetic dataset explicitly modeling non-linear electron attenuation, we design a Physics-Driven Unrolled Network mapping proximal gradient descent into a learnable architecture. To capture complex internal topologies, we formulate a hierarchical attention mechanism with Physics-Normalization for long-range 3D dependencies and scale invariance. Crucially, our Dual-Domain strategy leverages Sinusoidal Attention to explicitly model physical projection trajectories, enforcing strict sinogram consistency to mitigate missing wedge artifacts. Finally, an unsupervised dual-stream mechanism bridges the simulation-to-reality gap. Experiments demonstrate NanoMorph-3D reconstructs diverse topologies with superior fidelity and speed.

</details>


### [57] [Efficient Video Dataset Distillation via Cluster-Guided Prototype Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03269)
*Chongle Ren,Guang Li,Wenbo Huang,Naoki Saito,Takahiro Ogawa,Miki Haseyama*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了ProtoBlend框架，一种无需梯度优化的视频数据集蒸馏方法，通过"选择-分配-混合"三步流程（教师引导的时间片段选择、聚类引导的原型分配、原型与锚点的混合监督），在四个动作识别基准上实现了竞争性的精度-效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频数据集蒸馏方法主要依赖迭代优化来合成紧凑视频，其计算成本因时间维度而被放大。作者试图探索能否在不进行基于梯度的存储视频优化的情况下，直接构建有效的蒸馏视频，从而显著降低蒸馏成本。

Method: ProtoBlind框架包含三个步骤：(1) 教师引导的时间片段选择：从每个源视频中保留高置信度的片段；(2) 聚类引导的原型分配：在教师特征空间中对所选片段进行聚类，为每个类内聚类分配一个蒸馏槽位；(3) 原型混合：每个原型与类内锚点进行混合，并使用相同的系数组合教师预测，提供混合来源的监督信号。

Result: 在四个修剪后的动作识别基准上，ProtoBlend在不进行蒸馏视频迭代优化的情况下，实现了具有竞争力的精度-效率平衡。

Conclusion: 通过构造而非优化的方式可以有效实现视频数据集蒸馏，ProtoBlend验证了这种替代路径的可行性，为视频蒸馏提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Video dataset distillation aims to compress a large video dataset into a compact surrogate set that preserves its training utility. Most existing approaches synthesize condensed videos through iterative optimization, whose cost is amplified by the temporal dimension. Rather than further reducing the number of optimized variables, we investigate whether effective distilled videos can be constructed without gradient-based optimization of the stored videos. Such a construction-based approach must address three challenges: selecting informative temporal segments, covering diverse intra-class variations under a limited videos-per-class budget, and increasing the information carried by each stored sample. To this end, we propose ProtoBlend, an efficient select-allocate-blend framework. First, teacher-guided temporal clip selection retains a high-confidence segment from each source video. Second, cluster-guided prototype allocation partitions the selected clips in the teacher feature space and assigns one distilled slot to each intra-class cluster. Third, each prototype is blended with an in-cluster anchor, while their teacher predictions are combined using the same coefficient to provide mixture-source supervision. Experiments on four trimmed action-recognition benchmarks demonstrate that ProtoBlend achieves a competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-off without iterative optimization of the distilled videos.

</details>


### [58] [GUI-Lens: Coarse-to-Fine Cropping for GUI Grounding with General-Purpose VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03270)
*Zichuan Fu,Shirong Wang,Wenlin Zhang,Guojing Li,Yimin Deng,Jingtong Gao,Junjia Qi,Hanyu Yan,Yefeng Zheng,Xiaopeng Li,Wanyu Wang,Xian Wu,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GUI-Lens提出了一种由粗到细的GUI接地框架，通过逐步裁剪和放大视图，让通用VLM以主动视觉观察的方式逐步精确定位目标控件，在四个基准上最多提升24.9个百分点，并使用GPT-5.5达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GUI接地任务在分辨率高、元素密集的界面中仍然困难，现有方法依赖直接点击预测，视觉歧义或不准确的初始估计会传播到最终结果，缺乏主动验证和逐步细化机制。

Method: GUI-Lens采用粗到细的框架：首先从截图提取OCR文本和UI组件作为坐标参考，VLM根据指令、当前视图和这些参考选择下一视图的区域和缩放尺度，裁剪并放大以获得更精细的视觉细节；该过程在连续聚焦的视图上迭代，直到确定目标；每次提出的裁剪和点击都会与指令进行核查，最终局部坐标映射回原始屏幕坐标。

Result: 在四个GUI接地基准和三个通用VLM后端上的实验表明，GUI-Lens将整体接地准确率最多提升了24.9个百分点，并在GPT-5.5上实现了SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 通过将直接点击预测转化为一系列主动视觉观察和逐步细化的过程，可以有效缓解视觉歧义问题，提升GUI接地的精确性和鲁棒性，且不依赖特定的专用模型。

Abstract: GUI grounding maps natural-language instructions to click locations and is essential for reliable GUI agents. The task remains difficult on high-resolution, densely populated interfaces because a vision-language model (VLM) may recognize a requested control without locating it precisely enough for interaction. Most existing methods provide various forms of localization assistance, but still rely on a direct click prediction, allowing visual ambiguity or an inaccurate initial estimate to propagate to the final result. In this paper, we introduce GUI-Lens, a coarse-to-fine grounding framework that allows a general-purpose VLM to determine the target through active visual observations. Specifically, GUI-Lens extracts OCR text and detected UI components from the screenshot and presents their positions as coordinate references. Using the instruction, the current view, and these references, the VLM selects the region and scale of the next view, which is cropped and enlarged to provide finer visual details. This process continues over successively focused views until the target is determined. Proposed crops and clicks are checked against the instruction throughout the process, and the final local position is mapped back to the original screen coordinates. Experiments on four GUI grounding benchmarks and three general-purpose VLM backends show that GUI-Lens improves overall grounding accuracy by up to 24.9 percentage points and achieves state-of-the-art performance with GPT-5.5.

</details>


### [59] [3DGSI-Assessor: A Large-Scale Dataset and An LMM-based Method for 3D Gaussian Splatting Image Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03279)
*Yuke Xing,Jiarui Wang,William Gordon,Zhu Li,Guangtao Zhai,Yiling Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了3DGS-IEval-15K+，一个大规模、多维度的3D高斯溅射（3DGS）压缩图像质量评估（IQA）数据集，包含10个场景、15,200张图像、45,600个MOS评分（涵盖整体、几何和颜色三个维度）；并基于此提出3DGSI-Assessor框架，利用大多模态模型（LMM）集成全局语义和维度特定的局部特征，在单次前向传播中同时预测三个质量维度，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IQA指标无法捕捉3DGS训练和压缩中引入的表示特定失真（如浮动伪影和表面散射），且现有指标仅报告单一总体分数，无法分别诊断几何和颜色属性独立压缩带来的维度特定失真，缺乏针对压缩3DGS的多维质量评估基准。

Method: 构建3DGS-IEval-15K+数据集：从10个多样化场景、6种代表性3DGS算法、系统设计的压缩级别和20个战略性选择的视角（涵盖训练视角和挑战性新视角）生成15,200张图像，并标注45,600个MOS（分为整体、几何、颜色三个维度）。在此基础上提出3DGSI-Assessor，一个集成全局语义与维度特定局部特征的大多模态模型（LMM）框架，单次前向传播同时预测三个质量维度。

Result: 3DGSI-Assessor在3DGS-IEval-15K+上达到最先进性能，并在其他NVS基准上展现出有竞争力的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 本文填补了压缩3DGS多维图像质量评估的空白，为3DGS压缩的维度化诊断提供了新的数据基准和评估框架，数据集和代码将公开发布。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has become a dominant representation for real-time novel view synthesis (NVS), yet its storage footprint makes compression indispensable for practical deployment. 3DGS training and compression introduce representation-specific distortions such as floating artifacts and surface scattering, which conventional image quality assessment (IQA) metrics fail to capture. Moreover, the independent compression of geometric and color attributes may lead to decoupled dimension-specific distortions that must be diagnosed separately, yet existing metrics report only a single overall score. To address these gaps, we present 3DGS-IEval-15K+, a large-scale, multi-dimensional IQA dataset for compressed 3DGS, comprising 15,200 images from 10 diverse scenes, produced by 6 representative 3DGS algorithms at systematically designed compression levels and rendered from 20 strategically selected viewpoints spanning both training views and challenging novel views, annotated with 45,600 mean opinion scores (MOSs) across overall, geometry, and color quality. Based on 3DGS-IEval-15K+, we propose 3DGSI-Assessor, an all-in-one 3DGS IQA framework that integrates global semantic and dimension-specific local features within a large multimodal model (LMM), predicting all three dimensions in a single forward pass. 3DGSI-Assessor achieves state-of-the-art performance on 3DGS-IEval-15K+, and exhibits competitive generalization on other NVS benchmarks. Dataset and code will be released at https://github.com/YukeXing/3DGSI-Assessor.

</details>


### [60] [Test-Time Scaling for Safe Text-Guided Image Generation via Intermediate Clean Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03284)
*Jinya Sakurai,Shueicheng Yan,Xun Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于测试时安全干预的文本到图像扩散模型内容安全方法，利用生成过程中估计的中间干净图像和稀疏边际目标检测违规概念，并通过截断反向传播优化文本条件空间中的结构化低秩残差进行即时干预，实现权重保持、低延迟且可扩展的安全生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像扩散模型面临安全与政策合规挑战，良性或对抗提示可能生成违规内容（如裸体、受保护知识产权）。现有的基于训练的遗忘方法计算昂贵且易与通用能力产生灾难性干扰；而现有测试时防御方法主要以提示为中心，仅修改文本描述，忽视了视觉信号的检测价值。

Method: 提出利用扩散生成过程中估计的中间干净图像，使用稀疏边际目标检测被禁止概念；检测到违规时，通过截断反向传播在文本条件空间中优化一个结构化低秩残差进行即时干预。该设计实现权重保持的检测、非违规推理延迟几乎不随最大预算增加而改变，并可通过测试时扩展灵活调节安全性能。

Result: 在Stable Diffusion v1.4和v3.5上的大量实验（用于裸体去除、知识产权保护和风格擦除）表明，与先前的权重保持基线相比，该方法在抑制率、保真度和保留度方面均表现出优越性能。

Conclusion: 该方法为安全生成部署提供了一个可扩展且灵活的解决方案，在权重保持的同时实现高效、灵活的安全干预。

Abstract: Ensuring safety and policy compliance in text-to-image diffusion models remains a critical challenge, as benign or adversarial prompts can often elicit prohibited content, e.g. nudity and protected intellectual property. While training-based unlearning methods are effective, they are computationally expensive and prone to catastrophic interference with general capabilities.
