A motion prediction network maps temporal radar sequences to generalized coordinates, and differentiable forward kinematics converts predicted joint angles into 3D positions. A contact classification loss encourages physically plausible foot-ground interaction. Under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation on 11 healthy participants performing rehabilitation exercises, the framework achieves 6.456 +/- 1.759 cm mean per-joint position error (MPJPE), 8.083 +/- 0.884 degrees mean per-joint angle error (MPJAE), 0.935 +/- 0.009 contact classification F1, and 3.4 +/- 1.3 % scaling error. This proof-of-concept study demonstrates the feasibility of recovering interpretable biomechanical descriptors from a single low-cost radar sensor in a controlled laboratory setting, a prerequisite for future clinical motion analysis.

</details>


### [94] [When Do Fewer Visual Tokens Accelerate Multimodal Inference? A Break-Even Study Across Decision Locations and Hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03649)
*Hao Dou,Ruiwen Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉语言模型中减少视觉token是否真的能降低端到端延迟。通过可复现的实验协议，发现更少的视觉token并不保证更低的延迟，并对比了视觉前路由（pre-vision routing）与视觉后剪枝（post-vision pruning）两种策略的延迟收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作普遍假设减少视觉token数量可以降低推断延迟，但这一假设缺乏系统性验证，且未考虑决策开销、共享计算以及不同策略可避免的操作差异。

Method: 提出可复现的实验协议，通过阶段级分解来调和各组件与实测端到端延迟之间的关系。测试了两种自回归探针（autoregressive probes）、一个轻量级视觉后预测器（post-vision predictor）以及一个视觉前图像尺寸规则（pre-vision image-size rule），并在RTX 3090和A100两种GPU上进行评估，使用配对置信区间和保守的all-pairs Holm校正进行统计检验。

Result: 在30个示例的预实验中，即使有状态复用，两种自回归探针仍比Full方案更慢。轻量级视觉后预测器在两种GPU上均产生低于零的配对置信区间，且经Holm校正后仍然显著。视觉前图像尺寸规则在两种GPU上也产生低于零的区间，但校正后不再显著。视觉前路由可通过避免预处理和视觉编码获得结构性优势；在A100上，该优势抵消了后视觉策略近8倍的token缩减量（意味着后视觉策略减少了约8倍的token，但仍不敌前路由的延迟收益）。

Conclusion: 减少视觉token并不等同于降低端到端延迟；视觉前路由在结构上比视觉后剪枝更有潜力降低延迟，但需要谨慎的统计验证来区分真实的延迟收益与策略间的权衡效应。

Abstract: Fewer visual tokens do not guarantee lower end-to-end latency. We evaluate break-even with a reproducible protocol that accounts for decision overhead, shared work, and the operators each policy can avoid. A stage-level decomposition reconciles these components with measured end-to-end latency. In a 30-example pilot, the two tested autoregressive probes remain slower than Full despite state reuse. A lightweight post-vision predictor yields paired confidence intervals below zero on RTX 3090 and A100 and remains significant after a conservative all-pairs Holm correction. A pre-vision image-size rule also yields intervals below zero on both GPUs, although neither comparison remains significant after the same correction. Pre-vision routing has a structural opportunity unavailable to post-vision pruning: it can avoid preprocessing and vision encoding. On A100, this opportunity outweighs a nearly eightfold larger downstream token reduction by the post-vision policy. Reported quality is conditional on examples answered correctly by Full and is not benchmark accuracy.

</details>


### [95] [Morphology-Aware Implicit Super-Resolution Network for Pathological Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03664)
*Jiaming Liang,QiHui Han,Haolin Chen,Chengxin Ye,Jiawen Liu,Jiazhou Chen,Xiaoqi Sheng,Hongmin Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Morph-ISR，一个面向数字病理学的形态感知隐式超分辨率框架，通过连续坐标重建、自适应核生成器和预训练细胞分割网络的语义引导，实现亚像素精度的细胞边界和核纹理恢复，在TCGA和SurGen数据集上LPIPS和ST-LPIPS分别较次优方法降低38.37%和39.55%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字病理诊断依赖高分辨率全切片图像，但临床部署常受限于硬件成本。超分辨率是低分辨率采集的经济替代方案，但现有SR方法在复杂组织变异性下难以保持细粒度细胞形态，存在纹理过度平滑和结构边界模糊的问题。

Method: Morph-ISR将超分辨率重构为基于连续坐标的隐式重建问题，包含两个核心组件：(1) 隐式位置感知核生成器(IPKG)，自适应建模空间变化的组织形态；(2) 形态保真先验(MFP)，利用预训练细胞分割网络的语义引导，施加边界保持和区域感知的重构约束，从而改善关键细胞边界和核纹理的表征。

Result: 在TCGA和SurGen数据集上，Morph-ISR在LPIPS和ST-LPIPS指标上均达到最优，较次优方法分别降低38.37%和39.55%，同时保持较强的PSNR和SSIM，验证了其在保留诊断相关细胞边界和核纹理方面的优越性。

Conclusion: Morph-ISR通过形态感知的隐式超分辨率框架显著提升数字病理图像中的诊断关键细节还原能力，紧凑的参数化和高吞吐量支持高效的边缘部署，为临床病理诊断提供了一种可行的计算增强方案。

Abstract: Accurate diagnosis in Digital Pathology (DP) relies on high-resolution whole-slide images, yet clinical deployment is often limited by hardware costs. Super-Resolution (SR) offers a promising alternative by computationally enhancing low-resolution acquisitions. However, existing SR methods frequently struggle to preserve fine-grained cellular morphology, leading to texture oversmoothing and blurred structural boundaries under complex tissue variability. To address this issue, we propose Morph-ISR, a morphology-aware implicit super-resolution framework for DP that restores diagnostically relevant details with sub-pixel precision. Morph-ISR reformulates SR as a continuous coordinate-based reconstruction problem and integrates an Implicit Position-aware Kernel Generator (IPKG) to adaptively model spatially varying tissue morphology. To further enhance structural fidelity, a Morphological Fidelity Prior (MFP) is introduced, leveraging semantic guidance from a pre-trained cell segmentation network to enforce boundary-preserving and region-aware reconstruction, thereby improving the representation of critical cellular boundaries and nuclear textures. Experiments on TCGA and SurGen datasets show that Morph-ISR achieves the best LPIPS and ST-LPIPS among the evaluated methods, reducing them by up to 38.37% and 39.55%, respectively, over the second-best methods while maintaining strong PSNR and SSIM. These results demonstrate superior preservation of diagnostically relevant cellular boundaries and nuclear textures, while compact parameterization and high throughput support efficient edge deployment. Code and trained models will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [96] [XiDepth: a Lightweight and Efficient Network for Self-supervised Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03666)
*Elena Izzo,Riccardo Toniolo,Lamberto Ballan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出XiDepth，一种基于XiNet算子块的轻量级自监督单目深度估计架构，仅0.8M参数即在KITTI数据集上达到最先进性能，并在树莓派4上验证其嵌入式适用性，相比领先方法减少40% FLOPs和35%能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督单目深度估计无需昂贵的深度传感器和真值标注，适合计算资源受限的设备部署。然而现有最先进模型采用深度可分离卷积和注意力机制，这些操作在嵌入式环境中能耗高且存在兼容性问题。因此需要设计既保持性能又具备低计算复杂度和低能耗的轻量级网络架构。

Method: 提出XiDepth轻量级架构，其核心基于XiNet算子块（operator block），该设计在增强特征提取能力的同时保持低计算复杂度和低能耗需求，规避了深度可分离卷积和注意力机制在嵌入式环境中的高能耗与兼容性缺陷。

Result: 在KITTI数据集上，XiDepth以仅0.8M参数实现最先进的深度估计性能。在树莓派4上的实际测试验证了其嵌入式部署的适用性，相比领先方法实现40%的FLOPs降低和35%的能耗降低。

Conclusion: XiDepth通过轻量级架构设计成功在自监督单目深度估计任务中实现了性能与资源效率的良好权衡，证明了其在计算受限嵌入式设备上部署的可行性和实用价值。

Abstract: Self-supervised monocular depth estimation has emerged as an appealing solution to design lightweight and effective models for deployment on computationally constrained devices due to its reduced reliance on expensive depth sensors. By eliminating the need for ground-truth annotations and leveraging the simplicity of monocular camera setups, this approach facilitates cost-effective data collection and broad applicability across fields such as computer vision and robotics. A critical challenge is achieving resource-efficient neural networks without compromising the overall performance. State-of-the-art models generally adopt depth-wise convolutions and attention mechanisms; however, these functions often incur high energy costs and face compatibility issues in embedded environments. To address this, we propose XiDepth, a lightweight architecture based on the XiNet operator block, designed to enhance feature extraction while maintaining low computational complexity and energy demand. On the KITTI dataset, XiDepth achieves state-of-the-art performance with only 0.8M parameters. Tests on a Raspberry Pi 4 further confirm its suitability for real-world embedded applications, reducing FLOPs by 40% and energy consumption by 35% compared to leading methods.

</details>


### [97] [Keep the Needle, Prune the Haystack: Defect-Preserving Token Pruning for Efficient Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03681)
*Yanning Hou,Jingyuan Zhang,Xiaoyun Wang,Qixiang Ma,Sihang Zhou,Ke Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: KeepAD是一种保持缺陷的token剪枝框架，用于零样本视觉异常检测。它通过高召回率的异常感知路由，在浅层结合覆盖保持选择与确定性异常救援，在深层利用冻结的normal/abnormal原型在图像自适应token预算下进行剪枝，将token保留率降至20%以下，同时将AUROC降级控制在2.7个百分点内，在最激进配置下实现7.9倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本异常检测方法对所有图像和空间token进行密集计算，而真实场景中正常样本占主导且异常仅占小区域，存在大量冗余计算。token剪枝虽然可行，但带来不对称风险：移除正常token只是冗余计算，移除异常token则可能消除唯一检测证据，尤其在浅层（剪枝收益最大但异常语义不可靠）风险更严重。

Method: 提出KeepAD框架：(1) 浅层采用2×2局部patch邻域的覆盖保持选择结合确定性异常救援，降低丢失细微缺陷的风险；(2) 深层利用冻结的normal和abnormal原型在图像自适应token预算下引导剪枝，激进移除低风险正常token同时保留局部异常证据；(3) 通过密集到稀疏的自蒸馏监督早期token路由，不引入额外推理开销。

Result: 在六个工业基准和七个医学零样本异常检测基准上，KeepAD将token保留率降至20%以下，图像级和像素级AUROC平均降级限制在2.7个百分点以内；在最激进操作点相对最强CLIP基线实现7.9倍加速。

Conclusion: KeepAD通过异常感知的高召回率token路由在保持异常检测性能的同时大幅减少计算量，验证了token剪枝在异常检测中可行且高效，缓解了剪枝可能丢失异常证据的核心风险。

Abstract: Zero-shot visual anomaly detection has achieved remarkable progress, with recent vision-only approaches further improving performance while simplifying the inference pipeline. However, existing methods typically perform dense computation over all images and spatial tokens, despite the fact that normal samples dominate real-world scenarios and anomalies usually occupy only small regions. Token pruning offers a promising solution, but introduces an asymmetric pruning risk in anomaly detection: retaining normal tokens mainly incurs redundant computation, whereas removing anomalous tokens may eliminate the only evidence for detection and localization. This risk is particularly severe in early layers, where pruning provides the greatest computational benefit but anomaly semantics remain unreliable. We propose KeepAD, a defect-preserving token pruning framework that formulates token selection as high-recall, anomaly-aware routing. In shallow layers, KeepAD combines coverage-preserving selection over local $2\times2$ patch neighborhoods with deterministic anomaly rescue to reduce the risk of discarding subtle defects. In deeper layers, frozen normal and abnormal prototypes guide pruning under an image-adaptive token budget, aggressively removing low-risk normal tokens while preserving local anomaly evidence. Dense-to-sparse self-distillation further supervises early token routing without introducing additional inference overhead. Experiments on six industrial and seven medical zero-shot anomaly detection benchmarks show that KeepAD reduces the token retention ratio to below $20\%$, while limiting the average degradation in image-level and pixel-level AUROC to within $2.7$ percentage points. At the most aggressive operating point, KeepAD achieves a $7.9\times$ speedup over the strongest CLIP-based baseline.

</details>


### [98] [MultiCompose: Multi-Concept Personalized Composition with Per-Subject Attribute Binding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03708)
*Ruirui Zhang,Zhengkai Zhao,Pan Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MultiCompose框架，通过解耦单概念个性化与多主体推理，并引入语义保持正则化和两阶段推理过程，解决文本到图像扩散模型中多主体个性化时的身份退化与属性错配问题；同时提出MSP-Bench基准，联合评估身份保真度、属性绑定准确性和属性错配三个维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像的扩散模型虽能实现个性化概念生成，但在单张图像中同时包含多个个性化主体且每个主体绑定用户指定属性（如服装、配饰、手持物）的场景尚未被解决。多个概念检查点同时激活时会产生重叠的交叉注意力响应，导致主体身份退化与属性错配。此外，现有基准无法在这些多主体个性化场景下联合评估这两种失败模式。

Method: MultiCompose采用两个核心技术：1) 语义保持正则化（semantic preservation regularization），在微调阶段维持属性绑定能力；2) 两阶段推理过程，自动建立主体布局并通过空间互斥掩码组合各概念的预测。同时提出MSP-Bench基准，通过双路径协议联合评估ID（身份保真度）、BIND（属性绑定准确度）和MIS（属性错配）三个指标。

Result: 实验表明MultiCompose在传统指标和MSP-Bench上均优于现有方法，并验证了MSP-Bench能够揭示传统指标忽略的失败模式。

Conclusion: MultiCompose有效解决了多主体个性化生成中的身份退化与属性错配问题，MSP-Bench为多主体个性化场景提供了更全面的评估标准，代码已开源。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models enable personalization of specific visual concepts from a small number of reference images. However, generating a single image that contains multiple personalized subjects, each bound to user-specified attributes such as clothing, accessories, and held objects, remains largely unaddressed. Without explicit spatial constraints, concurrently activated concept checkpoints produce overlapping cross-attention responses, causing per-subject identity degradation and attribute misalignment. Moreover, no established benchmark jointly evaluates these two failure modes in the personalized multi-subject setting. We present MultiCompose, a composition framework that decouples per-concept personalization from multi-subject inference. A semantic preservation regularization maintains attribute binding capacity during fine-tuning, while a two-phase inference procedure automatically establishes subject layout and composes per-concept predictions through spatially exclusive masks. We further introduce MSP-Bench, a benchmark that jointly evaluates identity fidelity (ID), attribute binding accuracy (BIND), and attribute misalignment (MIS) through a dual-pathway protocol. Experiments show that MultiCompose outperforms existing methods on both conventional metrics and MSP-Bench, confirming the benchmark's ability to reveal failure modes that conventional metrics overlook. Code is available at https://github.com/I2-Multimedia-Lab/MultiCompose

</details>


### [99] [Attention is Case-Sensitive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03711)
*Maximilian Dillitzer,Tin Stribor Sohn,Jason J. Corso,Michael Auerbach*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统性地实证研究了"大小写切换效应"：当目标信息以交替大小写或全大写形式呈现于小写上下文中时，能集中LLM的内部注意力。该效应在13个模型（9个LLM和4个VLM）中普遍存在，但注意力的集中并不必然提升下游任务准确率，在高熵场景（交替大小写）中甚至会降低性能。推理模型的"思考"阶段可缓冲此效应，VLM中该效应会跨模态部分迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视觉感知中，大写字母是自然显著的注意力线索。作者推测LLM可能具有类似的特性，即字母大小写能调制内部注意力分配，但这一潜在属性此前未被系统探索。作者希望将其作为预训练Transformer的潜在属性进行特征化，而非提出一种启发式方法。

Method: 在13个模型（9个LLM和4个VLM）上，使用多样化的分词方案，将目标信息以交替大小写或全大写格式嵌入小写上下文中，通过内部注意力分析量化"大小写效应"对注意力分布的影响，并评估其对下游任务准确率的影响。同时分析推理模型与非推理模型的差异，以及VLM中跨模态注意力如何被重构。

Result: 大小写效应在所有评测的非推理模型中普遍存在；注意力集中与任务准确率之间存在分歧——集中注意力并不自动提升准确率，交替大小写等高熵场景会降低准确率；推理模型的"思考"阶段作为语义缓冲减弱了排版敏感性；VLM中该效应部分迁移，主要表现为宏观上视觉注意力向文本提示的脱离开放，以及次要的残余视觉注意力向目标区域的集中。

Conclusion: 大小写可作为一种零样本注意力引导机制，无需模型访问或微调，揭示了预训练如何内化排印强调的新基础理解。作者将此效应框定为预训练Transformer此前未被充分探索的潜在属性，而非一种规范性的方法。

Abstract: In human visual perception, uppercase lettering serves as a natural salience cue that captures attention within lowercase text. In this paper, we present a systematic empirical characterization study revealing that Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit an analogous property: letter casing modulates internal attention allocation. Through analysis across 13 models, nine LLMs and four Vision-Language Models (VLMs), with diverse tokenization schemes, we show that formatting target information in alternating or uppercase against a lowercase context concentrates attention on those textual spans. In text this effect is universal, holding across every evaluated non-reasoning model. We frame it as a previously under-explored latent property of pretrained transformers rather than a prescriptive method. Our investigation reveals a central attention-performance divergence: while this "casing effect" robustly shifts attention, its impact on downstream accuracy is non-trivial, increased concentration does not inherently improve task accuracy and, in high-entropy contexts like alternating case, can degrade it. We further identify a boundary condition: the deliberative "thinking" phase in reasoning models acts as a semantic buffer that mitigates typographic sensitivity in text. Extending the study to VLMs, we find the effect transfers partially: the same prompt-side casing reorganizes cross-modal attention along two coupled axes, predominantly a macroscopic disengagement from the image toward the text prompt, and secondarily a concentration of the residual visual attention on the target region. By isolating casing as a zero-shot mechanism for attention steering that requires no model access or fine-tuning, we provide a new foundational understanding of how pretraining internalizes typographic emphasis.

</details>


### [100] [Towards Reliable and Reproducible Fetal Brain Biometry: A Deep Learning Approach Using MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03724)
*Francesca Maccarone,Marina Di Stefano,Giorgio Longari,Giulia Frigerio,Gloria Rizzato,Rocco Prudentino,Nivedita Agarwal,Tommaso Ciceri,Denis Peruzzo,Simone Melzi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于深度学习的全自动胎儿脑MRI生物测量框架，通过3D CNN回归解剖学地标并结合几何优化，在3D超分辨率重建图像上实现五个关键生物指标的准确、可解释且可复现的测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胎儿脑部生物测量是评估脑发育、估算胎龄和检测异常的关键手段，但临床中人工测量耗时且存在变异。现有的自动化方法重复性差，尤其缺乏能提供解剖学可解释地标定位的可靠方法。

Method: 提出一个四步流水线：(1) 从脑分割标签图训练3D卷积神经网络回归地标坐标；(2) 对每个测量指标进行特定几何优化以精化地标位置；(3) 基于精化后的地标计算线性测量值；(4) 在两个公开胎儿MRI数据集上进行评估，涵盖不同扫描仪和协议（胎龄20-37周），评估五个关键生物测量指标。

Result: 在两个公开数据集（共150个体积）上，与现有唯一自动化流水线相比，所提方法在大多数测量指标上达到相当或更高的精度。研究同时评估了测量准确性和地标定位能力（定量指标和视觉评估）。

Conclusion: 提出了一种简单可靠的胎儿脑生物测量自动化流水线，具有高效性、可解释性和可扩展性，能够支持临床工作流集成，为胎儿脑发育评估提供可复现的量化工具。

Abstract: Fetal brain biometry is essential for quantitative assessment of brain development, supporting gestational age estimation, developmental monitoring, and detection of abnormalities. In clinical practice, measurements are manually performed, making them time-consuming and prone to variability. While automated approaches have been proposed, reproducible methods remain limited, particularly those providing anatomically interpretable landmark localization. We present a fully automated deep learning-based framework for reliable and reproducible brain biometry from 3D super-resolution-reconstructed fetal brain MRI. The proposed four-step pipeline derives biometric parameters by jointly estimating linear measurements and their corresponding anatomical landmarks. A 3D convolutional neural network is trained to regress landmark coordinates from brain segmentation label maps, followed by measurement-specific geometric optimization to refine landmark positions and compute measurements. The pipeline is evaluated on two publicly available fetal MRI datasets comprising 150 volumes (gestational age range: 20-37 weeks) acquired across different scanners and protocols, assessing five key biometric measurements across varying acquisition settings and providing a comprehensive evaluation of both measurement accuracy and landmark localization using quantitative metrics and visual assessment. Compared with the only available automated pipeline, the proposed method achieves comparable or improved accuracy for most measurements. In conclusion, we introduce a straightforward pipeline for reliable biometry estimations, with efficiency, interpretability and scalability that support integration into clinical workflows.

</details>


### [101] [TDVR: Joint Text Disambiguation and Viewpoint Reasoning for Zero-Shot 3D Visual Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03763)
*Qingxi Du,Junbo Wang,Yuke Li,Yining Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TDVR是一个无需训练的零样本3D视觉定位推理框架，通过构建语义3D场景图、利用LLM对模糊查询进行消歧、推理最优视角来解决现有方法在模糊文本和视角缺失方面的不足，在ScanRefer数据集上Acc@0.25和Acc@0.5分别提升15.25%和14.46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本3D视觉定位方法受限于两个关键瓶颈：一是查询文本具有歧义性，导致目标定位不准确；二是输入视点不足，难以从缺失视角中正确识别目标对象。这两个问题严重制约了零样本3D视觉定位的有效性。

Method: TDVR框架包含四个关键步骤：(1) 从3D点云的检测实例中构建语义3D场景图；(2) 将原始查询、外观和空间关系描述输入LLM进行融合，通过链式思维推理生成结构化消歧查询；(3) 基于场景图和结构化查询进行视角推理，获取最优视点以解决视角缺失问题，并区分干扰对象；(4) 通过计算类别文本和外观图像与查询的特征向量相似度，整合视角得分、混淆得分、类别得分和外观得分来确定目标对象。

Result: 在公开ScanRefer数据集上，TDVR在Acc@0.25和Acc@0.5指标上分别超越现有最优方法15.25%和14.46%，证明了其在模糊查询理解、视角推理和相似对象区分方面的有效性。

Conclusion: TDVR通过训练无关的推理框架有效解决了零样本3D视觉定位中的模糊查询和视角缺失两大难题，显著提升了定位精度，为无需训练的3D视觉定位提供了新的方法论。

Abstract: Zero-shot 3D visual grounding aims to localize specific objects based on textual descriptions and 3D visual input. However, the effectiveness of existing methods is significantly hindered by the ambiguous query text and deficient viewpoints. To address these issues, we propose TDVR, a training-free reasoning framework that disambiguates the input text and infers accurate viewpoints for zero-shot 3D visual grounding. First, we construct semantic 3D scene graph from the detected instances in the 3D point cloud. Subsequently, we put the original query, appearance and spatial relationship descriptions into the LLM for fusion, thereby disambiguating the initial input. We leverage chain-of-thought reasoning to generate the structured representation of disambiguated query. Then taking the scene graph and structured query as input, we get the optimal view via viewpoint reasoning to solve the problem of missing viewpoints during grounding. Based on the obtained optimal viewpoint, we further discriminate the distracting objects, enabling the model with the ability to distinguish similar instances. After that, we match the category text and appearance images with the query by computing the similarity of feature vectors. Finally, the target object was identified by integrating the viewpoint score, confusion score, category score, and appearance score. Compared with previous methods, our TDVR has stronger capabilities in viewpoint reasoning, similar object discrimination, and ambiguous query understanding. Experimental results on the public ScanRefer dataset show that our method outperforms the existing state-of-the-art methods by 15.25% and 14.46% in Acc@0.25 and Acc@0.5 respectively, demonstrating the effectiveness of our TDVR in addressing ambiguous query text and deficient viewpoints.

</details>


### [102] [AgenticVAU: Multi-Agent Explore-Verify Reasoning for Video Anomaly Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03779)
*Yuxiang Duan,Huining Li,Ao Li,Shuai Feng,Lanju Kong,Ning Liu,Jian Zhang,Xingdong Sheng,Yuntao Du*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AgenticVAU是一个无需训练的multi-agent框架，将视频异常理解建模为"探索-验证"过程，通过四个专业agent（视觉规则构建、搜索规划、视频观察、最终决策）配合共享锚点注册表进行协作，显著优于零样本和强化学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常理解（VAU）方法存在局限：要么依赖专门训练或有限观察导致泛化能力差、证据覆盖不足；要么单一agent虽然支持自适应视频观察，但将探索、观察和决策混合在统一推理过程中，缺乏角色专业化和结构化的证据协调。

Method: 提出AgenticVAU，一个无需训练的multi-agent框架，将VAU拆解为探索-验证过程：先发现潜在异常，再通过目标观察进行验证。框架包含四个专门agent分别负责视觉规则构建、搜索规划、视频观察和最终决策，通过锚点注册表（anchor registry）——一个共享的证据记忆库来绑定每次观察——进行通信。AgenticVAU交替进行广泛的时序探索、密集的局部验证和跨区间比较，直到收集到足够证据。

Result: 在ECVA、UCF-Crime和MSAD三个VAU-Bench子集上进行的大量实验表明，AgenticVAU在零样本推理和基于强化学习的基线上均取得更好表现，验证了多agent协作对视频异常理解的价值。

Conclusion: 多agent协作框架通过角色分工和结构化证据协调，能够有效提升视频异常理解的性能，且无需专门训练即可达到优于现有方法的效果。

Abstract: Video anomaly understanding (VAU) focuses on comprehensively interpreting abnormal events in videos, requiring models to identify anomalous occurrences, discover their supporting evidence, and explain the underlying causes beyond simple anomaly detection. Existing VAU methods often rely on specialized training or limited observations, restricting generalization or evidence coverage. Although single-agent alternatives support adaptive video observation, they still integrate exploration, observation, and decision-making within a unified reasoning process, offering limited role specialization and structured evidence coordination. To address these limitations, we present AgenticVAU, a training-free multi-agent framework that casts VAU as an explore--verify process, where the system first discovers potential anomalies and then verifies them through targeted observations. To achieve this, four specialized agents are introduced to handle visual-rule construction, search planning, video observation, and final decision, respectively. These agents communicate through an anchor registry, a shared evidence memory that binds each observation. Guided by this agent framework, AgenticVAU interleaves broad temporal exploration, dense local verification, and cross-interval comparison until sufficient evidence is collected. We conduct extensive experiments on the ECVA, UCF-Crime, and MSAD subsets of VAU-Bench, the results show that AgenticVAU outperforms zero-shot inference and reinforcement learning-based baselines, demonstrating the value of multi-agent collaboration for video anomaly understanding.

</details>


### [103] [OmniPack: Unified Token Compression for Efficient Omni-modal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03812)
*Wanshun Su,Yang Shi,Feihu Liu,Ziwen Yu,Yan Min,Zhuoran Zhang,Qixun Wang,Haotian Wang,Shixuan Liu,Yuanxing Zhang,Peng Wu,Chengfu Huo,Liang Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OmniPack是一个训练无关的框架，通过LLM前的结构压缩与LLM内的任务相关语义精炼相结合，在低token预算下实现高效的全模态大语言模型推理，在五个基准和三个骨干网络上取得最佳性能-效率权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全模态大语言模型处理长且高度冗余的音视频token序列时计算开销巨大，需要激进的token压缩。现有方法在低token预算下性能下降严重：LLM前的压缩可能丢弃结构重要和全局分布的证据，而LLM内的压缩未能充分利用查询条件化的音视频协作。

Method: OmniPack采用两阶段协调策略：LLM前阶段通过模态特定重要性、全局覆盖和相似度感知合并来消除结构冗余；LLM内阶段在充分的多模态交互后，通过文本引导和音视频协作进一步整合多样的任务相关表征。整个框架训练无关（training-free）。

Result: 在五个基准和三个Omni-LLM骨干网络上的实验表明，OmniPack在多种保留比例下一致取得最佳性能-效率权衡，优于所有现有方法。在Qwen2.5-Omni-7B上，OmniPack以16.7%的FLOPs保留98.0%的原始性能，以6.8%的FLOPs仍保留92.9%的原始性能。

Conclusion: OmniPack验证了"LLM前结构压缩+LLM内语义精炼"的两阶段协调范式在全模态token压缩中的有效性，为长音频-视觉序列的高效部署提供了一种无需训练即可迁移的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Omni-modal large language models (Omni-LLMs) have achieved remarkable performance on audio-visual understanding tasks, but processing long and highly redundant visual and audio token sequences incurs substantial computational overhead, demanding aggressive token compression for efficient deployment. Existing methods often degrade at low token budgets: pre-LLM compression may discard structurally important and globally distributed evidence, whereas inner-LLM compression often underexploits query-conditioned audio-visual collaboration. To address these limitations, we propose OmniPack, a training-free framework that coordinates structural compression before the LLM with task-relevant semantic refinement within the LLM. Before the LLM, OmniPack removes structural redundancy through modality-specific importance, global coverage, and similarity-aware merging. After sufficient multimodal interaction, it further consolidates diverse, task-relevant representations through textual guidance and audio-visual collaboration. Extensive experiments on five benchmarks with three Omni-LLM backbones demonstrate that OmniPack consistently achieves the best performance-efficiency trade-off across diverse retention ratios, outperforming all existing methods. Notably, on Qwen2.5-Omni-7B, OmniPack preserves 98.0% of the original performance while reducing FLOPs to 16.7%, and still retains 92.9% of the original performance with only 6.8% of the original FLOPs.

</details>


### [104] [UHP Detection: LVLMs have their Unique Hallucination Pattern in the Consistency Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03817)
*Amir Mohammad Ezzati,Kiyan Rezaee,Bardiya Kariminia,Mohamad Amin Yousefi,Asal Mohammadjafari Mamaqani,Behrad Samimi,Mohammad Hossein Rohban*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UHP检测框架，一种全黑盒的幻觉检测方法，通过扰动模态（图像vs文本）和逻辑极性（陈述vs否定）两个轴构建四种互补一致性组，提取组内和组间特征训练轻量级分类器，在AMBER和PhD基准上超越现有方法，AUC-ROC提升最高达+18.72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒幻觉检测方法仅通过单一一致性指标估计模型不确定性，但幻觉在不同行为探针下表现出多样的不确定性形态，单一指标不足以刻画其潜在行为特征。

Method: 将幻觉建模为二维结构化不确定性模式：轴上为扰动模态（图像扰动vs文本扰动），纵轴上为逻辑极性（陈述vs其否定），二者交叉产生四种互补一致性组。从这些组中提取组内与组间特征，训练轻量级分类器进行幻觉检测。

Result: 在AMBER和PhD两个基准、三个LVLM上，UHP检测一致优于现有黑盒和白盒基线，相对最强黑盒方法AUC-ROC提升最高+18.72%，AUC-PR提升最高+20.07%。消融实验证明四个一致性组提供互补信息，跨数据集评估显示学习到的模式可跨基准泛化。

Conclusion: 单一一致性指标不足以全面刻画LVLM幻觉行为，采用多维结构化不确定性模式（扰动模态×逻辑极性）能更有效捕获幻觉的多种表现，且该模式具有模型特异性和跨基准泛化性。

Abstract: Large vision--language models (LVLMs) demonstrate strong multimodal reasoning capabilities but remain prone to hallucination, where model predictions are not grounded in visual evidence. Existing black-box hallucination detection methods estimate uncertainty through a single consistency metric, implicitly assuming that model uncertainty can be adequately characterized by a single measure. However, hallucinations exhibit diverse manifestations of uncertainty across different behavioral probes, making a single measure insufficient to characterize their underlying behavior. We propose \emph{Unique Hallucination Pattern (UHP) Detection}, a fully black-box framework that models hallucination as a structured uncertainty pattern defined by two axes: perturbation modality (image vs.\ text) and logical polarity (a statement vs.\ its negation). Their intersection produces four complementary consistency groups that capture distinct manifestations of model uncertainty, from which both within-group and between-group features are extracted to train a lightweight classifier. Through comprehensive experiments on AMBER and PhD across three LVLMs, UHP Detection consistently outperforms prior black-box and white-box baselines, with improvements of up to $+18.72\%$ AUC-ROC and $+20.07\%$ AUC-PR over the strongest black-box methods. Extensive ablation studies demonstrate that each consistency group contributes complementary information and that their combination forms a structured hallucination pattern. Furthermore, cross-dataset evaluation shows that this learned pattern generalizes across benchmarks, indicating that hallucination behavior reflects a model-specific consistency pattern. \textbf{Code is publicly available at} https://github.com/amirezzati/uhpdet.

</details>


### [105] [FlowForm: Synergizing Fluid Physics with Topological Consistency for Satellite Flood Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03822)
*Zhang Weihui,Wang Ruizhi,Xu Hongye,Wang Huiqiong,Sun Li,Song Mingli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlowForm是一个用于卫星洪水图像合成的新型框架，通过SWE启发的潜空间正则化和结构感知条件机制生成地理上合理、结构保真的洪水前后配对影像，并配套发布了大规模高分辨率数据集FloodScape。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 洪水风险评估模型需要高质量的配对卫星影像，但这类数据稀缺。现有生成模型在数据增强中常产生不合理的洪水淹没空间布局并扭曲场景结构，亟需一种能保持地理和物理一致性的洪水图像生成方法。

Method: FlowForm包含两个核心组件：1) 洪水描述模块(FDM)，在扩散模型的瓶颈处对辅助潜空间施加稳态浅水方程残差的微分惩罚，实现洪水物理动力学的正则化；2) 地形锚定适配器(TAA)，在U-Net四个编码器尺度注入深度、语义和边缘特征，为生成过程提供结构感知的条件信息。此外还构建了FloodScape高分辨率配对数据集。

Result: 在标准图像生成指标的基础上，FlowForm还评估了淹没区域一致性、对地理上留出洪水事件的零样本泛化能力及各组件的敏感性。在所有对比中，FlowForm在视觉保真度、配对图像相似度和淹没区域一致性方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: FlowForm通过物理约束与结构条件相结合，有效解决了洪水卫星图像生成中空间布局不合理和场景结构失真的问题，为洪水风险评估模型提供了更可靠的数据增强手段。

Abstract: Developing robust flood assessment models requires high-quality paired satellite imagery, yet such data remain scarce for flood-specific image generation. Although generative models provide a promising means of data augmentation, existing methods often yield implausible spatial layouts of flooded regions and distort scene structures. We propose FlowForm, a framework for satellite flood synthesis that integrates SWE-inspired latent regularization with structure-aware conditioning. The Flood Descriptor Module (FDM) imposes differentiable penalties on residuals of the steady-state Shallow Water Equation in auxiliary latent fields at the diffusion bottleneck. The Terrain Anchor Adapter (TAA) injects depth, semantic, and edge features at four encoder scales of the U-Net. We further curate FloodScape, a large-scale, high-resolution dataset comprising paired satellite images acquired before and after disasters. In addition to standard image-generation metrics, we evaluate the consistency of flooded regions, zero-shot generalization to a geographically held-out flood event, and sensitivity to individual components. Across all reported comparisons, FlowForm achieves higher visual fidelity, greater similarity between paired images, and stronger consistency of flooded regions.

</details>


### [106] [Geo-Embed: Towards Unified Multimodal Embeddings for Urban Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03826)
*Jiapeng Li,Yong Li,Junjie Zhou,Fan Zhang,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了GeoMEB，一个大规模多模态地理空间嵌入基准（包含45个城市评估任务、1.32M训练样例和286K评估查询），并提出了Geo-Embed，一个统一的嵌入模型，通过指令条件化的查询-目标匹配适应共享的视觉-语言骨干网络，支持异构地理空间输入（单图、多图、文本、区域和掩码）。Geo-Embed在GeoMEB上取得了最优性能，相对最强基线提升15.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态嵌入模型和基准主要围绕通用图像-文本匹配设计，无法验证统一嵌入空间是否能支持涉及空间关系、细粒度语义和时间变化的地理空间异构任务。地理空间和城市应用需要模型在街景图像、遥感情报、文本描述、区域提案和时间变化线索等异构证据之间进行比较，但现有方法缺乏针对这些需求的标准评估体系。

Method: （1）构建GeoMEB基准：标准化45个城市评估任务，覆盖检索、视觉问答、变化检测、分类和视觉定位，并提供1.32M训练样例和286K评估查询；（2）提出Geo-Embed模型：将共享的视觉-语言骨干网络适配为指令条件化的查询-目标匹配框架，能够处理包括单图、多图、文本、区域和掩码在内的异构地理空间输入。

Result: Geo-Embed在GeoMEB上取得了最强的综合性能，相对最强基线有15.3%的相对提升。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，围绕明确的查询-目标关系（包括语义对应、跨视角对应、区域级对应和时间对应）来组织训练与评估，是推动地理空间嵌入模型发展的重要方向，为未来的地理空间嵌入研究提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Geospatial and urban applications increasingly require models to compare heterogeneous evidence across street-view imagery, remote-sensing observations, text descriptions, region proposals, and temporal change cues. However, existing multimodal embedding models and benchmarks are still largely designed and evaluated around general-purpose image-text matching, leaving unclear whether unified embedding space can support heterogeneous geospatial tasks involving spatial relationships, fine-grained semantics, and temporal changes. To address this gap, we make three key contributions. First, we introduce GeoMEB, a large-scale multimodal embedding benchmark that standardizes 45 urban evaluation tasks across retrieval, visual question answering, change detection, classification, and visual grounding, together with training collections comprising 1.32M examples and 286K evaluation queries. Second, we present Geo-Embed, a unified embedding model that adapts a shared vision-language backbone to instruction-conditioned query-target matching over heterogeneous geospatial inputs, including single images, multiple images, text, regions, and masks. On GeoMEB, Geo-Embed achieves the strongest overall performance among representative multimodal embedders, with a 15.3% relative improvement over the strongest baseline. These results motivate future geospatial embedders that organize training and evaluation around explicit query-target relations, including semantic, cross-view, region-level, and temporal correspondence.

</details>


### [107] [LiteMVS: Efficient Multi-View Stereo with Foundation Distillation and Expert Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03851)
*Tianbao Zhang,Zeyu Liu,Shuyu Wu,Fanxing Li,Zhaoxin Fan,Wenjun Wu,Danping Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiteMVS是一个轻量级多视图深度估计模型，将平面扫描几何推理与单目语义和结构先验相结合，通过语义描述符丰富代价体、Mixture-of-Experts自适应几何聚合以及从视觉基础模型蒸馏的几何先验，在ScanNetv2和7-Scenes数据集上实现高质量深度预测与3D重建，同时保持高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多视图立体（MVS）方法依赖几何对应关系，在无纹理或重复区域容易失效；而单目深度模型虽具有强图像级先验但缺乏多视图几何约束。此外，在机器人和具身智能场景中，高质量的3D几何不仅用于静态重建，更是学习时间一致的4D表示的基础。因此需要一种兼顾几何一致性和强先验的高效深度估计方法。

Method: LiteMVS的核心思想是将轻量级分割模型和大规模视觉基础模型获得的高层单目知识高效注入多视图立体框架中。具体包括：(1) 用语义描述符丰富代价体；(2) 采用Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)结构实现跨深度假设的自适应几何聚合；(3) 从视觉基础模型蒸馏几何先验以增强单目引导，且不增加推理成本。

Result: 在ScanNetv2和7-Scenes数据集上的实验表明，LiteMVS在静态场景中实现了高质量的深度预测和3D重建，同时保持了有竞争力的效率。

Conclusion: LiteMVS通过融合多视图几何推理与单目语义/结构先验，不仅提升了静态场景的深度估计与3D重建质量，还为后续时间建模和4D表示学习提供了更可靠的几何基础，展示了高效且结构感知的视觉表示学习潜力。

Abstract: Real-time 3D perception is crucial for robotics, augmented reality, and embodied intelligence applications. Existing multi-view stereo (MVS) methods primarily rely on geometric correspondences, which often fail in textureless or repetitive regions, while monocular depth models leverage strong image-level priors but lack robust multi-view geometric constraints. More importantly, in robotics and embodied manipulation scenarios, high-quality 3D geometry is not only essential for static reconstruction, but also serves as a critical foundation for learning temporally consistent 4D representations. To obtain visual representations with stronger structural awareness and greater potential for spatiotemporal extension, we present LiteMVS, a lightweight multi-view depth estimation model that integrates plane-sweep geometric reasoning with strong monocular semantic and structural priors. The central idea of LiteMVS is to efficiently inject high-level monocular knowledge, obtained from lightweight segmentation models and large-scale vision foundation models, into a multi-view stereo framework. In particular, LiteMVS enriches the cost volume with semantic descriptors and employs a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) formulation to enable adaptive geometric aggregation across depth hypotheses. Moreover, geometric priors distilled from vision foundation models further strengthen monocular guidance without increasing inference cost. Through this design, LiteMVS not only improves depth estimation and 3D reconstruction quality in static scenes, but also provides a more reliable geometric foundation for subsequent temporal modeling and 4D representation learning. Experiments on ScanNetv2 and 7-Scenes demonstrate that LiteMVS achieves high-quality depth prediction and 3D reconstruction while maintaining competitive efficiency.

</details>


### [108] [CPrefix: A Combinatorial Tensor Framework for Structured Discrete Color Mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03863)
*Yvan Richard*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CPrefix，一种用于离散多通道映射的组合可观测表示框架，通过统一的张量框架实现映射的表示、重建和结构分析。它利用多项计数观测诱导的计数张量形成离散Pascal单纯形作为潜在组合表示，在ICC显示和打印机配置文件上验证了精确重建与感知色域传输能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散多通道映射通常通过采样值表示，虽然评估准确但对其底层结构理解有限，缺乏一种能够同时支持表示、重建和结构分析的统一框架。

Method: 提出CPrefix框架，基于多项计数观测引入计数张量，其支撑构成离散Pascal单纯形——不是对观测空间的约束，而是作为潜在组合表示用于重建映射。该公式将映射的组合组织与测量值分离，暴露映射底层的可观测结构。

Result: 在ICC显示和打印机配置文件上通过潜在重建和感知色域传输进行验证。精确重建表明颜色映射具有忠实的可观测表示，重建残差揭示了底层映射与所提出表示的兼容性。

Conclusion: 该框架不依赖于观测的物理解释，适用于颜色成像、光谱测量及其他离散系统中的结构化多通道映射，具有广泛的通用性。

Abstract: Discrete multi-channel mappings are typically represented through sampled values, providing accurate evaluations but limited insight into their underlying structure. We introduce CPrefix, a combinatorial observable representation for discrete mappings, realized within a unified tensor framework that enables representation, reconstruction, and structural analysis.
  The framework is based on a counting tensor induced by multinomial counting observables. Its support forms a discrete Pascal simplex, not as a constraint on the observable space, but as a latent combinatorial representation from which mappings are reconstructed. This formulation separates the combinatorial organization of a mapping from its measured values, exposing the observable structure underlying the mapping.
  The framework is validated on ICC display and printer profiles through latent reconstruction and perceptual gamut transport. Accurate reconstruction demonstrates that color mappings admit faithful observable representations, while reconstruction residuals provide insight into the compatibility of the underlying mapping with the proposed representation.
  Although demonstrated on color transformations, the framework is independent of the physical interpretation of the observables, making it applicable to structured multi-channel mappings arising from color imaging, spectral measurements and other discrete systems.

</details>


### [109] [BanglaWild: An In-the-Wild Bengali Scene Text Recognition Benchmark for OCR and Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03884)
*Sadab Shiper,Tawsif Tashwar Dipto,Mir Md Inzamam,Eshat Tanzeem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍BANGLAWILD基准，包含2,535张孟加拉语场景文本图像，配有逐字转录、分类轴、诊断属性和正字法标准形式。评估了15个VLM和3个传统OCR系统、3种提示策略及LoRA微调，发现视觉误识别占最强系统错误约60%，而合字错误不足2%，挑战了孟加拉语OCR研究的长期假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有孟加拉语场景文本识别研究存在明显空白：已有资源针对手写文档或受限标牌解析，仅报告聚合编辑距离指标，且对传统OCR和VLM分别评估，从未在同一野外数据上同时比较。需要建立全面基准来填补这一缺口。

Method: 构建BANGLAWILD基准（2,535张图像，含逐字转录、两个分类轴、四个诊断属性和正字法标准形式）；评估15个VLM和3个传统OCR系统，使用3种提示策略；对6个开源模型进行LoRA微调；采用编辑距离指标和LLM-as-a-Judge评估；建立十五类错误分类法。

Result: 同家族中较大模型并不优于较小模型；十五类错误分类法显示视觉误识别占最强系统约60%错误，合字相关错误不足2%；视觉主导的错误分布跨架构一致；提示语言主要影响跨脚本漂移；LoRA减少弱模型的灾难性失败但无法提升强模型的上限。

Conclusion: BANGLAWILD为孟加拉语野外场景文本识别提供了首个全面基准，推翻了合字是主要错误来源的长期假设，揭示了视觉误识别是核心瓶颈，为未来研究方向提供了新见解。

Abstract: In-the-wild Bengali scene text recognition is largely unmeasured: existing resources target handwritten documents or constrained sign-board parsing, report only aggregate edit-distance metrics, and evaluate either conventional OCR or VLMs, never both on the same in-the-wild data. To address this gap, we introduce BANGLAWILD, a benchmark of 2,535 Bengali scene text images, each paired with a verbatim gold transcription, two categorical axes, four diagnostic attributes, and an orthographically standard form where the in-image text deviates from canonical spelling. We evaluate fifteen VLMs and three conventional OCR systems under three prompting strategies, fine-tune 6 open-source models with LoRA, and complement edit-distance metrics with an LLM-as-a-Judge evaluation. Our results reveal a persistent gap in which larger models within the same family do not outperform smaller ones. Our fifteen-class error taxonomy shows that visual mis-recognition accounts for ~60% of errors in the strongest systems, while conjunct-related errors contribute under 2%, challenging a long-standing assumption in Bengali OCR research; the same visual dominant profile also holds across architectures, including the one conventional baseline that reads Bengali reliably. Prompt language mainly affects cross-script drift and LoRA reduces catastrophic failures in weak models without lifting the ceiling on already competent ones. Code and data will be publicly released.

</details>


### [110] [MuRA: Multi-Rank Adaptation for Efficient and Effective Test-Time Vision-Language Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03885)
*Gengyuan Liu,Nanzhou Wang,Chang Liu,Qinwen Wu,Zhenhao Wang,Jiacong Wang,Bokui Chen,Xiangyang Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MuRA是一个动态多秩自适应框架，通过基于token级视觉复杂度动态选择和融合不同容量的适配模块，解决视觉-语言模型在测试时自适应中因固定秩配置导致的欠拟合/过拟合瓶颈，在域泛化和跨数据集基准上取得最优精度并显著降低计算与内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LoRA的测试时自适应方法依赖静态秩配置，而视觉输入天然具有变化的信息密度，固定秩会导致复杂场景欠拟合、简单场景过拟合的根本性优化折中问题。

Method: MuRA框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 多秩正交分解，提供知识保留的优质初始化；(2) 统一组件融合与连续路由器更新，可持续学习语义到秩的映射。同时提供理论证明验证该自适应机制的必要性和梯度稳定性。

Result: 在广泛的域泛化和跨数据集基准上取得最先进精度，同时显著降低计算和内存开销。动态设计在视觉最深层的表现最优，利用最短的梯度反向传播路径。

Conclusion: MuRA通过动态多秩自适应机制有效解决了传统固定秩LoRA的优化折中问题，为视觉-语言模型在分布偏移场景下的测试时自适应提供了更高效、更准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models exhibit remarkable zero-shot capabilities but suffer significant performance degradation under distribution shifts. While test-time adaptation (TTA) via Low-Rank Adaptation offers a parameter-efficient solution, we identify a fundamental bottleneck in current methods: the reliance on static rank configurations. Because visual inputs inherently possess varying information densities, a fixed rank forces an inevitable optimization compromise, leading to underfitting on complex scenes and overfitting on simple ones. To bridge this gap, we propose Multi-Rank Adaptation (MuRA), a novel framework that dynamically selects and fuses adaptation modules of varying capacities based on token-level visual complexity. MuRA synergizes Multi-Rank Orthogonal Decomposition to provide a superior, knowledge-preserving initialization, and Unified Component Fusion with Continuous Router Updating to sustainably learn semantic-to-rank mappings. Furthermore, we provide rigorous theoretical justifications mathematically proving the necessity and gradient stability of this adaptive mechanism. Crucially, MuRA's dynamic design uniquely thrives at the deepest visual layer, capitalizing on the shortest gradient backpropagation path. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MuRA achieves state-of-the-art accuracy across extensive domain generalization and cross-dataset benchmarks while significantly reducing both computational and memory overhead.

</details>


### [111] [CARE-X: Towards Clinically Useful Radiology VLMs with Auxiliary Supervision, Reward-Aligned Learning, and Tool-Augmented Measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03890)
*Mercy Prasanna Ranjit,Anirban Porya,Sathvik Joel,Niharika Vadlamudi,Nikhilesh Chowdary Eathamukkala,Prasanth V,Abhyuday Kumara Swamy,Pranay Narhari Umredkar,Pradeep Narayan,Vivek Rajagopal,Tanuja Ganu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CARE-X是一个胸部X光视觉语言模型(VLM)，通过将辅助判别监督（焦点损失分类头和复合损失定位头）与基于奖励的对齐生成（DAPO策略优化）统一起来，弥合了放射科医生需求与生成模型能力之间的差距。它在四个报告生成基准上取得SOTA性能，ReXVQA上VQA准确率达94.0% (+6.0pp)，且通过工具调用机制实现解剖测量，在五个依赖测量的临床条件下平均F1提升+43.6pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有胸部X光VLM系统仅关注流畅的报告生成，将发现分类、空间定位和医学测量作为分离问题处理（甚至根本不处理），无法满足放射科医生的实际临床需求。作者认为存在"放射科医生所需"与"生成模型所提供"之间的关键差距。

Method: CARE-X包含三大核心组件：(1) 生成骨干网络配备焦点损失分类头和复合损失定位头，与语言建模目标联合训练，提供可调决策阈值的判别式预测和精确空间定位；(2) 解耦裁剪与动态采样策略优化(DAPO)，利用任务特定奖励信号优化报告生成、VQA和空间定位；(3) 混合推理方案，将Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct与原生工具调用能力耦合，调用确定性测量工具，同时保留完整图像视觉访问。

Result: 在四个报告生成基准上取得多数指标的SOTA性能；ReXVQA上VQA准确率94.0%，超出次优基线6.0个百分点；生成式空间解码接近专用检测头的性能水平；在五个测量依赖条件上，混合推理较纯感知基线平均F1提升43.6个百分点。

Conclusion: 结构化预测与生成任务可以相互增强而非互斥；统一的辅助判别监督能同时提升分类、定位和报告生成质量；将VLM与确定性测量工具进行混合推理是解决测量依赖型诊断的有效范式。

Abstract: A clinically useful chest X-ray system must go beyond fluent report generation: it should classify findings with tunable decision thresholds, localize them spatially, and derive the anatomical measurements upon which many diagnoses depend. Today's Vision-Language Models (VLMs) treat these as separate problems, if they address them at all, leaving a gap between what radiologists need and what generative models provide. We introduce CARE-X, a chest X-ray VLM that narrows this gap by unifying auxiliary discriminative supervision with reward-aligned generation. CARE-X augments its generative backbone with focal-loss classification and composite-loss grounding heads, co-trained alongside the language-modeling objective. This auxiliary supervision produces discriminative diagnostic predictions with tunable decision thresholds and precise spatial localization while also improving report quality, providing evidence that structured prediction and generation reinforce one another. Building on this foundation, Decoupled Clip and Dynamic Sampling Policy Optimization (DAPO) leverages task-specific reward signals for report generation, visual question answering (VQA), and spatial grounding, directly optimizing the clinical quality metrics that matter in practice. The result is state-of-the-art performance on the majority of metrics across four report-generation benchmarks, 94.0% VQA accuracy on ReXVQA (+6.0 pp over the next-best baseline), and generative spatial decoding that reaches near parity with dedicated detection heads. Separately, to address measurement-dependent diagnoses, we couple Qwen3-VL-4B-Instruct with native tool-calling capabilities for invoking deterministic measurement tools, while retaining full visual access to the image. This hybrid inference yields +43.6 pp average F1 over perception-only baselines across five measurement-dependent conditions.

</details>


### [112] [NCGR: Noise-Conditional Gated Rectification for Camera Extrinsic Perturbations in BEV 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03895)
*Wenbin Pan,Wanhao Liu,Liwei Luo,Panshuo Li,Yong Xu,Renquan Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NCGR是一种针对相机外参扰动下BEV 3D检测的性能补偿方法，通过预测2D校正偏移并借助相机级门控调制，无需显式估计6-DoF外参修正即可补偿投影误差。在nuScenes动态外参扰动测试中，NCGR达到39.69% NDS，远超BEVFormer（28.00%）和CAPE（33.23%），且在外参干净时性能与BEVFormer相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于相机的外参（extrinsics）通常假设准确且固定，但实际中常发生扰动。在采用空间交叉注意力（SCA）的检测器中，外参扰动会使BEV参考点在图像平面上的投影发生偏移，导致查询从错误区域采样特征，严重降低检测性能。因此需要一种无需显式估计完整6-DoF外参修正的方法来补偿投影误差。

Method: NCGR为每个查询-相机对预测一个2D校正偏移量，并通过相机级门控（gate）来调制该偏移，以在原生可变形采样之前校正基础投影。训练时，用于构建条件和门控的扰动派生量通过调度插值逐步替换为由相机特征预测的辅助标量，从而实现无需扰动元数据的盲推理。训练采用权值共享的干净教师/扰动学生对，校正模块由两分支间的BEV一致性目标监督。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，模拟动态和静态外参扰动下评估。五相机动态压力测试中，NCGR达到39.69% NDS，显著优于BEVFormer的28.00%和CAPE的33.23%。在外参干净条件下，NCGR保持与BEVFormer相当的性能。

Conclusion: NCGR通过轻量级的基于门控的2D校正机制有效地补偿了相机外参扰动带来的投影误差，在扰动场景下大幅提升检测精度，同时在外参干净时保持基线性能，验证了该方法的普适性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Camera-based bird's-eye-view (BEV) 3D detection typically assumes accurate and fixed camera extrinsics. In detectors using spatial cross-attention (SCA), extrinsic perturbations displace the image-plane projections of BEV reference points, causing queries to sample features from incorrect regions and degrading detection performance. To address this failure mode, Noise-Conditional Gated Rectification (NCGR) is proposed to compensate for projection errors without explicitly estimating a full six-degree-of-freedom extrinsic correction. For each query-camera pair, a 2D rectification offset is predicted and modulated by a camera-level gate to rectify the base projection before native deformable sampling. During training, the perturbation-derived quantities used to construct the condition and gate are gradually replaced through scheduled interpolation by counterparts generated from an auxiliary scalar predicted from camera features. This transition enables blind inference without perturbation metadata. During training, a weight-shared clean-teacher/perturbed-student pair is used, and the rectification module is supervised by a BEV-consistency objective between the two branches. NCGR is evaluated on nuScenes with simulated dynamic and static extrinsic perturbations. In a five-camera dynamic stress test, NCGR achieves 39.69% NDS, compared with 28.00% for BEVFormer and 33.23% for CAPE. Under clean extrinsics, NCGR maintains performance comparable to that of BEVFormer.

</details>


### [113] [UniEvo-RS: Omni-Prompt Unified Remote Sensing Segmentation with Representative Exemplar-Driven Prototype Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03911)
*Kunquan Zhang,Peilang Li,Xikun Hu,Yunkai Yang,Yushan Zou,Zhiwei Zhang,Runmin Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UniEvo-RS，一个面向遥感密集标注的统一提示分割框架，通过代表性样本驱动的原型演化机制，在无需训练的情况下实现跨场景、跨类别的渐进式精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于提示的视觉-语言模型在处理遥感图像时，面对新场景、未见类别或视觉混淆背景时性能严重退化；同时现有统一范式缺乏灵活的任务路由机制来适应多意图的操作流程。

Method: 1) 构建多指令提示数据集，统一文本驱动和视觉驱动提示于单架构中，并建立动态任务路由机制；2) 提出代表性反馈驱动的免训练原型演化机制，通过对比人工标注与初始预测，将预测错误蒸馏为正负原型，在固定预算聚类记忆下增强LLM查询召回并抑制空间背景噪声。

Result: 实验表明UniEvo-RS统一了多种提示任务，在大多数设定下达到最先进性能；通过少量代表性样本的交互，在批量标注过程中无需训练即可对未见类别实现渐进式精度提升。

Conclusion: UniEvo-RS验证了利用少量代表性样本进行原型演化可以显著提升密集遥感标注的效率与泛化能力，为实际批量标注场景提供了可行的免训练解决方案。

Abstract: Prompt-driven vision-language models (VLMs) hold immense promise for accelerating dense remote sensing (RS) annotation, but static models suffer from severe performance degradation when deployed on novel scenes, unseen categories, or visually confusing backgrounds. Moreover, existing unified paradigms primarily rely on intra-image specific prompts, lacking flexible task routing to adapt to multi-intent operational workflows. In practical batch mapping, annotators typically refine a small set of representative samples before processing large datasets. Motivated by this practice, we propose UniEvo-RS, an omni-prompt unified RS segmentation framework equipped with representative exemplar-driven prototype evolution. First, we construct a multi-instruction prompt dataset that unifies text-driven and visual-driven prompts within a single architecture, establishing a dynamic task-routing mechanism for highly diverse RS annotation scenarios. Second, we introduce a representative feedback-driven, training-free prototype evolution mechanism. By contrasting manual annotations with initial predictions on exemplars, UniEvo-RS distills prediction errors into positive and negative prototypes. These prototypes enhance LLM query recall and suppress spatial background noise under a fixed-budget clustering memory. Extensive experiments show that UniEvo-RS unifies diverse prompting tasks, achieving state-of-the-art performance across most settings. Crucially, with minimal interaction on a few exemplars, it enables training-free, progressive accuracy enhancement on unseen categories during batch annotation.

</details>


### [114] [StreamDAM: Presence-Aware Memory for Real-Time Streaming Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03912)
*Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出高质量Video Object Segmentation追踪器在离线评测中表现优异，但在真实的30FPS流式协议下性能崩溃，原因是其依赖的丰富记忆流水线太慢且缺乏目标存在性判断。作者提出\method{}，通过在模型内优化使记忆机制本身达到帧率运行，并用单一学习的存在性信号统一控制记忆写入、读取范围、输出抑制与重检测，在四个基准和五个基线上成为最强流式追踪器，在时钟约束下几乎完全恢复离线精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VOS追踪器（如DAM4SAM）在离线逐帧评测中占据精度榜首，但在真实流式场景（30FPS、帧超时需复用上一帧掩码）下严重失效。问题根源在于：富记忆使追踪器精度高但更新速度慢，且输出无法感知目标是否仍存在。作者认为这两个失败都源于追踪器的记忆流水线设计，因此需要针对流式协议重新构建记忆机制。

Method: \method{}通过in-model优化使记忆机制本身以帧率运行，而非采用"临时应急"的回退方案；同时引入单一学习的存在性信号，统一调控四个关键决策：哪些信息进入记忆、追踪器回溯多远的记忆、何时抑制/延迟输出、以及何时触发重新检测。该设计允许逐帧自适应决策。

Result: 在四个基准测试和五个现代基线方法上，\method{}成为最强的流式追踪器；在时钟约束下几乎完全恢复了离线模型的精度；在最具挑战性的内容上甚至超过了其所基于的离线模型。机制分析表明固定策略无法取胜——对目标真正消失有帮助的控制会损害目标只是难以看清时的表现，因此必须逐帧决策。

Conclusion: 流式VOS需要将记忆视为可优化、可感知目标存在性的核心组件，而非简单地在离线模型外加一个回退机制。基于单一存在性信号的逐帧自适应记忆控制是兼顾流式速度与精度的关键路径，其性能甚至可超越同源离线模型。

Abstract: Quality-tier video object segmentation (VOS) trackers such as DAM4SAM top accuracy leaderboards, but they are measured offline, one frame at a time with no clock. Under an honest streaming protocol at 30 frames per second, where a frame that misses its budget is served the last mask already computed, the winner collapses: the rich memory that makes it accurate is too slow to keep up, and what it emits is blind to whether the object is even present. We trace both failures to one place, the tracker's memory pipeline, and rebuild it for streaming. \method{} makes the memory machinery itself run at frame rate through in-model optimization rather than a bolted-on fallback, and governs it with a single learned presence signal that decides what enters memory, how far back the tracker reads, when to withhold output, and when to re-detect. A mechanism analysis shows why a fixed policy cannot win: the control that helps when an object truly disappears is the one that hurts when it is merely hard to see, so the choice must be made per frame. Across four benchmarks and five modern baselines, \method{} is the strongest streaming tracker, recovers nearly all of the offline model's accuracy under the clock, and on the hardest content exceeds the offline model it is built from.

</details>


### [115] [When and Where to Look: Adaptive Visual Evidence Scheduling for Efficient Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03918)
*Ke Li,Jiayu Chen,Maoliang Li,Zihao Zheng,Hailong Zou,Hengyi Zhang,Xuanzhe Liu,Xiang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EcoFrame是一个无需训练的查询自适应视觉证据调度框架，通过熵门控预算调度和注意力引导的候选提出机制，在长视频理解中实现更高的准确率-效率权衡，相比现有方法在多VLM骨干上取得更优性能并实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的长视频理解方法在帧选择上存在两难：基于相关性的静态一次性选择方法使用固定预算和候选池，缺乏适应性；而基于智能体的调度方法虽然实现了适应性，但需要昂贵的多轮推理和交互式搜索。因此需要一种既能根据查询自适应调度证据、又保持低开销的解决方案。

Method: EcoFrame包含两个核心机制：(1) 熵门控预算调度：利用VLM推理输出的不确定性（熵）来判断当前视觉证据是否充分，若充分则提前停止，否则迭代式扩大帧预算；(2) 注意力引导候选提出：将帧级注意力转化为时间先验，在信息密集区域进行稠密局部搜索，同时在注意力分散时保持全局覆盖。

Result: 在Video-MME、LongVideoBench和MLVU三个基准上验证了有效性。在Qwen2.5-VL上，EcoFrame平均准确率达64.4，超过BOLT的63.5，同时相比AKS和BOLT提供1.85倍加速；相比基于智能体的A.I.R.，在保持可比准确率的情况下实现最高13.5倍推理加速。

Conclusion: EcoFrame证明通过利用VLM自身的推理反馈（输出熵和注意力）可以在无需训练的情况下实现低成本、查询自适应的视觉证据调度，在准确性和效率之间取得更好的平衡。

Abstract: Efficient long-video understanding requires vision--language models (VLMs) to reason over a small number of frames selected as sparse visual evidence. Existing relevance-based methods rely on static one-shot selection with fixed frame budgets and candidate pools, while agent-based schedulers achieve adaptivity through costly multi-round reasoning and interactive search. We propose EcoFrame, a training-free framework for low-overhead query-adaptive visual evidence scheduling. EcoFrame leverages the VLM's inference feedback to determine when to increase the frame budget and where to search for additional candidate evidence. Specifically, entropy-gated budget scheduling uses output uncertainty to stop early when the current evidence is sufficient or progressively expand the frame budget otherwise. Meanwhile, attention-guided candidate proposal converts frame-level attention into a temporal prior, enabling dense local search in informative regions while preserving global coverage when attention is diffuse. Experiments on Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and MLVU demonstrate that EcoFrame achieves a better accuracy--efficiency trade-off across multiple VLM backbones. On Qwen2.5-VL, EcoFrame achieves an average accuracy of 64.4, surpassing BOLT at 63.5, while providing a $1.85\times$ speedup over AKS and BOLT. Compared with the agent-based A.I.R., EcoFrame maintains comparable accuracy with up to a $13.5\times$ inference speedup. Code will be available at https://github.com/AK-DREAM/EcoFrame.

</details>


### [116] [Low-Dimensional High-Leverage Subspace Optimization: Beyond Full-Parameter Coupled Training for Neural Network Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03919)
*Peng Xia,Junbiao Pang,Zheng Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出归一化仿射预条件（NAP）方法，通过仅优化归一化层的仿射参数（即低维高杠杆子空间）来同时提升PTQ和QAT的低比特量化效果，打破传统全参数耦合训练范式，以极低代价恢复严重塌陷的低比特量化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化在紧凑网络上精度严重退化，根本原因在于传统的全参数耦合训练范式忽略了参数子空间的异质性——紧凑网络特征冗余有限，难以吸收量化误差。现有PTQ仅重构固定预训练模型，不提升模型内在的量化友好性；而QAT联合更新所有参数，导致主干网络权重与校准参数之间出现梯度耦合。需要一种针对低维高杠杆子空间进行定向优化的方案。

Method: 将归一化层的仿射参数识别为支配量化鲁棒性的低维高杠杆子空间，提出NAP方法。对PTQ：冻结骨干网络权重，在目标伪量化图中仅微调仿射参数，在下游重建前主动提升量化友好性。对QAT：引入交替式QAT-NAP模式，解耦特征学习与数值校准。理论分析证明BN仿射参数能完全抵消量化畸变的逐通道仿射分量，剩余非线性舍入和裁剪残差构成不可约误差边界；蒸馏引导的NAP作为方向性平坦度优化，将师生logit失配投影到受限子空间。

Result: 在ImageNet和CIFAR-100上的实验表明，NAP能恢复严重塌陷的低比特量化精度，持续提升基于重建的PTQ方法，并以极小的调优代价超越饱和的全参数QAT。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了针对低维子空间的定向优化原理，为高效深度学习提供了超越全参数耦合训练的新视角，表明量化鲁棒性可由极小维度的参数子空间支配。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization suffers severe accuracy degradation on compact networks, rooted in the dominant full-parameter coupled training paradigm that ignores parameter subspace heterogeneity. Their limited feature redundancy leaves little room to absorb quantization errors. Conventional pipelines adopt monolithic optimization: PTQ reconstructs fixed pretrained models without improving inherent quantization friendliness; QAT updates all parameters jointly, suffering from gradient coupling between backbone weights and calibration parameters. In this paper, we identify normalization affine parameters as a low-dimensional high-leverage subspace dominating quantization robustness, and propose Normalization Affine Preconditioning (NAP) for targeted subspace optimization. For PTQ, NAP freezes backbone weights and fine-tunes only affine parameters under the target fake-quantization graph on full-precision models, proactively boosting quantization friendliness before downstream reconstruction. For QAT, we introduce an alternating QAT-NAP schema that decouples feature learning and numerical calibration, breaking the performance ceiling of saturated joint training. Theoretical analysis confirms BN affine parameters fully cancel the channel-wise affine component of quantization distortion, while nonlinear rounding and clipping residuals form the irreducible error boundary; distillation-guided NAP acts as directional flatness optimization, projecting teacher-student logit mismatch onto the restricted subspace. Experiments on ImageNet and CIFAR-100 show NAP recovers severely collapsed low-bit quantization, consistently boosts reconstruction-based PTQ, and outperforms saturated full-parameter QAT with negligible tuning cost. This work reveals the principle of targeted low-dimensional subspace optimization, offering a new perspective beyond full-parameter coupled training for efficient deep learning.

</details>


### [117] [GeoMAR: Unleashing Geometrically Aligned Features for Masked Autoregressive Blind Face Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03923)
*Lu Gan,Hanyu Yan,Chaofeng Chen,Junqi Hu,Dan Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoMAR是一种基于几何对齐特征与掩码自回归（MAR）细化机制的盲人脸复原（BFR）框架，通过双输入特征提取管道和KV-Q交换策略生成几何对齐特征，并将一步映射重构为多步MAR粗到细生成过程，在合成和真实世界基准上取得了具有竞争力的感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于码本（codebook）的盲人脸复原方法面临两大挑战：一是退化条件下条件特征存在模糊性（ambiguous），难以提供有效引导；二是预测机制脆弱（fragile），在严重退化下难以一步映射到高质量人脸。作者旨在解决这两个核心问题，提升BFR的鲁棒性和重建质量。

Method: 方法包含两个核心创新：(1) 特征条件方面，提出双输入提取管道（dual-input extraction pipeline），提取基于组件（component-based）的几何描述，并带有显式、空间可信的锚点（explicit, spatially faithful anchors）；随后通过Aligned Geometric Priors Injector（AGPI），采用KV-Q交换策略将这些文本先验与低质量（LQ）特征融合，生成几何对齐特征。(2) 预测机制方面，将传统的一步映射重新表述为多步掩码自回归（masked autoregressive, MAR）过程，通过由可靠上下文逐步细化的粗到细（coarse-to-fine）生成来恢复复杂面部区域。

Result: 在1个合成数据集和3个真实世界基准上进行实验，GeoMAR相比现有方法取得了极具竞争力的感知质量和连贯的视觉结构，验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: GeoMAR证明了将几何对齐的特征条件机制与MAR多步生成过程相结合，能够有效克服码本式BFR在严重退化下条件模糊和预测脆弱的问题，实现鲁棒且高质量的人脸复原。

Abstract: Codebook-based blind face restoration (BFR) often suffers from ambiguous conditioning features and a fragile prediction mechanism under severe degradation. To address these challenges, we propose GeoMAR, a framework designed to unleash geometrically aligned features with masked autoregressive (MAR) refinement for robust face restoration. For feature conditioning, we introduce a dual-input extraction pipeline to extract component-based geometric descriptions with explicit, spatially faithful anchors. These textual priors are integrated with low-quality (LQ) features via an Aligned Geometric Priors Injector, which employs a KV-Q exchange strategy to generate geometrically aligned features. For prediction mechanism, we reformulate the one-step mapping into a multi-step MAR process. This coarse-to-fine generation progressively refines complex facial regions based on increasingly reliable context. Experiments on one synthetic and three real-world benchmarks demonstrate that GeoMAR achieves highly competitive perceptual quality and coherent visual structures compared with existing methods. The code is available at https://github.com/BRL-SYSU/GeoMAR.git.

</details>


### [118] [Progressive Learning of a Diffusion-based Inpainting Model for Separating Overlapped Fingerprints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03937)
*Noor Hussein,Anil K. Jain,Karthik Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的指纹分离管道，将重叠指纹分离问题建模为图像修复任务，通过对预训练的Stable Diffusion进行多阶段渐进式训练（融入指纹先验、补全部分指纹、重叠感知修复），在两个公开数据集上实现了高概率的匹配精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重叠摩擦脊线模式在犯罪现场潜在指纹提取和活体扫描场景中频繁出现，而现有方法要么依赖需要强领域知识的基于规则的取向场补全，要么训练不考虑领域特定因素的端到端深度神经网络，缺乏有效的分离方案。

Method: 将重叠指纹分离问题形式化为图像修复（inpainting）任务，以预训练Stable Diffusion为基础，分多个阶段渐进式训练扩散模型：首先融入指纹先验，然后增加补全部分指纹的能力，最后提出基于多通道条件的重叠感知扩散修复模型来重建每个分量指纹。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上的实验表明，使用所提出的基于扩散修复方法重建的分量指纹，能够以非常高的概率与其配对（mated）指纹成功匹配。

Conclusion: 扩散模型结合领域特定的多阶段训练策略能够有效地解决重叠指纹分离问题，且通过将分离任务转化为修复问题并引入指纹先验，可以有效提升重建指纹的匹配可靠性。

Abstract: Overlapped friction ridge patterns are a recurring problem in latent fingerprints recovered from crime scenes and in live-scan scenarios where residual fingerprints on the sensor may corrupt subsequent acquisitions. Existing approaches for separating overlapped fingerprints either rely on rule-based orientation field completion that requires strong domain knowledge or train end-to-end deep neural networks that do not account for domain-specific considerations. This work introduces a diffusion-based pipeline for separating component fingerprints from an image containing overlapping friction ridge patterns. We formulate the separation problem as an inpainting task and progressively learn a diffusion model for this task in multiple stages. Starting from a pre-trained Stable Diffusion model, we progressively incorporate a fingerprint prior, add the ability to complete partial fingerprints, and finally propose \textbf{overlap-aware inpainting} that reconstructs each component print using a diffusion inpainting model based on multi-channel conditioning. Experiments on two public datasets demonstrate that component fingerprints reconstructed using the proposed diffusion-based inpainting method can match with their mated counterparts with very high probability.

</details>


### [119] [Multi-Camera Trajectory Forecasting with Trajectory Tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2108.04694)
*Olly Styles,Tanaya Guha,Victor Sanchez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出多摄像头轨迹预测（MCTF）问题，通过"Which-When-Where"框架和轨迹张量（trajectory tensors）技术，在多个摄像头视角下同时预测运动目标的位置，弥补单摄像头轨迹预测在视野和长期预测上的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹预测方法主要聚焦于单摄像头轨迹预测（SCTF），而实际应用中多摄像头网络广泛存在（如监控、交通监控），单摄像头视野受限导致难以进行长期轨迹预测，因此需要一种能在多摄像头网络中进行轨迹预测的统一框架。

Method: 提出"Which-When-Where"方法，预测目标出现在哪些摄像头、何时出现以及出现在摄像头视图中的具体位置。核心创新是提出轨迹张量（trajectory tensors），用于跨多个摄像头视角编码轨迹及其不确定度，并基于此开发多种编码器-解码器MCTF模型。

Result: 构建了专门的MCTF数据库（600小时视频、15个摄像头视角），实验表明轨迹张量模型优于基于坐标轨迹的MCTF模型以及将现有SCTF方法适配到MCTF任务的方法。

Conclusion: 轨迹张量是一种有效的多摄像头轨迹编码方式，MCTF框架能够同时利用多视角的相对位置信息实现多视角的长期轨迹预测，为监控和交通等应用提供了更实用的轨迹预测方案。

Abstract: We introduce the problem of multi-camera trajectory forecasting (MCTF), which involves predicting the trajectory of a moving object across a network of cameras. While multi-camera setups are widespread for applications such as surveillance and traffic monitoring, existing trajectory forecasting methods typically focus on single-camera trajectory forecasting (SCTF), limiting their use for such applications. Furthermore, using a single camera limits the field-of-view available, making long-term trajectory forecasting impossible. We address these shortcomings of SCTF by developing an MCTF framework that simultaneously uses all estimated relative object locations from several viewpoints and predicts the object's future location in all possible viewpoints. Our framework follows a Which-When-Where approach that predicts in which camera(s) the objects appear and when and where within the camera views they appear. To this end, we propose the concept of trajectory tensors: a new technique to encode trajectories across multiple camera views and the associated uncertainties. We develop several encoder-decoder MCTF models for trajectory tensors and present extensive experiments on our own database (comprising 600 hours of video data from 15 camera views) created particularly for the MCTF task. Results show that our trajectory tensor models outperform coordinate trajectory-based MCTF models and existing SCTF methods adapted for MCTF. Code is available from: https://github.com/olly-styles/Trajectory-Tensors

</details>


### [120] [UniWorld-Design: From Pixel Generation to Layer-Native Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03971)
*Zongjian Li,Zhiyuan Yan,Chenxu Bai,Chen Chen,Haoxiang Sun,Shaodong Wang,Feize Wu,Shenghai Yuan,Bin Lin,Zheyuan Liu,Yuwei Niu,Li Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniWorld-Design是一个将图像生成从像素合成重构为结构化视觉组合的框架，以语义RGBA图层作为生成、理解和编辑的基本单元。包含T2RGBA模型（从文本生成独立RGBA资产）和I2L模型（从图像联合生成有序、完整的语义RGBA图层）。在Crello基准上，I2L在逐层RGB L1误差上降低37%，Alpha Soft IoU提升34%；T2RGBA在CLIP Score上超越LayerDiffuse和OmniAlpha。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心洞察在于：像素决定图像如何渲染，而图层决定图像如何被创建、理解和编辑。如同人类设计师通过图层而非原始像素来创作和操控视觉内容，该框架旨在赋予多模态生成模型一个图层原生的设计空间，解决现有模型直接像素合成缺乏结构化编辑能力的局限。

Method: 提出包含两个模型的框架：T2RGBA模型直接从文本生成独立RGBA资产；I2L模型以完整图像、全局指令和逐层提示为条件，联合生成有序且完整的语义RGBA图层。I2L的指令接口支持顶层分解、递归分解和目标提取，使分层成为一种可寻址的指令操作，适用于智能体编辑场景。

Result: 在Crello基准上，I2L相比Qwen-Image-Layered在逐层RGB L1误差上降低37%，Alpha Soft IoU相对提升34%。T2RGBA在CLIP Score上取得最高分，超越LayerDiffuse和OmniAlpha。

Conclusion: 由于I2L学习的是完整的语义对象而非可见像素分割，其生成的图层在移动或移除后仍保持可用性，这验证了以图层为核心的全新设计空间在图像生成、理解与编辑一体化任务中的有效性与可行性。

Abstract: We introduce UniWorld-Design, a framework that redefines image generation from flat pixel synthesis to structured visual composition, with semantic RGBA layers as the atomic units of generation, understanding, and editing. Our key insight is that pixels define how an image is rendered, whereas layers define how an image is created, understood, and edited. Just as human designers create and manipulate visual content through layers rather than raw pixels, UniWorld-Design equips multimodal generative models with a layer-native design space. UniWorld-Design comprises two models. The Text-to-RGBA (T2RGBA) model generates standalone RGBA assets directly from text. The Image-to-Layer (I2L) model conditions on a finished image, a global instruction and per-layer prompts, and jointly produces ordered, complete semantic RGBA layers. Its instruction interface supports top-level decomposition, recursive decomposition and targeted extraction, making layering an instruction-addressable operation for agentic editing. Because I2L learns complete semantic objects rather than visible-pixel partitions, its layers stay usable when moved or removed. On the Crello benchmark, I2L reduces per-layer RGB L1 error by 37% and achieves a 34% relative improvement in Alpha Soft IoU over Qwen-Image-Layered. Separately, T2RGBA achieves the highest CLIP Score, outperforming LayerDiffuse and OmniAlpha.

</details>


### [121] [CLIP-EBC: CLIP Can Count Accurately through Enhanced Blockwise Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2403.09281)
*Yiming Ma,Victor Sanchez,Tanaya Guha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CLIP-EBC，首个完全基于CLIP模型的密集人群计数方法。通过提出增强分块分类（EBC）框架，采用整数分箱和基于密度图的回归损失解决现有分类式计数方法的标签模糊和预测不准问题，在NWPU-Crowd测试集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP模型在零样本图像分类等识别任务中表现出色，但其在计数任务上的潜力尚未被充分探索。现有基于分类的人群计数框架存在两个关键缺陷：一是计数值的量化使用边界相邻的实值分箱，导致边界附近的标签歧义；二是仅关注分类误差，导致计数值预测不准确。因此直接应用CLIP于这些框架会得到次优性能。

Method: 1) 提出EBC（Enhanced Blockwise Classification）框架，使用整数分箱替代实值分箱以减少边界歧义，并引入基于密度图的回归损失来改进计数值预测；2) 在该backbone-agnostic的EBC框架内引入CLIP，构建CLIP-EBC模型，充分利用CLIP的识别能力进行人群计数任务。

Result: 1) EBC框架在UCF-QNRF数据集上可将现有基于分类的方法提升最高44.5%；2) CLIP-EBC在NWPU-Crowd测试集上达到SOTA，MAE为58.2，RMSE为268.5，较之前最优方法STEERER分别提升8.6%和13.3%。

Conclusion: 本文验证了CLIP模型在密集人群计数任务中的可行性和有效性，通过EBC框架解决了分类式计数方法的固有缺陷，实现了全CLIP架构的计数模型，并取得了state-of-the-art的性能。

Abstract: We propose CLIP-EBC, the first fully CLIP-based model for accurate crowd density estimation. While the CLIP model has demonstrated remarkable success in addressing recognition tasks such as zero-shot image classification, its potential for counting has been largely unexplored due to the inherent challenges in transforming a regression problem, such as counting, into a recognition task. In this work, we investigate and enhance CLIP's ability to count, focusing specifically on the task of estimating crowd sizes from images. Existing classification-based crowd-counting frameworks have significant limitations, including the quantization of count values into bordering real-valued bins and the sole focus on classification errors. These practices result in label ambiguity near the shared borders and inaccurate prediction of count values. Hence, directly applying CLIP within these frameworks may yield suboptimal performance.
  To address these challenges, we first propose the Enhanced Blockwise Classification (EBC) framework. Unlike previous methods, EBC utilizes integer-valued bins, effectively reducing ambiguity near bin boundaries. Additionally, it incorporates a regression loss based on density maps to improve the prediction of count values. Within our backbone-agnostic EBC framework, we then introduce CLIP-EBC to fully leverage CLIP's recognition capabilities for this task. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of EBC and the competitive performance of CLIP-EBC. Specifically, our EBC framework can improve existing classification-based methods by up to 44.5% on the UCF-QNRF dataset, and CLIP-EBC achieves state-of-the-art performance on the NWPU-Crowd test set, with an MAE of 58.2 and an RMSE of 268.5, representing improvements of 8.6% and 13.3% over the previous best method, STEERER. The code and weights are available at https://github.com/Yiming-M/CLIP-EBC.

</details>


### [122] [JoyAI-Video-Edit: Real-Time Open-Ended Video Editing with Autoregressive Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03974)
*Yicheng Xiao,Wenxun Dai,Xinran Qin,Lin Song,Maoquan Zhang,Hang Xu,Yukang Chen,Yitong Li,Guohui Zhang,Yuan Zhang,Xuying Zhang,Tommy Zhang,Jianlong Yuan,Peihao Li,Shuai Lu,Siming Fu,Chuyang Zhao,Xin Han,Jie Huang,Wenbo Li,Guoqing Ma,Wei Huang,Xiaojuan Qi,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JoyAI-Video-Edit是一个16B参数的自回归扩散框架，用于实时开放式的视频编辑。它结合分块自回归适配、源锚定分布匹配蒸馏（SA-DMD）和长时程自回归蒸馏，在无需未来帧和预定时长的情况下实现低延迟因果生成，在单个Nvidia B200 GPU上以约30 FPS的速度进行720p视频编辑，并显著优于现有流式编辑系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时视频编辑需要低延迟的因果生成和有限的计算资源，同时要保持源视频保真度和长期时间一致性。现有方法存在训练与推理不匹配、源保真度下降以及时间漂移累积等问题，且通常依赖未来帧或预定义的视频时长，限制了其在实时开放场景中的应用。

Method: 提出JoyAI-Video-Edit，一个16B参数的自回归扩散框架。核心方法包括三个组件：(1) 分块自回归适配（chunk-wise autoregressive adaptation），实现无需未来帧和预定时长的因果生成；(2) 源锚定分布匹配蒸馏（Source-Anchored Distribution Matching Distillation, SA-DMD），在两步生成过程中保持源保真度；(3) 长时程自回归蒸馏（Long-Horizon Autoregressive Distillation），减少训练-推理不匹配并缓解长时间序列中的时间漂移累积。

Result: 广泛的自动评估和人工评估表明，JoyAI-Video-Edit在短片和长视频上均大幅超越现有流式编辑系统，且与强离线系统相比具有竞争力。完整系统在单个Nvidia B200 GPU上实现了约30 FPS的端到端720p视频编辑。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了基于自回归扩散框架的实时视频编辑在保持源保真度和长期时间一致性方面的可行性，提出的三种核心技术在减少训练-推理不匹配、缓解时间漂移以及加速推理方面均有效，为实时流式视频编辑提供了新的SOTA方案。

Abstract: Real-time video editing requires low-latency causal generation with bounded computational resources while preserving source fidelity and long-term temporal consistency. We present JoyAI-Video-Edit, a 16B-parameter autoregressive diffusion framework for real-time, open-ended video editing without access to future frames or a predefined video duration. Our method combines chunk-wise autoregressive adaptation, Source-Anchored Distribution Matching Distillation (SA-DMD), and Long-Horizon Autoregressive Distillation to reduce train--inference mismatch, preserve source fidelity during two-step generation, and mitigate accumulated temporal drift. Extensive automatic and human evaluations show that JoyAI-Video-Edit substantially outperforms existing streaming editors and remains competitive with strong offline systems on both short and long videos. The complete system achieves end-to-end 720p video editing at approximately 30 FPS on a single Nvidia B200 GPU. Code is available at https://github.com/jd-opensource/JoyAI-Video-Edit.

</details>


### [123] [Video-DeepResearch: Towards the Next-Generation Multimodal Deepresearch Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03979)
*Zhen Fang,Yu Zeng,Wenxuan Huang,Yiming Zhao,Shiting Huang,Tianfei Ren,Qi Lu,Qingnan Ren,Qisheng Su,Lionel Z. Wang,Qingyu Yin,Shuang Chen,Zehui Chen,Lin Chen,Zhenfei Yin,Yao Hu,Shaohui Lin,Wanli Ouyang,Shaosheng Cao,Feng Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Video-DeepResearch (Video-DR)，将多模态代理从静态图像扩展到连续视频流，解决开放网络探索与密集时空定位相结合的挑战。针对模态偏差和参数知识泄漏两大瓶颈，提出解耦感知-探索流水线与分阶段工具解锁机制，采用SFT+GRPO两阶段训练。构建了包含200个多跳VQA实例的Video-DR-Bench基准，35B-A3B模型达到64.0%平均准确率，超越Claude-4.5-Sonnet等强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态代理在处理视频流时面临两个关键瓶颈：(1)模态偏差——代理倾向于绕过视觉工具直接使用文本搜索；(2)参数知识泄漏——模型依赖内部记忆而非真正的工具增强执行。这两个问题阻碍了代理在复杂视频理解任务中的表现。

Method: 提出Video-DR框架，包含：(1)解耦的感知-探索流水线，通过分阶段工具解锁，强制模型在网页检索前进行穷尽式跨帧视觉定位；(2)两阶段训练方案：监督微调（SFT）后接组相对策略优化（GRPO），实现自主探索并突破模仿学习上限；(3)构建Video-DR-Bench，一个基于人机协作的200个复杂多跳VQA实例基准。

Result: Video-DeepResearch-35B-A3B达到64.0%平均准确率，超越Claude-4.5-Sonnet（59.0%）5.0个百分点，显著优于GPT-5（52.5%）和Gemini 2.5 Pro（57.5%）。30B-A3B变体达到59.3%，与Claude-4.5-Sonnet相当，证明训练范式在紧凑规模下同样有效。

Conclusion: Video-DR通过解耦感知-探索策略和两阶段强化学习训练，有效解决了视频多模态代理的模态偏差和参数知识泄漏问题，在视频推理任务上取得了最先进性能，且训练范式具有规模可扩展性。

Abstract: We introduce Video-DeepResearch (Video-DR), extending multimodal agents from static images to continuous video streams, a setting that demands dense spatiotemporal grounding coupled with open-web exploration. Preliminary evaluations reveal two critical bottlenecks in current models: (1) modality bias, where agents bypass visual tools in favor of textual search, and (2) parametric knowledge leakage, where models rely on internal memory rather than genuine tool-augmented execution. To address these challenges, we propose Video-DR, featuring a decoupled perception-exploration pipeline with stage-wise tool unlocking that compels exhaustive cross-frame visual grounding prior to web retrieval. Our framework adopts a two-stage training recipe: supervised fine-tuning followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), enabling autonomous exploration that breaks the imitation-learning ceiling. Furthermore, we curate Video-DR-Bench, a human-AI collaborative benchmark comprising 200 complex, multi-hop VQA instances. Empirical results demonstrate that our Video-DeepResearch-35B-A3B establishes a new state-of-the-art of 64.0% average accuracy, surpassing proprietary Claude-4.5-Sonnet (59.0%) by 5.0 points and significantly outperforming GPT-5 (52.5%) and Gemini 2.5 Pro (57.5%). The 30B-A3B variant achieves 59.3%, competitive with Claude-4.5-Sonnet and demonstrating the effectiveness of our training paradigm even at compact scale. Code: https://github.com/Osilly/Vision-DeepResearch.

</details>


### [124] [Perceptual Anchoring: Prototype-Guided Text Calibration for Training-free Open-Vocabulary Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03991)
*Wanli Ma,Jiangwen Lu,Qinmu Peng,Xinge You*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为原型引导文本校准（PTC）的训练无关开放词汇语义分割方法，通过视觉原型校准文本嵌入，弥合通用类别概念与实例特定视觉表征之间的语义鸿沟，无需额外训练即可作为即插即用模块提升现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关开放词汇语义分割方法主要聚焦于改进视觉表征，而将仅编码通用类别概念的文本嵌入视为固定的分类参考，导致通用概念与捕获目标实例具体外观的视觉表征之间存在语义鸿沟，产生不完整掩码和错误预测。

Method: 受感知锚定中符号-知觉对应关系的启发，PTC包含两个阶段：(1) 感知阶段（Perceiving）：基于初始匹配分数选择可靠的视觉证据，构建类别特定的视觉原型；(2) 锚定阶段（Anchoring）：利用视觉原型校准对应的文本嵌入，校准强度根据视觉证据的数量自适应调整。

Result: 在八个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，PTC显著提升了六种代表性方法的性能，产生了更完整、更准确的分割结果。

Conclusion: 校准后的文本嵌入能更准确地与实例特定的视觉表征对齐，同时保留通用类别语义和开放词汇泛化能力。PTC无需额外训练或外部模型，可作为现有方法的即插即用模块，是一种简单有效的改善视觉-文本对齐的方法。

Abstract: Training-free open-vocabulary semantic segmentation (OVSS) partitions an image into semantically distinct regions based on arbitrary text descriptions, without learning any additional parameters. However, existing methods typically focus on improving visual representations while treating text embeddings that encode only generic category concepts as fixed classification references. The resulting semantic gap between these generic concepts and the visual representations that capture the specific appearances of target instances often causes incomplete masks and erroneous predictions in non-target regions. Inspired by the symbol-percept correspondence underlying perceptual anchoring, we propose Prototype-Guided Text Calibration (PTC) for training-free OVSS. In the Perceiving stage, PTC selects reliable visual evidence based on initial matching scores to construct category-specific visual prototypes. In the Anchoring stage, PTC uses these prototypes to calibrate their corresponding text embeddings, with the calibration strength adaptively adjusted based on the amount of visual evidence. Consequently, the calibrated text embeddings align more accurately with instance-specific visual representations while preserving generic category semantics and open-vocabulary generalization. Moreover, PTC requires neither additional training nor external models and can serve as a plug-and-play module for existing methods. Extensive experiments across eight benchmarks show that PTC significantly enhances the performance of six representative methods and yields more complete and accurate segmentation results. These results validate PTC as a simple and effective approach to improving visual-text alignment.

</details>


### [125] [ParVL: Parallel Scaling and Expandable Compute Allocation for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04010)
*Yang Yang,Qinyu Zhao,Mouxiang Chen,Xiaohui Li,Lixin Gu,Wenhai Wang,Hongjie Zhang,Wenwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ParVL 是一个针对多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的并行缩放框架，通过复用现有 ViT 和 LLM 骨干参数，在多个视觉和语言分支上扩展并行计算，从而在固定骨干参数预算下优化视觉与语言模态间的计算分配，提升整体多模态性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 缩放策略主要扩展模型参数或增加序列推理计算，带来较大的内存或延迟开销；更重要的是，大多数方法无法改变 Vision Transformer 与 LLM 组件之间刚性且固定的计算分配，限制了任务特定的优化空间。

Method: ParVL 框架通过复用现有 ViT 和 LLM 骨干参数，在多个视觉和语言分支上扩展并行计算。每个并行计算流在共享骨干上使用分支特定的前缀参数（prefix parameters）进行实例化，并通过约 130 亿 token 的全参数监督微调进行端到端训练。系统性地研究了 ViT 编码器与 LLM 解码器之间的计算分配权衡。

Result: ParVL 在整体多模态性能上优于同配方（same-recipe）的单分支基线模型；同时，最佳的视觉-语言计算分配因任务而异，表明灵活的模态间计算分配具有任务特定优化价值。

Conclusion: 研究验证了在固定骨干参数预算下，通过并行共享骨干分支的方式可以有效扩展 MLLM 的并行计算，且视觉与语言之间的计算分配需要根据具体任务进行动态优化，而非采用固定的分配策略。

Abstract: Existing scaling strategies for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) typically expand either model parameters or sequential inference computation, incurring substantial memory or latency overhead. More importantly, most existing methods fail to alter the rigid, fixed computation allocation between the Vision Transformer and the Large Language Model components, limiting task-specific optimization. To address this, we introduce the Parallel Vision-Language (ParVL) scaling framework for MLLMs, which scales parallel computation by reusing the existing ViT and LLM backbone parameters across multiple vision and language branches.
