This framework raises a central question: given a fixed backbone parameter budget, how should additional shared-backbone computation be allocated between the vision and language modalities? We instantiate each parallel computational stream with branch-specific prefix parameters over a shared backbone, and train the entire model end-to-end via full-parameter supervised fine-tuning on roughly 13B tokens. We systematically study the computation-allocation trade-off between the ViT encoder and LLM decoder. ParVL improves overall multimodal performance over same-recipe single-branch baselines, and the best evaluated vision--language allocation varies across tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/YangYangGirl/ParVL.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Deep Divide-and-Reduce in Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02628)
*Yusong Deng,Yanjie Li,Weijun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DDRSR方法，通过严格的数学推导扩展了符号回归中表达式分解与化简的适用范围，避免了对子表达式进行暴力搜索，在表达式分解和数值回归任务上均展现出显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习符号回归方法缺乏对表达式内在数学物理原理的深刻理解；AI Feynman方法虽利用数学性质，但其表达式化简机制适用范围窄、在复杂方程上易失败，且依赖暴力搜索子表达式，严重限制实用性。

Method: 通过严格的数学推导和证明，提出Deep Divide and Reduce in Symbolic Regression (DDRSR)方法。该方法从根本上拓宽了表达式分解与化简的适用范围，规避了暴力搜索子结构的需求，同时保证了更广泛的通用性和严格的理论正确性。

Result: 实验评估表明，DDRSR所依据的理论原则在表达式分解和数值回归任务中均带来显著优势。论文还讨论了该范式的适用场景、内在局限性及未来研究方向。

Conclusion: DDRSR通过理论驱动的表达式分解与化简策略，克服了现有方法在复杂方程上的局限性，提供了一种兼具理论严谨性和实际效用的符号回归新范式。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) is the task of discovering underlying patterns from data and representing them using mathematical expressions. Current machine learning approaches to SR often lack a profound understanding of the intrinsic mathematical and physical principles governing these expressions. While the pioneering AI Feynman method leverages the mathematical properties underlying the data, its expression simplification mechanism suffers from a narrow scope of applicability and is prone to failure on complex equations. Furthermore, its underlying mechanisms rely heavily on brute-force searches for sub-expressions, severely limiting its practical utility. Through rigorous mathematical deduction and proofs, we propose our method, Deep Divide and Reduce in Symbolic Regression (DDRSR). DDRSR fundamentally broadens the applicability of expression decomposition and reduction, circumvents the need for brute-force sub-structure searches, and ensures both wider versatility and strict theoretical correctness. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that these theoretical principles yield significant advantages in both expression decomposition and numerical regression tasks. Finally, we discuss the applicable scenarios and inherent limitations of this paradigm, alongside promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [127] [Multimodal Auto-regressive Transformer Surrogate for Modeling Variable Operations and Quantifying Uncertainty in Geological Carbon Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02629)
*Yifu Han,Louis J. Durlofsky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多模态自回归Transformer代理模型，用于在地质不确定性下对CO2地质封存中可变射孔和注入策略进行高效建模。该模型融合3D地质模型、相对渗透率标量参数和控制变量三种模态，通过自注意力融合和编码器-解码器交叉注意力的时间解码器进行自回归预测。使用4000次GEOS流动模拟训练后，在测试集上实现中位饱和度量MAE为0.028，其他量中位相对误差0.2-5%，并能捕捉注入控制从速率到井底压力的切换。代理模型进一步嵌入分层马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛数据同化流程，在合成真模型上显著降低了关键元参数（尤其断层渗透率）的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变井射孔和注入策略可以提升地质碳封存（GCS）操作的效率，但在地质不确定性条件下对这些操作进行高效建模是一个挑战。传统全阶流动模拟计算成本高昂，难以支撑大规模不确定性量化和数据同化，因此需要开发高效且准确的代理模型。

Method: 提出一个多模态自回归Transformer代理模型：使用三个独立编码器分别处理3D地质模型、相对渗透率标量参数和控制变量三种输入模态；通过Transformer编码器中的自注意力机制融合各模态特征；时间解码器通过编码器-解码器交叉注意力以自回归方式生成预测。模型基于修改的SEAM CO2地质模型（含断层系统和三个堆叠含水层），两注入井自下而上分段射孔，阶段持续时间和注水速率作为控制变量。训练使用4000次GEOS流动模拟。

Result: 在随机采样的测试集上，代理模型实现中位饱和度量MAE为0.028，其他关注量的中位相对误差在0.2-5%之间，并能准确捕捉从速率控制到井底压力控制的切换。在分层马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛数据同化中，用于合成真模型和三种操作策略，显著降低了关键元参数（尤其断层渗透率）的不确定性，后验预测的饱和度足迹、总注入量和可移动CO2质量与真模型结果总体一致。

Conclusion: 所提出的多模态自回归Transformer代理模型能高效且准确地预测CO2封存操作性能，在地质不确定性下实现了显著的不确定性缩减（尤其对断层渗透率），为地质碳封存优化和数据同化提供了一种有效的计算工具。

Abstract: The use of variable well perforation and injection strategies can improve the efficiency of geological carbon storage operations. We develop a new multimodal auto-regressive transformer surrogate to model these operations under geological uncertainty. A modified SEAM CO2 geomodel, which involves a faulted system with three stacked aquifers, is considered. The two injection wells are perforated in stages, from bottom to top, with the stage durations and individual well injection rates treated as control variables. The surrogate model processes three input modalities - the 3D geomodel, scalar parameters characterizing relative permeability functions, and control variables - through separate encoders. These are fused via self-attention in a transformer encoder, and a temporal decoder generates predictions auto-regressively through encoder-decoder cross-attention. The surrogate is trained, using 4000 GEOS flow simulations, to predict saturation and pressure at monitoring locations, total injected and mobile CO2 mass, and saturation footprints. For a new test set, involving randomly sampled geomodels and control variables, the surrogate achieves a median saturation MAE of 0.028 and median relative errors of 0.2-5% for the other quantities of interest. Importantly, it captures the switch from rate to bottom-hole-pressure control. The surrogate model is used within a hierarchical Markov chain Monte Carlo data assimilation procedure for a synthetic true model under three operational strategies. Substantial uncertainty reduction is achieved for key metaparameters, particularly the fault permeabilities. Posterior predictions for saturation footprints and total injected and mobile CO2 mass are also shown to be generally consistent with true model results.

</details>


### [128] [LLMs Can Annotate Attribution Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02632)
*Ameen Patel,Max Zhang,Nathan Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自动化电路追踪流水线，通过直接向语言模型展示特征描述并将其分组为超节点，替代人工注释步骤。实验表明该流水线生成的超节点与人工注释同样可解释，在双跳Capitals任务中97/100恢复中间跳超节点，并展示了开放探索场景中的初步概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路追踪（circuit tracing）是揭示语言模型内部计算的有效技术，但需要耗时的手动步骤将单个特征或MLP神经元分组为超节点（supernodes）。这一手动步骤严重限制了电路追踪的规模和可扩展性，因此需要自动化方案来加速和扩展该流程。

Method: 提出一个简单流水线：将特征描述直接呈现给语言模型，由语言模型将特征分组为超节点。具体包括：(1) 使用LM基于特征描述进行自动化分组；(2) 使用自动可解释性指标验证超节点质量；(3) 在双跳Capitals任务中评估中间跳超节点的恢复率；(4) 对1000个Wikipedia提示完成生成的归因图进行自动注释，并用LLM法官标记值得人工审查的图。

Result: (1) 自动生成的超节点与人工注释者在可解释性上相当；(2) 在双跳Capitals任务中，97/100个提示恢复了对应用间跳的超节点；(3) 在1000个Wikipedia归因图上成功进行了开放式的自动注释和筛选概念验证。

Conclusion: 即使是简单的自动化也能产生有意义的归因图注释，说明大幅降低电路追踪人工成本的可行性，为未来更高级的自动化电路追踪研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Circuit tracing is an exciting technique for revealing the internal computation of language models, but it requires a time-intensive manual step of grouping individual features or MLP neurons into supernodes. We present a simple pipeline for automating this step: directly presenting feature descriptions to a language model that groups them into supernodes. Using automated interpretability metrics, we confirm that supernodes generated by our pipeline are as interpretable as those generated by human annotators. On a two-hop Capitals task, our pipeline recovers a supernode corresponding to the intermediate hop in 97 of 100 prompts. Finally, we present a simple proof of concept using our pipeline for open-ended exploration, where we automatically annotate 1000 attribution graphs from Wikipedia prompt completions and then use an LLM judge to flag interesting graphs worth human review. We hope this work demonstrates that even simple automation can produce meaningful attribution graph annotations, motivating further work on automated circuit tracing.

</details>


### [129] [GeoID-PINN: Identifiability-Aware Regional Epidemic Inference with Geographic Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02633)
*Weixiong Hua,Fan Bu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoID-PINN，一种用于易感-感染-恢复-死亡（SIRD）动力学的物理信息神经网络，通过行随机源组成矩阵表示区域间的空间依赖结构，并用距离、邻接、通勤或领先-滞后信息构建的先验进行正则化。四区域模拟和路易斯安那州64县COVID-19回溯分析表明，该方法在轨迹拟合和点预测准确性上优于基线，但准确轨迹不代表能可靠恢复区域依赖结构，且对先验选择敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域流行病学监测数据反映局部传播、报告、播种及外部感染压力等混合因素，这些来源难以分离。需要一种方法能够同时拟合传染病动力学轨迹并识别区域间的空间依赖结构，同时区分轨迹拟合质量与结构恢复可靠性之间的差异。

Method: GeoID-PINN将PINN与SIRD动力学模型结合，以行随机源组成矩阵编码区域间空间依赖关系，其中每行行分配非负且和为1的源权重。该矩阵通过距离、邻接、通勤或领先-滞后信息构造的空间先验进行正则化。模型在四区域模拟（已知真值）和路易斯安那州64县COVID-19数据上评估，并与自回归负二项基线对比。

Result: 四区域模拟中，兼容距离先验下源组成误差为0.099，无正则化时为0.159，强误设先验下升至0.577，但轨迹拟合和传播尺度估计保持相似。COVID-19数据中，与自回归负二项基线相比，Forecast-Trained Geo-PINN将MSE从32,957降至11,468，MAE从70.60降至57.73；基线NLL更低（5.158 vs 5.346）。15县对照中，县邻接使MSE降6.85%，MAE降3.1%。

Conclusion: 准确的轨迹拟合不能保证区域依赖结构的可靠恢复；结构化正则化有助于提高点预测精度，但无法唯一确定边/依赖结构。在解读结果前必须进行先验敏感性和观测模型检查。

Abstract: Regional surveillance data reflect local transmission, reporting, seeding, and external infection pressure, which are difficult to identify separately. We introduce GeoID-PINN, a physics-informed neural network (PINN) for susceptible-infectious-recovered-deceased (SIRD) dynamics. The model represents spatial dependence with a row-stochastic source-composition matrix whose rows assign nonnegative source weights that sum to one. We regularize this matrix toward a spatial prior constructed from distance, adjacency, commuting, or lead-lag information. In a four-region simulation with known truth, a compatible distance prior gives source-composition error 0.099. The error rises to 0.159 without regularization and 0.577 under a strongly misspecified prior, while trajectory fit and transmission-scale estimates remain similar. Accurate trajectories therefore do not guarantee recovery of the regional dependence structure. We also evaluate GeoID-PINN retrospectively using COVID-19 data from 64 Louisiana counties. Relative to an autoregressive negative-binomial baseline, Forecast-Trained Geo-PINN reduces mean squared error (MSE) from 32,957 to 11,468 and mean absolute error (MAE) from 70.60 to 57.73. The baseline has lower negative log likelihood (NLL), 5.158 versus 5.346, indicating better distributional fit but worse point accuracy. In a controlled 15-county comparison, county adjacency reduces MSE by 6.85 percent and MAE by 3.1 percent. Similar performance across plausible priors supports structured regularization but not unique edge recovery. These results require prior-sensitivity and observation-model checks before interpretation.

</details>


### [130] [Verifier-Guided Model Discovery for Physical Dynamical Systems with Pretrained Symbolic Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02662)
*Farbod Faraji,Francesco Belardinelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种验证器引导（VG）的神经到符号工作流，以ODEFormer为符号骨干，通过动力学和物理可接受性准则从多轨迹候选方程池中筛选方程，实现对Van der Pol振荡器和涡街脱落等高维物理系统的可解释、可审计的可靠预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真物理仿真成本高昂，机器学习代理模型不透明且依赖对系统动力学的假设，限制了泛化能力。预训练Transformer虽然能将ODE轨迹映射为符号方程并提供可解释替代方案，但将其可靠迁移到高维物理数据仍是开放挑战。

Method: 提出验证器引导（VG）工作流，围绕ODEFormer符号骨干，利用动力学与物理可接受性准则从多轨迹候选方程池中筛选最佳方程实现迁移。对涡街脱落问题，采用坐标约简降低维度，再在固定和变化雷诺数下进行符号发现。

Result: 在Van der Pol振荡器上，VG在留出初始条件下优于原始ODEFormer工作流。在涡街脱落中，VG发现了固定参数的约简阶方程，能恢复基本脱落振荡器和高次谐波，无需特定尾流候选库或预设Navier-Stokes结构；跨参数模型可泛化到留出工况。重建保真度本身并不决定符号可发现性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了验证器引导的神经到符号方法论，为自然科学中可解释且物理可审计的预测提供了新途径，并强调了潜在动力学与骨干预训练分布之间的兼容性对符号发现的重要性。

Abstract: Reliable forecasting of nonlinear physical systems underpins scientific discovery and engineering decision-making. Yet high-fidelity simulations are prohibitively costly, and machine-learning surrogates can be opaque and encode assumptions about system dynamics, limiting generalizability. Pretrained transformers mapping synthetic ODE trajectories to equations offer interpretable alternatives, promising transfer without system-specific equation knowledge. Transferring them reliably to high-dimensional physical data, however, remains an open challenge. We develop a verifier-guided (VG) workflow around ODEFormer as a symbolic backbone, using dynamical and physical-admissibility criteria to select from a multi-trajectory candidate equation pool, enabling transfer. On canonical Van der Pol oscillators, VG outperforms the original ODEFormer workflow across held-out initial conditions. We then address vortex shedding, a phenomenon occurring in atmospheric and plasma systems of societal relevance, through coordinate reduction and symbolic discovery at fixed and varying Reynolds numbers. VG discovers fixed-parameter reduced-order equations that recover the fundamental shedding oscillator and higher harmonics without a wake-specific candidate library or prescribed Navier-Stokes structure, while the cross-parameter model generalizes to withheld regimes. Reconstruction fidelity alone did not determine symbolic discoverability, highlighting the importance of compatibility between latent dynamics and the backbone's pretraining distribution. This work establishes a verifier-guided neural-to-symbolic methodology for interpretable and physically auditable forecasting in the natural sciences.

</details>


### [131] [CT-HEG: A Bidirectional, Timestamp-Attributed Event Graph for ICU In-Hospital Mortality Prediction - An Architectural Ablation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02663)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin,Rahnuma Tabassum Orpita,Asaduzzaman Anik,Eklachur Rahman Bhuiyan,Marjahan Risalat,SM Wali Ullah,Asif Ahamed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CT-HEG（连续时间异构EHR图）schema，将ICU住院建模为带类型和时间戳的图（三类节点：visit、vital、lab_event，带2D边属性编码时间与数值），并以其为骨架实现CHIRP-Net——一个四层异构GATv2Conv网络。在MIMIC-IV v3.1（31,142次ICU住院，LOS≥48h，死亡率13.4%）上，五种子平均AUROC为0.8449±0.0071（AUPRC 0.4958±0.0209），集成AUROC达0.8618；消融实验显示反向边移除导致AUROC大幅下降0.1968，时间注意力边特征贡献0.0247 AUROC；校准后ECE为0.0307。研究还发现将异构边类型折叠为单一关系（参数减少7倍）在全种子上的表现优于完整模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICU死亡预测需要建模不规则临床观测间的关系，但现有序列模型忽略类型化关系结构，现有图模型则假设固定间隔输入，无法同时处理不规则采样与异构实体类型。

Method: 提出CT-HEG schema，将每次ICU住院编码为带类型和时间戳的图，包含三类节点（visit、vital、lab_event）和2D边属性（t_hours/48, value_norm）以编码时间与数值信息，无需插补。实现为CHIRP-Net，即四层异构GATv2Conv网络，在MIMIC-IV v3.1上以五种子和自举置信区间进行系统评估，并与逻辑回归、mTAND、Transformer和GRU-D对比，同时进行消融研究与温度缩放校准。

Result: CHIRP-Net五种子平均AUROC 0.8449±0.0071（AUPRC 0.4958±0.0209）；集成AUROC 0.8618（95% CI: 0.8485–0.8745）。移除反向边使AUROC下降0.1968±0.0073；时间注意力边特征贡献0.0247±0.0093 AUROC；折叠异构边类型为单一关系（参数减少7倍）反而在所有种子上表现更好；校准后ECE为0.0307。

Conclusion: CT-HEG适用于不规则EHR数据，双向连通性对模型利用输入至关重要，模型具有合理的校准性能。但还须进行外部验证、预设的时间评估和人口统计公平性审计才能断言其鲁棒性。

Abstract: Accurate ICU mortality prediction requires modeling irregular clinical observations across heterogeneous entity types. Existing sequence models handle irregular sampling but ignore typed relational structure; existing graph models assume fixed-interval inputs. We introduce the Continuous-Time Heterogeneous EHR Graph (CT-HEG) schema and evaluate which architectural choices drive predictive performance. CT-HEG encodes each ICU stay as a typed, timestamped graph with three node types (visit, vital, lab_event) and 2D edge attributes (t_hours/48, value_norm) encoding timing and value without imputation. We instantiate CT-HEG as CHIRP-Net, a four-layer heterogeneous GATv2Conv network, evaluated on MIMIC-IV v3.1 (31,142 ICU stays, LOS>=48h, 13.4% mortality) with five seeds and bootstrapped confidence intervals, against logistic regression, mTAND, a Transformer, and GRU-D, plus an ablation study. CHIRP-Net achieved 5-seed mean AUROC 0.8449+/-0.0071 (AUPRC 0.4958+/-0.0209); the ensemble achieved AUROC 0.8618 (95% CI: 0.8485-0.8745). Removing reverse edges disconnected observation nodes from the visit readout, cutting AUROC by 0.1968+/-0.0073. Time-attentive edge features contributed 0.0247+/-0.0093 AUROC. Collapsing heterogeneous edge types into one relation (7x fewer parameters) outperformed the full model on all seeds. Post-calibration ECE was 0.0307. Temporal and demographic subgroup analyses were explored but not reported here, pending follow-up work. Bidirectional connectivity was necessary for the model to use its inputs at all, and CT-HEG was reasonably well calibrated after validation-fitted temperature scaling. These results support CT-HEG for irregular EHR data, while external validation, a pre-specified temporal evaluation, and a demographic fairness audit remain necessary before any claim of robustness. Code: https://github.com/nasiruddinstudents-ctrl/chirp-net-mimic-iv.

</details>


### [132] [Sphere Retraction Normalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02668)
*Jie Zhang,Cheng-Fang Su,Yi-Jui Huang,Min-Te Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出p-SpheretNorm，一个将残差连接统一到黎曼流形框架下的一参数族角映射方法。通过在超球面上将各种retraction映射归结为单个标量设计选择——即更新幅度到旋转角度的转换方式，作者证明GeoNorm和欧几里得残差连接是这一谱系的两个极端，而度量投影和Cayley retraction则对应p=1和p=2的有限参数点。实验表明在nanoGPT上有限p值取得最优验证损失，说明指数映射并非球形残差流的最佳选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残差连接是深度网络稳定训练的标准机制，GeoNorm将其推广到黎曼流形上，但仅使用了指数映射这一种retraction。作者希望探索更广泛的retraction家族，统一欧几里得残差连接与GeoNorm，并发现比指数映射更优的规范保持方案。

Method: 作者证明在超球面上所有retraction映射坍缩为单个标量设计选择，即更新幅度到旋转角度的转换函数。据此提出p-SpheretNorm一参数族角映射，其中p=1对应Proj-SpheretNorm（度量投影retraction），p=2对应Cay-SpheretNorm（Cayley retraction），p→∞对应GeoNorm（指数映射），p→0对应恒等映射。

Result: 在nanoGPT上，三种方法均优于现有轻量级深度连接方案（如残差连接本身）。最佳验证损失出现在有限p值处，表明指数映射（GeoNorm）并非球形残差流的最优retraction，而是谱系的一个端点。

Conclusion: 球形残差流的retraction选择是一个连续谱系，有限p值的Proj/Cay-SpheretNorm因只需代数运算且精确保范而优于两端极限（GeoNorm和恒等映射），这为深度网络的归一化设计提供了新的理论框架实用选择。

Abstract: Residual connections are the de facto mechanism for training deep neural networks stably. Geodesic Normalization (GeoNorm) recasts them on a Riemannian manifold, orthogonalizing each layer output against the current hidden state and applying the resulting update through the Riemannian exponential map. Every hidden state thus keeps a constant $\ell_{2}$-norm, confining the residual stream to a hypersphere. The exponential map, however, is only one member of a broad family of retraction maps. We show that on the hypersphere this entire family collapses to a single scalar design choice. What distinguishes one retraction from another is only how the magnitude of an update is converted into a rotation angle within the plane spanned by the hidden state and the update. This view places Euclidean residual connections and GeoNorm in one framework. Instantiating it with the metric projection retraction and the Cayley retraction yields Proj-SpheretNorm and Cay-SpheretNorm, which are exactly norm-preserving yet require only algebraic operations. Both prove to be members of a one-parameter family of angular retractions, $p$-SpheretNorm, whose rotation angle saturates rather than growing without bound. The two methods above are recovered exactly at $p = 1$ and $p = 2$, while the identity map and GeoNorm arise only as limits at either end. On nanoGPT, all three methods outperform existing lightweight deep connection schemes, and the best validation loss is attained at finite $p$, indicating that the exponential map is not the preferred retraction for spherical residual streams but merely one end of a spectrum.

</details>


### [133] [Learning Molecular Representations from Cellular Phenotypes with Structure Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02688)
*Xuan Lin,Jingyu Sheng,Tengfei Ma,Li Sun,Dapeng Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PhenMol框架，在表型感知的分子表示学习中保留化学空间结构，通过解耦分子与细胞表示为共享和私有组件，实现表型引导对齐的同时不破坏分子邻域组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨模态表示学习方法仅优化跨模态对齐，未考虑化学空间的内在组织，导致分子表示扭曲并丢失结构信息。

Method: PhenMol将分子和细胞表示解耦为共享和私有组件，通过专用分子分支在整合细胞表型信息的同时保留分子邻域组织，实现结构保持的表型引导对齐。

Result: 在约3.04×10⁴个分子-细胞形态对上，PhenMol在270个生物活性任务的分子性质预测、分子-表型检索和临床试验结果预测上均取得提升；ECFP4分析显示其更好地保留了分子邻域并减少了嵌入扭曲。

Conclusion: 结构感知约束在多模态分子表示学习中至关重要，PhenMol为将细胞表型与化学知识整合用于药物发现提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Phenotypic drug discovery enables the discovery of functional relationships between molecular structures and cellular responses. However, existing multimodal representation learning methods often optimize cross-modal alignment without considering the intrinsic organization of chemical space, resulting in distorted molecular representations and loss of structural information. We propose \textbf{PhenMol}, a structure-preserving framework for phenotype-aware molecular representation learning. PhenMol disentangles molecular and cellular representations into shared and private components, enabling phenotype-guided alignment while preserving chemical structures through a dedicated molecular branch. This design integrates cellular phenotype information without disrupting molecular neighborhood organization. Experiments on approximately $3.04 \times 10^{4}$ molecule--cell morphology pairs demonstrate that PhenMol improves molecular property prediction across 270 bioactivity tasks, molecule--phenotype retrieval, and clinical trial outcome prediction. Moreover, ECFP4-based structural analysis shows that PhenMol better preserves molecular neighborhoods and reduces embedding distortion compared with existing multimodal alignment methods. These results highlight the importance of structure-aware constraints in multimodal molecular representation learning and provide an effective approach for integrating cellular phenotypes with chemical knowledge for drug discovery.

</details>


### [134] [GLOBE: Trajectory-Aligned Gradient Matching with Structured SparseOptimization for Coreset Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02690)
*Hetian Liu,Jin Cui,Mengcheng Shi,Yanbin Hu,Xinyue Long,Boran Zhao,Pengju Pen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GLOBE（Gradient Local-Balanced Extraction）是一种面向设备端深度神经网络训练的轨迹对齐核心集选择框架，通过多阶段检查点构建梯度轨迹、多阶矩匹配目标以及Group LASSO与Elastic Net结构化稀疏正则化，将样本选择建模为全局优化的稀疏加权问题，在六个基准和五个评估架构上持续优于现有核心集选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端深度神经网络训练受限于大规模数据集带来的计算与内存成本，核心集选择虽可保留紧凑的真实训练样本子集，但现有基于梯度的方法仅在单一模型快照上计算梯度，并采用贪心或追踪式选择策略，难以捕捉优化的动态演化过程，也无法有效处理强相关样本。

Method: GLOBE将样本选择建模为全局优化的稀疏加权问题：1）为每个样本构建跨多个训练检查点的梯度轨迹以捕捉其在不同优化阶段的影响；2）引入多阶匹配目标，联合对齐梯度轨迹的一阶均值与投影非中心二阶矩；3）结合Group LASSO、Elastic Net正则化与非负预算约束，诱导组级和样本级稀疏性并稳定相关轨迹的权重；4）通过类别均衡的Top-K选择在有限采样预算下维持充分的类别覆盖。

Result: 在六个基准数据集和五个评估架构上的实验表明，GLOBE在下游测试准确率上持续优于现有核心集选择方法，尤其在低保留率场景下优势更为显著。

Conclusion: 动态梯度信息、多阶分布匹配与结构化稀疏性的结合对于数据高效学习是有效的，GLOBE为设备端训练提供了一种性能优越的多阶段轨迹对齐核心集选择框架。

Abstract: On-device training of deep neural networks is fundamentally constrained by the computational and memory costs of large-scale datasets. Coreset selection offers a practical solution by retaining only a compact subset of real training samples. However, existing gradient-based methods commonly rely on gradients computed at a single model snapshot and employ greedy or pursuit-based selection procedures, limiting their ability to capture evolving optimization dynamics and handle strongly correlated samples. We propose GLOBE (Gradient Local-Balanced Extraction), a trajectory-aligned coreset selection framework that formulates sample selection as a globally optimized sparse weighting problem. GLOBE represents each sample by a gradient trajectory constructed across multiple training checkpoints, thereby capturing its influence throughout different stages of optimization. To preserve the training behavior of the full dataset, we introduce a multi-order matching objective that jointly aligns the first-order mean and projected uncentered second-order moments of gradient trajectories. GLOBE further combines Group LASSO, Elastic Net regularization, and nonnegative budget constraints to induce group- and sample-level sparsity while stabilizing the weights of correlated trajectories. Finally, class-balanced Top-K selection maintains adequate category coverage under limited sampling budgets. Experiments across six benchmarks and five evaluation architectures demonstrate that GLOBE consistently outperforms existing coreset selection methods in downstream test accuracy, particularly at low retention ratios. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining dynamic gradient information, multi-order distribution matching, and structured sparsity for data-efficient learning.

</details>


### [135] [Output-Aware Rotation for INT2 KV-Cache Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02691)
*Vincent-Daniel Yun,Woosang Lim,Minsoo Cheong,Sunwoo Lee,Murali Annavaram,Sai Praneeth Karimireddy,Sungjoo Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出OptR，一种面向输出的旋转（rotation）方法，用于低比特KV缓存量化。OptR将经过输出投影$W_O$后的注意力输出误差分解为键引发和值引发的项，并通过完整的INT2量化与注意力路径学习逐头正交校正。OptR还应用注意力等价的键重参数化来减少大的通道偏移而不改变softmax分布。在三个模型和五个推理与编码基准上，OptR一致优于QuaRot和OSCAR，增强长上下文检索，同时保持分页KV缓存格式并引入可忽略的推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于旋转的INT2量化方法在完整的注意力读出之前优化缓存统计量或代理误差，但模型最终受通过注意力和输出投影$W_O$传播的误差影响，这种不匹配导致量化性能次优。

Method: OptR将后$W_O$注意力输出误差分解为键引发和值引发的两项，通过完整的INT2量化和注意力路径学习逐头正交校正；同时采用注意力等价的键重参数化以减小大通道偏移而不改变softmax分布。

Result: 在三个模型和五个推理与编码基准上，OptR一致优于QuaRot和OSCAR，并增强了长上下文检索能力，同时保持分页KV缓存格式且推理开销可忽略。

Conclusion: OptR通过直接针对最终注意力输出误差进行优化，解决了旋转式INT2量化中优化目标与实际传播误差不匹配的问题，在低比特KV缓存量化领域取得了一致的性能提升。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache has become a major memory and bandwidth bottleneck in long-context large language model inference, making ultra-low-bit quantization increasingly important. However, existing rotation-based INT2 methods optimize cache statistics or proxy errors before the complete attention readout, even though the model is ultimately affected by the error propagated through attention and the output projection $W_O$. To address this mismatch, we propose \textit{OptR}, an output-aware rotation method that minimizes post-$W_O$ attention-output error. OptR decomposes the post-$W_O$ attention-output error into key- and value-induced terms and learns per-head orthogonal corrections through the full INT2 quantization and attention path. OptR further applies an attention-equivalent key reparameterization to reduce large channel-wise offsets without changing the softmax distribution. Across three models and five reasoning and coding benchmarks, OptR consistently improves both QuaRot and OSCAR and strengthens long-context retrieval, while preserving the paged KV-cache format with negligible inference overhead.

</details>


### [136] [PatTree: a novel approach for automated creation of multimodal, graph-based patient representations for medical classification tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02692)
*Julia Gehrmann,Lars Quakulinski,Hamza Naseem,Oya Beyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PatTree，一种基于图结构的患者整体表示方法，通过自动化结构化多模态临床数据实现早期数据整合，无需预先标准化输入。在ADNI-1数据集（n=763）上实现三分类任务（AD、MCI、CN）98.5%的平衡准确率和0.987的F1分数，达到现有最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态临床数据能提升AI在医学分类任务中的表现，但真实世界临床数据存在异质性（缺失值、多时间点、多模态、格式与语义不一致），传统数据协调方法资源密集且易出错，限制了可扩展性和可复现性。需要一种无需预标准化的自动化数据整合方法。

Method: PatTree是一种基于图谱的患者整体表示方法。它通过自动化结构化多模态临床数据，将异质临床数据表示在统一知识图谱中，同时保留跨模态和数据源之间的语义关系。PatTree支持早期数据整合，无需依赖预标准化输入，并促进互操作性和机器可解释的数据访问。

Result: 在ADNI-1队列子集（n=763）上，基于PatTree直接进行分类可达到现有最优性能。在三分类任务（区分阿尔茨海默病、轻度认知障碍和认知正常个体）中，在留出测试集上实现98.5%的平衡准确率和0.987的F1分数。

Conclusion: 无假设的自动化多模态医学数据结构化可作为临床AI流水线的可扩展基础，绕过繁琐的数据准备和标准化步骤。

Abstract: Access to holistic, multimodal data improves the performance of Artificial Intelligence (AI) in medical classification tasks compared to utilizing single modalities or data sources. However, the inherent heterogeneity and complexity of clinical real-world data pose significant challenges to structured data analysis and AI application. This heterogeneity includes missing values, multiple time points, diverse modalities, and inconsistent formats and semantics. Data harmonization prior to data integration tackles this challenge but remains resource-intensive and error-prone, limiting the scalability and reproducibility of holistic, AI-driven decision support on clinical real-world data. We therefore propose PatTree, a graph-based, holistic representation of patients that can be derived from real-world clinical data through the automated structuring of multimodal clinical data. PatTree enables early-stage data integration without relying on pre-standardized inputs. While representing heterogeneous clinical data within a unified knowledge graph, PatTree preserves the semantic relationships between data elements across modalities and data sources, facilitating interoperability and machine-interpretable data access. Using a subset of the ADNI-1 cohort (n = 763), we demonstrate that classification of patients is directly feasible on PatTree reaching state-of-the-art classification performance. In the three-class classification task distinguishing Alzheimer's disease, mild cognitive impairment, and cognitively normal individuals, we achieve a balanced accuracy of 98.5% and an F$_1$ score of 0.987 on the held-out test set. Our results show that assumption-free, automated structuring of multimodal medical data can serve as a scalable foundation for clinical AI pipelines bypassing tedious data preparation and standardization.

</details>


### [137] [Measuring Explainer Stability via Attribution Separability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02697)
*Eddie Conti,Álvaro Parafita,Axel Brando*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于分布的框架来量化归因方法（AMs）的稳定性。该框架通过分析排序归因向量的可分离程度，确定特征排名保持可靠的最大索引，并扩展到跨数据集比较不同归因方法的排名稳健性，为评估解释器稳定性提供了补充准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有归因方法因定义中的随机成分会产生不稳定的归因分数，这削弱了其在解释黑箱模型时的可信度。然而，目前缺乏系统性评估归因分数稳定性的框架，需要一种方法来理解排名归因向量的稳定性并比较不同归因方法的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出一个基于分布的框架来捕捉归因分数的稳定性。首先分析排序归因向量的可分离程度，获得特征排名保持可靠的最大索引；然后扩展框架用于跨数据集比较不同归因方法排名的稳健性。通过实验验证了该方法在评估解释器稳定性方面的有效性。

Result: 实验证明该方法能够有效地评估归因方法的稳定性，为评估解释器稳定性提供了一种补充且实用的准则。

Conclusion: 该分布框架为归因方法的稳定性评估提供了新视角，可作为现有评估指标之外的补充标准，有助于在实践中选择更可靠的归因方法。

Abstract: Attribution methods (AMs) assign an importance score to each feature and are widely adopted to explain black-box models. However, most methods can produce variable attribution scores due to stochastic components in their definition. In this paper, we propose a distribution-based framework to capture the stability of attribution scores. In particular, our approach allows to understand the degree of separability in the ranked attribution vector and obtain the largest index for which a feature ranking remains reliable. We further extend this framework to compare AMs based on the robustness of their rankings across a dataset. Through experiments, we demonstrate how to apply our method to evaluate explainer stability. Overall, our approach provides a complementary criterion for evaluating the stability of AMs.

</details>


### [138] [NANQ: Noise-Floor-Aware Mixed-Precision Non-Uniform Quantization for Analog Compute-in-Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02700)
*Yizhe Chen,Wenshuai Yao,Saiya Wang,Yuannuo Feng,Wenbo Qi,Kechao Tang,Ngai Wong,Wenyong Zhou,Wang Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NANQ，一个面向模拟CIM的噪声感知混合精度非均匀量化框架，利用硬件噪声分布自适应分配量化精度与位宽，在eFlash CIM SoC上取得了显著精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟CIM虽能实现高能效神经网络推理，但器件变异和读取噪声会严重损害低位量化模型性能。现有CIM量化方法只关注理想量化误差，忽略硬件噪声底，导致精度分配低效。

Method: NANQ首先从eFlash CIM阵列的实际测量响应中建模与幅值相关的权重噪声，将噪声分布转换为自适应量化密度，在低噪声区域分配更细的精度而避免在噪声主导区域浪费精度；随后通过统一阈值识别每层在硬件噪声下的精度饱和点，分配逐层位宽。

Result: 在eFlash CIM SoC上的实测实验中，2-bit权重量化下NANQ使视觉模型精度平均提升8.05个百分点，语言模型PPL平均降低54.7%；混合精度NANQ仅用3.2-3.8等效比特即可获得大部分额外精度收益。

Conclusion: NANQ验证了在模拟CIM中结合硬件噪声建模的量化策略能显著优于传统理想量化误差最小化方法，实现高效且鲁棒的硬件部署。

Abstract: Analog compute-in-memory (CIM) enables energy-efficient neural network inference, but device variation and read noise can severely degrade low-bit quantized models. Existing CIM-oriented quantization methods mainly minimize ideal quantization error, ignoring the hardware noise floor and thus causing inefficient precision allocation. We propose NANQ, a noise-aware mixed-precision non-uniform quantization framework for analog CIM. NANQ models magnitude-dependent weight noise from measured responses of an eFlash CIM array and converts the noise profile into an adaptive quantization density, assigning finer resolution to low-noise regions while avoiding ineffective precision in noise-dominated regions. It further assigns layer-wise bit-widths by identifying each layer's precision saturation point under hardware noise using a unified threshold. On-chip experiments on an eFlash CIM SoC show that, under 2-bit weight-magnitude quantization, NANQ improves vision-model accuracy by 8.05 percentage points and reduces language-model PPL by 54.7% on average over PowerQuant. Mixed-precision NANQ captures most of the gains obtainable from additional quantization resources with only 3.2-3.8 equivalent bits.

</details>


### [139] [Can Training Logs Make Model Comparisons More Precise?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02705)
*Wei-Jung Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用随机训练模型过程中的训练日志（training logs）来提高模型比较（performance comparison）的精度。通过"arm-specific covariate adjustment"方法，仅利用每个模型自身运行的统计量进行协变量调整，在三个架构和三个数据集上的视觉实验中，基于早期训练日志的简单调整通常能降低比较的不确定性。但主要局限在于协变量选择——广泛搜索最相关的统计量会增加噪声而非减少噪声，因此需要避免较大的选择噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机训练的模型（如深度神经网络）之间进行比较时，需要从多次重复运行中估计性能差异及其不确定性。已有的比较方法通常只使用最终性能指标，而忽略了训练过程中产生的丰富信息（训练日志）。本文想要探究这些训练日志是否可以用来提高模型比较的精度，即减小性能差异估计的不确定性。

Method: 作者提出使用"arm-specific covariate adjustment"方法：每个模型仅用来自其自身运行（runs）的统计量（如早期训练日志中的指标）作为协变量进行调整，而报告的效应量仍然是原始均值差异。具体来说，将训练日志中的协变量引入到性能比较的估计中，以减少方差。实验覆盖三个神经网络架构和三个数据集，测试了基于早期训练日志的简单调整以及广泛搜索日志池中最佳协变量两种策略。

Result: 实验发现：基于早期训练日志的简单协变量调整在多数情况下能有效降低模型比较的不确定性；然而，如果在日志池中广泛搜索与结果最相关的统计量来进行调整，往往引入的选择噪声会超过其带来的方差缩减，即使事后看确实存在有用的统计量。

Conclusion: 训练日志对于提高模型比较的精度是有价值的，但前提是协变量调整策略需要避免大规模选择带来的噪声。该工作为利用训练过程信息进行更精确的模型比较提供了实证基础，并指出了协变量选择在其中的关键挑战。

Abstract: Comparing stochastically trained models requires estimating both a performance difference and its uncertainty from repeated runs. We study whether training logs from those same runs can make such comparisons more precise. Because training-log covariates are produced during training rather than measured before it, we use arm-specific covariate adjustment: each model is adjusted only with statistics from its own runs, and the raw mean difference remains the reported effect. In a vision study spanning three architectures and three datasets, simple adjustments based on early training logs often reduce uncertainty in model comparisons. The main limitation is covariate selection. Broadly searching the log pool for the most correlated statistic often adds more noise than it removes, even when useful statistics exist in hindsight. Training logs therefore appear useful for more precise model comparisons, but only when the adjustment avoids large selection noise.

</details>


### [140] [Designing a Good Virtual Node: Addressable and Cardinality-Preserving Global Memory for Message Passing Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02709)
*Félix Marcoccia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需自注意力、可寻址且保持基数（多重性）的虚拟内存机制，用于缓解消息传递神经网络（MPNN）中虚拟节点的信息瓶颈。通过将全局状态分解为独立可读写的可寻址交叉注意力槽，并引入私有键/值锚点以保留多重性，实现了1-WL等级的区分能力，计算成本为O(nMd)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准虚拟节点管道（节点→VN→节点）将图压缩为单一同质状态并向所有节点广播相同信息，形成有限容量瓶颈。作者旨在构建一种无需自注意力的辅助虚拟内存机制，以缓解该瓶颈。

Method: 基于Two-Radius分析，提出两个关键要求：（1）全局内存应分解为独立可写可读的状态，通过可寻址交叉注意力槽实现；（2）可寻址性本身无法保持多重性，因为softmax注意力对均匀复制具有不变性。通过将每个槽查询作为私有键/值锚点插入，恢复被丢弃的归一化质量，在有界颜色域上得到单射多重集表示，可实现1-WL细化。

Result: 在多重性感知的Two-Radius、子图计数和约束链路集预测实验中，该可寻址且保持基数（cardinality-preserving）的虚拟内存在O(nMd)算术成本下取得良好效果，验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过可寻址交叉注意力槽与私有锚点的组合，可以构建无需自注意力的、可扩展的虚拟内存机制，在保持计算效率的同时增强MPNN的表达能力与多重性感知能力。

Abstract: Virtual nodes give message-passing neural networks a simple global communication route, but the standard node--VN--node pipeline compresses the graph into one homogeneous state and broadcasts it identically to every node. Building on the Two-Radius analysis of Mishayev et al., we ask how auxiliary virtual memory can relieve this finite-capacity bottleneck without self-attention. We identify two requirements. First, the global memory should be factorized into independently writable and readable states: this can be achieved using addressable cross-attention slots. Second, addressability alone does not preserve multiplicity, because softmax attention is invariant to uniform replication. Inserting each slot query as a private key/value anchor recovers the discarded normalization mass and yields, on bounded color domains, an injective multiset representation able to implement a 1-WL refinement. Experiments on multiplicity-aware Two-Radius, motif counting, and constrained link-set prediction support this addressable and cardinality-preserving virtual memory at (O(nMd)) arithmetic cost.

</details>


### [141] [Temporal Leakage in LLM Backtesting: Measurement, Validation, and Adjusted Scores](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02985)
*Zeyu Zhang,Bradly C. Stadie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出LLM回测中标准的"训练截止前/后分数对比"污染检测方法本质上是无效的，因为模型对截止时间附近的知识合法地更多，导致"近因性"与"记忆泄漏"无法通过被动回测区分。作者提出两种外部信息来源（已知截止日期识别边界泄漏、匹配干净对照组进行全局识别并给出泄漏调整分数），并通过孪生模型注入实验验证估计器准确性，以及在前沿模型上部署检测到一个截止局部化信号并洗清五个模型的"表面优势"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的污染检测方法——比较训练截止前后分数——被证明毫无信息量。四大旗舰模型在不可记忆的问题上（即截止后解决的问题）也未能通过该测试，原因在于结构性的"近因性"效应：模型对接近其截止时间的数据具有合法优势，这模仿了泄漏效应。作者的核心动机是证明被动回测无法区分近因性与真正技能，因此需要外部信息来实现测量而非仅仅检测。

Method: 作者采用理论证明加实证验证的方法：(1) 理论上证明没有任何被动回测方法能区分"近因性"和"泄漏"；(2) 提出两种外部信息形式：已知截止日期用于识别边界泄漏，匹配的干净对照组用于全局识别并计算泄漏调整分数；(3) 推导泄漏的分布位置——集中在令公众惊讶且训练中覆盖良好的结果上，且部分记忆会被不成比例地奖励；(4) 通过孪生模型（twin models）注入已知泄漏量验证估计器能恢复注入剂量并在干净问题上返回零值；(5) 在前沿模型上部署检测。

Result: 实证结果表明：标准截止对比测试在四个旗舰模型上失败（即使是它们不可能记忆的问题）；注入实验中，估计器能准确恢复注入剂量并对干净问题返回零；部署到前沿模型后，检测到一种截止局部化的泄漏签名；在审计的功率下限（power floor）处，证明五个模型的表面优势完全可归因于近因性而非泄漏。

Conclusion: 回测不应被废弃，而是需要一个可辩护的参考基准（即外部信息来源）。仅靠被动回测无法测量泄漏；测量需要来自回测之外的信息。核心结论是：近因性模仿泄漏，而区分二者需要已知截止日期或匹配对照组等外部参照。

Abstract: The standard check for contamination in LLM backtests is simple: compare scores before and after the training cutoff. We show this check is uninformative. Four flagship models fail it on questions they cannot have memorized: every scored question resolved after their cutoffs. The reason is structural. Models legitimately know more about times near their cutoff, so recency mimics leakage, and we prove no passive backtest can separate the two from genuine skill. Measurement, not just detection, requires information from outside the backtest. We supply it in two forms. A known cutoff identifies leakage at the boundary; a matched clean control identifies it globally and yields a leakage-adjusted score. We also derive where leakage hides: it concentrates on outcomes that surprised the crowd and were well covered in training, and partial memorization is disproportionately rewarded. We validate the estimators against ground truth by planting leakage in twin models, where they recover the injected dose and return null on clean questions. Deployed on frontier models, they detect one cutoff-localized signature and, at the audit's power floor, clear five models whose apparent advantages were recency alone. Backtests need not be discarded; they need one defensible reference.

</details>


### [142] [Neural Networks with Local Converging Inputs for Efficient Options Pricing Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02778)
*Harris Cobb,Wenbo Hao,Yingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的神经局部收敛输入（NNLCI）方法，通过神经网络对粗网格和细网格数值解进行局部校正，提高多资产期权定价数值方法的效率。该方法在Black-Scholes方程下的现金或零期权（1-3维）和Heston随机波动模型下的单资产障碍看涨期权上进行了验证，将细网格数值解的均方根误差（RMSE）降低了约4-12倍，即使仅使用少量高保真训练数据也能实现，显著降低了高维问题的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多资产期权定价的高维数值方法（如PDE求解）计算成本高昂，在实时交易和风险管理中效率不足。现有数值方法需要在精度和速度之间权衡，粗网格效率高但精度低，细网格精度高但计算量大。因此需要一种方法能够以较低的计算成本获得高精度的定价结果。

Method: 提出NNLCI（Neural Networks with Local Converging Inputs）方法：使用神经网络对粗网格和细网格的数值解进行局部校正，采用最简洁的输入格式以提高效率和便利性。仅需要最少数量的高保真训练数据，网络学习粗网格解与细网格解之间的误差校正关系，从而在测试时以接近粗网格的计算成本获得接近细网格的精度。

Result: 在以下场景中验证了NNLCI的有效性：（1）Black-Scholes方程下1维、2维和3维的现金或零期权；（2）Heston随机波动模型下单资产下降敲出障碍看涨期权（定价PDE在S和v两个维度）。在所有情况下，即使仅使用少量参数组合子集进行训练，NNLCI也能将细网格数值解的RMSE降低约4-12倍，展现了低训练成本和强泛化能力。

Conclusion: NNLCI显著降低了高维期权定价问题的计算需求，在实时期权交易和风险管理中具有广阔应用前景。低训练成本与强泛化能力的结合使其成为加速现有数值方法的有效工具。

Abstract: We present a novel application of Neural Networks with Local Converging Inputs (NNLCI) to improve the efficiency of existing numerical methods for pricing multi-asset options. The most concise input format for NNLCI has been introduced, offering substantial convenience and efficiency. NNLCI uses a neural network to locally correct solutions from a coarse mesh and a refined mesh (relative to the coarse one), requiring only a minimal amount of high-fidelity training data. We demonstrate this approach on cash-or-nothing options under the Black-Scholes equation in one, two, and three spatial dimensions, and on single-asset down-and-out barrier call options under the Heston stochastic-volatility model (whose pricing PDE is two-dimensional in the spot price $S$ and the instantaneous variance $v$). In each case, NNLCI reduces the root-mean-square error (RMSE) of the refined-mesh numerical solution by a factor of approximately 4-12 on test sets, even when the neural network is trained on only a small subset of parameter combinations. These results demonstrate that NNLCI significantly reduces computational requirements for high-dimensional problems in real-time options trading and risk management, offering low training costs and strong generalization ability.

</details>


### [143] [Beyond the Gegenbauer Paradigm: q-Orthogonal Kernels for Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03482)
*Álvaro Sánchez-Paniagua Ríos,Juan P. Llerena,Alberto Lastra,Nuria Torrado,Edmundo J. Huertas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于离散q-Hermite I多项式的新型核函数家族，扩展了正交多项式核范式。该核在Mercer定理下具有有效性，其有界性特性天然避免了核值的湮灭和爆炸效应。在20个基准数据集上的实验表明，该核相比经典核和其他正交多项式核具有竞争力，且在数值稳定性和计算简洁性方面具有优势，并可能推广至量子计算领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支持向量机的性能严重依赖于核函数的选择。经典的RBF核虽然广泛使用，但缺乏结构化的先验知识嵌入能力；现有的正交多项式核虽具有数学可解释性，但仍有改进空间。作者希望通过引入q-正交多项式（具体为离散q-Hermite I多项式）来构造新的核函数家族，以同时获得数学严谨性、数值稳定性和实际性能优势。

Method: 作者定义了q-Hermite核，基于离散q-Hermite I多项式构建核函数，并通过变形参数q对经典Hermite多项式进行推广。通过Mercer定理证明了核的有效性，利用核的固有有界性避免显式缩放机制的依赖。在20个基准数据集上与经典核及其他正交多项式核进行对比实验，并提供了完整的开源实现。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的q-Hermite核在20个基准数据集上取得了与经典核和其他正交多项式核相当或更优的性能，并在数值稳定性和计算简洁性方面表现突出。核的固有有界性有效预防了湮灭和爆炸问题。

Conclusion: q-正交多项式是核函数设计的富有前景的方向，它将数学优雅性与实用机器学习应用相结合。该核函数家族不仅在当前实验中表现良好，还为未来的量子计算范式提供了概念和算法层面的扩展资源。

Abstract: The performance of Support Vector Machines (SVMs) critically depends on the kernel function choice, which enables implicit mapping of data into high-dimensional feature spaces. While classical kernels like Radial Basis Function (RBF) remain popular, orthogonal polynomial kernels offer mathematically interpretable alternatives that can incorporate structured prior knowledge. This work extends the orthogonal polynomial kernel paradigm by introducing a novel family based on discrete $q$-Hermite I polynomials, a class of $q$-orthogonal polynomials that generalize classical Hermite polynomials through a deformation parameter $q$. We formally define the q-Hermite kernel and establish its validity under Mercer's theorem. The kernel's inherent boundedness properties naturally prevent annihilation and explosion effects without requiring explicit scaling mechanisms. Extensive experiments across 20 benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed kernel achieves competitive performance compared to both classical kernels and other orthogonal polynomial kernels, while offering advantages in numerical stability and computational simplicity. Our results confirm that $q$-orthogonal polynomials constitute a promising direction for kernel design, bridging mathematical elegance with practical machine learning applications, that provides conceptual and algorithmic resources that may be further extended to emerging quantum computing paradigms. To facilitate full reproducibility, we provide the complete implementation and experimental pipeline in an open-access GitHub repository at https://github.com/Kokechacho/SVMs-QSVMs.

</details>


### [144] [Evaluation Blindness: How Silent Measurement Failures Corrupt AI Systems from Training to Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02786)
*Priyanka Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"评估盲区"（evaluation blindness）概念，指测量函数在系统实际故障时仍输出与健康状态无法区分的读数且无辅助信号提示。论文统一了训练期和部署期两个生命周期阶段的检测盲区问题，通过形式化的可检测性谓词、四个训练期案例研究（包括真实存在bug的TRL PR #6594）、基于50个真实事故的六类故障分类体系（53%可验证的公共故障是静默的），以及将可接受故障率与用例风险等级关联的故障预算框架，论证了测量基础设施是整个AI生命周期中的正确性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统可能静默失败，故障在训练循环、评估管道和生产监控栈中传播，直到下游危害显现才被察觉。现有文献将训练期和部署期的测量盲区问题分开处理，缺乏统一的理论框架来理解和检测这类静默故障。

Method: 论文定义了评估盲度的形式化概念（测量函数M对故障类F表现出评估盲度），提出统一的可检测性谓词覆盖训练和部署两阶段；通过四个训练期案例（包括TRL PR #6594中的真实渐变损坏bug）进行实证分析；构建六类生产故障分类体系并基于50个真实事故（来自法庭文件和监管文件）进行验证；提出故障预算框架将可接受故障率与用例风险等级关联。

Result: 基于50个真实事故的验证发现53%可验证的公共故障是静默的；发现一个完全静默的Operational类别（按结构性定义100%静默）；识别出包括奖励模型被攻破、重要性采样校正静默算错、基准污染膨胀微调评估等具体故障模式。

Conclusion: 测量基础设施的正确性应贯穿AI全生命周期而非仅限于评估阶段，评估盲区问题需要在训练和部署两个阶段统一对待，并应通过故障预算机制将可接受失败率与用例风险等级绑定。

Abstract: AI systems can fail silently. The failure propagates through training loops, evaluation pipelines, and production monitoring stacks until downstream harm makes it visible. This paper introduces evaluation blindness: a measurement function M exhibits evaluation blindness with respect to failure class F when it produces readings indistinguishable from a healthy state while the system is actually failing, with no auxiliary signal flagging the gap.
  The problem surfaces at two lifecycle stages the literature has treated separately. At training time, reward models are gamed, importance-sampling corrections are silently miscalculated, and benchmark contamination inflates fine-tuning evaluations, all while loss curves look healthy and gradient updates proceed normally. At deployment time, monitoring fails to catch six classes of production failure, including an Operational category that is 100% silent by structural definition.
  We provide a formal detectability predicate unifying both stages. Four training-time case studies trace concrete breakdowns, including a real implementation bug in TRL PR #6594 where gradients are corrupted as loss decreases normally. A six-class taxonomy validated against 50 real-world incidents from court documents and regulatory filings finds that 53% of verifiable public failures were silent. A failure budget framework ties acceptable failure rates to use-case risk class.
  The implication is direct: measurement infrastructure is a correctness concern across the full AI lifecycle, not just at evaluation time. Data, code, and taxonomy schema are at https://github.com/priyanka25aug/llm-failure-taxonomy.

</details>


### [145] [Topological Simplification in Predictive Coding Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02816)
*Adam Shaw,Jiayu Li,Michael Sperling,Michael Kim,Alvin Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用逐层持久同调分析，定量研究了预测编码网络（PCN）中学习表示的拓扑结构。研究发现：较小PCN在更早层折叠连通分量；简化发生的深度与重建误差呈负相关；PCN比同宽多层感知机（MLP）平均晚3.6层折叠连通分量。结果表明持久同调为PCN的压缩-重建权衡提供了有价值的定量视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络的表示学习机制是深度学习理论的核心问题之一。预测编码网络作为一种神经启发式的双向架构，其学习表示的拓扑结构尚未被系统研究。传统分析手段难以捕捉网络层间表示几何的变化，因此需要引入拓扑数据分析（特别是持久同调）来量化观察PCN在不同层之间如何压缩信息。

Method: 采用逐层持久同调分析（layer-wise persistent homology）对PCN的隐藏层激活进行拓扑特征提取。实验设置在合成分类数据集（测试准确率≥99.9%）和MNIST（≥95%）上训练高性能PCN，比较不同架构和激活函数下拓扑特征（如连通分量数）随层的变化。使用Spearman相关系数度量模型大小与拓扑折叠的关联，并用bootstrap方法进行架构间的种子级对比。

Result: （1）较小PCN在更早层折叠连通分量，Spearman ρ在[0.72, 0.79]区间；（2）简化深度与重建误差呈强负相关（ρ=-0.58），即更晚简化的架构重建效果更好；（3）PCN比匹配的MLP平均晚3.6层折叠连通分量，差异具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 持久同调是分析PCN压缩-重建权衡的有效定量工具。模型容量和预测编码推理的循环双向动力学共同决定了这种权衡在网络各层何时被解决，为理解神经启发式架构的信息处理机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study the topology of learned representations in predictive coding networks (PCNs), a neuro-inspired bidirectional architecture, using a quantitative layer-wise persistent homology analysis. We train well-performing PCNs on a synthetic classification dataset ($\geq 99.9\%$ test accuracy) and on MNIST ($\geq 95\%$ test accuracy), and measure how topological features change across layers for different architectures and activation functions. We find that smaller PCNs collapse connected components across layers earlier than larger models (Spearman $\unicode{x1D70C} \in [0.72, 0.79]$ across activations), with model size measured as the sum of hidden-layer widths. We also observe a strong negative correlation ($\unicode{x1D70C} = -0.58$) between the depth at which simplification occurs and reconstruction error; i.e., architectures that simplify later reconstruct better. Finally, a seed-level bootstrap comparison across architectures and activations shows that PCNs consistently collapse connected components later than matched MLPs, with an average difference of $3.6$ layers. These results suggest that persistent homology offers a useful quantitative lens on the compression--reconstruction tradeoff in PCNs, and that both model capacity and the recurrent, bidirectional dynamics of predictive coding inference shape when this tradeoff is resolved across layers.

</details>


### [146] [Trajectory inference via Acceleration Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03916)
*Bartolo Dazzini,Giovanni Conforti,Alain Durmus,Aram-Alexandre Pooladian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为加速度匹配（AM）的新算法，通过将插值问题提升到相空间并回归到显式条件加速度场，生成与给定边缘分布一致的随机平滑轨迹。该算法仅需位置数据，避免训练过程中的轨迹模拟和昂贵的预处理，在多个基准问题上与现有算法表现相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹推断是许多科学领域中的基本问题，给定离散时间点的未配对观测快照，目标是生成最能拟合和插值数据的平滑轨迹。现有算法存在计算挑战：要么依赖预处理子程序来强制平滑性，要么依赖基于模拟的训练目标，这两种方式都可能导致高昂的计算成本。

Method: 提出加速度匹配算法（Acceleration Matching, AM），核心思想是将原始插值问题提升到相空间（phase space），然后回归到显式条件加速度场，该场诱导产生与给定边缘分布一致的随机平滑轨迹。训练过程仅需位置数据，无需在训练期间进行轨迹模拟，也无需昂贵的预处理步骤。

Result: 在现有文献中的多个基准问题上提供了充分的数值证据，表明AM算法与现有算法相比具有竞争力或表现更优。

Conclusion: AM算法通过避免预处理和模拟式训练目标带来的计算瓶颈，提供了一种更高效且有效的轨迹推断方法，在多个基准测试中展现出与现有方法相当或更好的性能。

Abstract: Trajectory inference is a fundamental problem in many scientific domains: given a collection of unpaired snapshots of observations at discrete time points, the goal is to generate smooth trajectories that best resemble and interpolate the data. Existing algorithms exhibit computational challenges: they either rely on preprocessing subroutines to enforce smoothness or on simulation-based training objectives, both of which can be expensive. In order to overcome these limitations, we propose a new algorithm called Acceleration Matching (\texttt{AM}). Our approach consists of lifting the original interpolation problem to phase space and then regressing onto an explicit conditional acceleration field that induces random, smooth trajectories that agree with the prescribed marginals. Importantly, our resulting training algorithm only requires positional data, avoids trajectory simulation during training, and is devoid of expensive preprocessing. We provide ample numerical evidence suggesting that \texttt{AM} is competitive with or superior to existing algorithms on several benchmark problems from the existing literature.

</details>


### [147] [Wiring Beats Blending: What Transfers Between Transformer Sizes -- and What Doesn't](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02829)
*Ravi Satya Durga Prasad Yenugula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了Pythia系列中1.4B→410M的模型压缩问题，发现大模型可以转化为小模型，价值在于初始化而非参数投影；通过最小二乘补偿和方差保持缩放两个独立杠杆，在低预算下能显著超越从头训练和子克隆方法，但预算增大后趋同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型家族需要从头训练每个尺寸的模型，计算成本高昂。作者探索能否将预训练大模型直接转换为更小的变体，以避免重复训练，并试图从表征对齐、参数映射和初始化价值三个维度深入理解这种转换的机制与边界。

Method: 使用Pythia家族1.4B→410M规模进行端到端分析：首先用岭回归量化表征对齐（R²=0.84）和参数对齐；然后检验稠密权重投影的功能破坏性，排除组件组装伪影；通过洗牌控制检验线性算子后的残差是否为统计噪声；最后将转换分解为最小二乘补偿（优化零样本性能）和方差保持缩放（优化动态端点）两个独立杠杆，在匹配预算的持续预训练中对比宽度缩减和深度缩减两种子克隆变体。

Result: 表征对齐强（R²=0.84）而参数对齐弱；稠密投影因破坏rotary、逐头、GELU和LayerNorm结构而功能上有害；最佳线性算子后残差与噪声无统计差异，说明转换价值在初始化。30M token预算下，补偿杠杆在宽度缩减对（84.0±1.8 vs 89.7±3.7）和深度缩减对（109.3 vs 117.9）均3/3种子胜出；33倍更大预算时二者趋同（40.0 vs 40.0），且均远超从头训练（低预算最高可达18倍优势）。在6.9B→1.4B尺度上双杠杆叠加过校正，归因于大宽度下补偿求解的病态条件。

Conclusion: 预训练模型家族转换的核心价值在于初始化而非参数空间投影；最小二乘补偿是token高效、低预算场景下的最优策略而非普适方案；未来工作应聚焦维度感知正则化以解决大规模下的病态条件问题。

Abstract: Model families train every size from scratch. Can a pretrained large model be converted into a smaller sibling? We characterize the 1.4B->410M conversion in the Pythia family end-to-end: (i) representations align strongly across sizes (ridge R^2=0.84) while parameters align weakly; (ii) dense weight projection is functionally destructive -- provably not an assembly artifact -- because basis mixing breaks rotary, per-head, GELU, and LayerNorm structure; (iii) after the best-fit linear operator, weight residuals are statistically indistinguishable from noise under shuffle controls; (iv) conversion value therefore lives in initialization. In matched-budget continued pre-training we decompose conversion into two independent levers -- least-squares compensation (function: best zero-shot) and variance-preserving rescale (dynamics: best endpoints). Compensation is a token-efficient, low-budget win rather than a universal one: at 30M tokens it beats the strongest subcloning variant on both a width-reduced pair (84.0 +/- 1.8 vs. 89.7 +/- 3.7, 3/3 seeds) and a held-out depth-reduced pair (109.3 vs. 117.9, 3/3 seeds), reaching a given quality with fewer tokens; at a 33x larger budget the two converge to parity (40.0 vs. 40.0), both far ahead of from-scratch, which transfer initialization always beats -- by up to 18x at low budget, the margin narrowing at convergence and at the largest scale. We further map the method's boundary: at ~5x the donor scale (6.9B->1.4B) stacking both levers over-corrects, which we trace to ill-conditioning of the compensation solve at large width, pointing to dimension-aware regularization as the fix. Code, checkpoints, and the frozen evaluation corpus are released.

</details>


### [148] [NOMADD: Numerical Optimization of Models Adapting to Data Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02845)
*Swapn Shah,Keith Burghardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种后处理式的概念漂移缓解方法，通过分别训练基模型、锚定参数变化量、低秩分解并外推潜在因子，在18个数据集的Drift-Resilient TabPFN基准上提升所有基模型性能，训练仅需数秒，远超需1300 GPU小时预训练的Drift-Resilient TabPFN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格模型在特征分布或特征与目标关系随时间变化时性能会退化（数据漂移与概念漂移）。实时缓解漂移困难，因为带标签数据可能不可立即获得，或重训练模型不切实际。现有缓解工具多为特定神经网络架构定制，缺乏通用且轻量的方案。

Method: 该算法将基模型分别在每个带标签训练时段单独拟合，测量其参数相对一个跨越所有时段的锚定模型的演化方式，用低秩因子化压缩参数变化，然后对每个潜在因子用阻尼、正则化的预测方法向前外推。该方法为事后（post-hoc）方法，适用于从树模型到神经网络再到表格基础模型的多种模型。

Result: 在Drift-Resilient TabPFN基准的18个数据集上，按该基准自身的协议和指标评估，外推方法提升了其应用的每个基模型家族的性能，并实现了与state-of-the-art的Drift-Resilient TabPFN竞争力相当的性能，但训练仅需数秒。相比之下，Drift-Resilient TabPFN需要在约1300个GPU小时上对数百万个合成数据集进行预训练，且推理速度慢数个数量级。

Conclusion: 该后处理方法在概念漂移缓解上提供了高效且模型无关的替代方案，尤其适用于在精度、推理时限或模型大小约束下需在不同模型间做选择的场景。论文还探讨了将该工具扩展到其他模态的前景与挑战。

Abstract: Tabular model performance degrades when feature distributions change over time or the relationship between features and outcome variables change over time, known as data drift and concept drift, respectively. These issues are challenging to mitigate in real time because labeled data may not be immediately available, or re-training a model could be impractical. While tools exist to reduce drift, they are typically bespoke to neural network architectures and adapt how models are trained. In this paper, we offer an alternative post-hoc method to reduce concept drift, which is applicable to a variety of models, from trees to neural networks to tabular foundation models. This new tool is especially useful when constraints, such as high model accuracy, bounded inference time, or model size requires users to choose between different models for their specific use-cases. Our algorithm fits the base model separately on each labeled training period, measures how its parameters evolve against a single anchor model pooled over all of those periods, compresses those changes with a low-rank factorization, and extrapolates each latent factor forward with a damped, regularized forecast. On the 18-dataset Drift-Resilient TabPFN benchmark, evaluated under that benchmark's own protocol and metric, the extrapolation improves every base family it is applied to, and achieves performance competitive with the state-of-the-art Drift-Resilient TabPFN with seconds of training. In contrast, Drift-Resilient TabPFN requires pre-training on millions of synthetic datasets over approximately 1,300 GPU-hours, and is orders of magnitude slower in inference (depending on the model). In the discussion, we explore the promise and challenges of extending this tool to other modalities.

</details>


### [149] [Adaptive Sampling for Automated Post-Disaster Rapid Damage Assessment via Level-Set Cost-Aware Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02868)
*Boyang Xu,Mostafa Reisi Gahrooei,Mohammad Ilbeigi,Hao Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合成本感知贝叶斯优化与水平集估计的框架，用于引导自主数据采集器（如无人机）在灾后动态环境中高效评估建筑损伤。该方法通过动态更新不同地理区域的损伤估计，在最小化运行成本的同时系统性地降低不确定性，并在合成数据与高保真R2D仿真数据上均验证了其准确性和时效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统灾后损伤评估方法依赖静态、劳动密集的数据采集策略，成本高昂且难以适应灾后动态变化的环境，亟需一种快速、可靠且经济的自主评估方法。

Method: 提出一种成本感知的贝叶斯优化框架，结合水平集估计（level-set estimation）技术，持续引导自主数据采集器（如无人机）飞向信息量最大的区域；通过动态更新各地理区域的损伤估计，在降低预测不确定性的同时最小化运行成本。

Result: 在受控的合成玩具研究中，代理能够高效追踪损伤边界、恢复潜在损伤地图并快速降低预测不确定性；在高保真R2D软件生成的灾害数据上，算法提供了准确且及时的损伤估计。

Conclusion: 该框架能够为应急响应提供准确、及时的损伤评估，支持信息充分且快速的灾害响应决策。

Abstract: Natural disasters frequently inflict severe damage to the built environment, which demands a rapid, reliable, and cost-effective damage assessment for emergency response. However, traditional methods for post-disaster damage assessment often rely on static, labor-intensive data collection strategies that can be prohibitively expensive and struggle to adapt to dynamic post-disaster conditions. In this study, we propose a cost-aware Bayesian optimization framework combined with level-set estimation that continuously guides autonomous data collectors, e.g., an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV), toward the most informative regions. By dynamically updating damage estimates across different geographic zones, our approach systematically reduces uncertainty while minimizing operational costs. The proposed framework is first validated using a controlled synthetic toy study, demonstrating the agent's ability to efficiently trace damage boundaries, recover the underlying damage map, and rapidly reduce predictive uncertainty. Furthermore, the approach is evaluated using high-fidelity disaster data generated by the Regional Resilience Determination (R2D) software. The results of the algorithm provide accurate and timely damage estimates that support informative and fast emergency response.

</details>


### [150] [Contrast-invariant deep ptychography neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02869)
*Albert Vong,Steven Henke,Oliver Hoidn,Hanna Ruth,Junjing Deng,Apurva Mehta,David Shapiro,Alexander Hexemer,Nicholas Schwarz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对Ptychography神经网络在分布外泛化时的尺度不一致问题，提出分解策略解耦物体纹理与测量尺度，结合实/虚部表示预测和合成物体采样策略，跨5个实验数据集实现最高5倍的傅里叶误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ptychography神经网络在分布外泛化时存在尺度不一致问题，限制了其实际应用中的可靠性。现有方法无法在变化的照明条件下保持测量一致的重建结果，需要解决这一规模化失配问题以提升真实世界可用性。

Method: 采用因子分解策略将学习的物体纹理与测量尺度解耦，使单个训练好的网络能够适应不同照明条件；将学习目标从传统的幅值/相位表示改为实部/虚部（实数和虚数单位）表示；引入合成物体采样策略以减少合成训练数据与实验目标之间的相位分布不匹配。

Result: 相比之前的PtychoPINN-torch基线，在跨越多个光束线和设施的5个实验数据集上，傅里叶误差最高降低5倍。

Conclusion: 通过解耦纹理与测量尺度、改用实/虚部表示并优化合成数据采样策略，有效解决了Ptychography神经网络的尺度不一致问题，显著提升了跨设施、跨实验条件的泛化能力。

Abstract: Ptychography neural networks suffer from scaling inconsistencies when generalizing out of distribution, limiting their real world viability. We address this scaling mismatch using a factorization strategy which decouples the learned object texture from measurement scaling, enabling a single trained network to produce measurement-consistent reconstructions across varying illumination conditions. This requires predicting the learned object in real and imaginary units instead of the canonical amplitude and phase representation. We additionally introduce a synthetic object sampling strategy that minimizes phase distribution mismatch between synthetic training data and experimental targets. These improvements yield up to a 5x reduction in Fourier error over the previous PtychoPINN-torch baseline across 5 experimental datasets spanning multiple beamlines and facilities.

</details>


### [151] [Maglev: Sliding Recurrent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02870)
*Bo Liu,Qiang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种具有固定大小记忆的循环Transformer架构，通过将带全注意力的预填充器Q与使用滑窗注意力和循环K/V注入的解码器P进行耦合训练，并引入记忆一致性损失对齐两者的记忆，从而在保持训练可并行化的同时推广滑窗注意力，实现高效的序列建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统滑窗注意力虽然高效但受限于局部上下文窗口，而全注意力模型计算成本过高。现有循环Transformer（如潜伏循环Transformer）在记忆表达上存在局限，且训练并行性受限。作者希望通过一种新的架构设计，既保持训练可并行化，又能超越固定窗口的限制，获得更强的上下文建模能力。

Method: 提出一种由两个耦合模型组成的循环Transformer架构：1) 预填充器Q，利用全注意力（实践中交替使用全注意力和滑窗注意力）生成记忆目标m'_t；2) 解码器P，仅使用滑窗注意力和循环K/V注入生成解码器记忆m_t用于下一token预测。训练时通过记忆一致性损失将m_t与m'_t对齐，推理时仅使用解码器P。此外还探索了P和Q之间共享参数以减少参数开销。

Result: 实验表明，该架构相比滑窗注意力和潜伏循环Transformer基线，在验证损失和下游预训练基准上均有改进。共享P和Q参数可以在减少参数存储的同时保留大部分性能增益。

Conclusion: 本文提出的固定大小记忆循环Transformer有效地结合了全注意力的表达力与循环架构的推理效率，通过知识蒸馏式的记忆对齐机制将强大的预填充器能力迁移到轻量解码器中，为长序列建模提供了一种兼具训练效率和推理性能的新方案。

Abstract: We introduce \ours{}, a recurrent Transformer architecture with fixed-size memory that generalizes sliding-window attention while remaining parallelizable during training. \ours{} consists of two coupled models: a prefiller $Q$, which leverages full attention\footnote{In practice, we use interleaved full and sliding-window attention for $Q$, as this yields stronger performance. The essential requirement is that $Q$ be more expressive than $P$, with access to the full history.} to produce memory targets $m'_t$, and a decoder $P$, which uses only sliding-window attention and recurrent K/V injection to produce decoder memories $m_t$ for next-token prediction. We train \ours{} with a memory consistency loss that aligns $m_t$ with $m'_t$, allowing inference to use $P$ alone. Empirically, \ours{} improves validation loss and downstream pretraining benchmarks over sliding-window and latent recurrent transformer baselines. Moreover, sharing parameters between $P$ and $Q$ reduces parameter memory while preserving most of the gains.

</details>


### [152] [GoT-CD: Graph-of-Thoughts Causal Discovery and the Fragility of Post-hoc Path-Specific Fairness Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02877)
*Nitish Nagesh,Elahe Khatibi,Thomas Dean Hughes,Mahdi Bagheri,Pratik Gajane,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GoT-CD方法，将Graph-of-Thoughts推理应用于因果发现，以完整候选边集为推理单元，在多个基准上生成结构上具竞争力的DAG，并揭示结构保真度不能保证路径特定公平审计的忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 路径特定的反事实公平性度量依赖于给定的因果图，但因果发现步骤引入的误差会继承到公平审计中。现有发现方法仅按聚合结构指标评分，不评估特定路径是否在发现中幸存，也不考察路径缺失时审计报告会如何失真。

Method: GoT-CD方法：以完整候选边集作为推理单元，并行生成多个图，通过确定性有效性函数评分，并在硬联合约束下合并（禁止发明边），通过贪心投影强制执行DAG结构。

Result: 在五个基准上均返回有效DAG，在Asia、Alzheimer's和COVID-Respiratory数据集上取得LLM方法中最佳的DAG有效F1分数。在含已知不公平路径的Alzheimer's基准上，八个发现图中有五个未恢复从敏感属性到结果变量的路径，导致报告总体效应为零而介导效应仍存在。

Conclusion: 结构保真度不足以保证公平审计的忠实性；在结构发现之外还需结合下游路径特定的公平性分析，以确保因果发现结果能支持可靠的公平性审计。

Abstract: Causal discovery recovers directed structure from observational data and is increasingly used in clinical settings to support mechanism reasoning and fairness audits of predictive models. Path-specific counterfactual fairness asks whether a protected attribute influences an outcome through illegitimate pathways, but these estimands are defined relative to a supplied causal graph and therefore inherit whatever errors the discovery step introduces. Discovery methods are routinely scored on aggregate structural metrics that weight all edges equally, and no established evaluation asks whether the specific pathway an audit depends on survives discovery---or what the audit reports when that pathway is missing. Here we show that full-graph Graph-of-Thoughts reasoning yields acyclic discovered graphs that are structurally competitive with large language model (LLM) baselines, yet that structural fidelity alone does not guarantee fairness-faithful audits. We introduce GoT-CD, in which the reasoning unit is a complete candidate edge set: multiple graphs are generated in parallel, scored by a deterministic validity function, and merged under a hard union constraint that forbids invented edges, with greedy projection enforcing a DAG before commitment. GoT-CD returns a valid DAG on all five reported benchmarks and achieves the best DAG-valid F1 score among LLM methods on Asia, Alzheimer's, and COVID-Respiratory datasets. On an Alzheimer's benchmark with known unfair path, a post-hoc path-specific audit shows that five of eight discovered graphs recover no path from the sensitive attribute to the outcome and therefore report a null overall effect while mediated effects persist, necessitating downstream path-specific fairness analysis along with structural discovery.

</details>


### [153] [Population-Robust Feature Selection via Generalized Welfare Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02887)
*Ruiqi Lyu,Alistair Turcan,Bryan Wilder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PopFS，一种学习跨人群共享且可部署的特征选择方法，在允许各人群训练自己模型的同时，通过可调福利目标在总体预测性能与最弱势人群保护之间取得平衡，并在多个公共健康数据集及COVID-19预测任务上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署中，同一特征集（如问卷、测试面板或传感器）需服务于多个异质人群，但传统特征选择方法仅针对单一大规模人群优化，而现有鲁棒方法倾向于为所有人群学习一个共享模型，忽视了各人群可独立训练模型的灵活性。

Method: PopFS首先利用多任务稀疏学习缩减候选特征池，然后通过排序有希望的添加和交换操作直接在硬特征集上搜索，仅对候选短列表进行完整重拟合。其核心是可调福利目标，允许实践者权衡总体预测收益与对最不受益人群的更强保护。

Result: 在来自五个表格和公共健康数据集的六个预测任务的八个群体划分上，PopFS持续实现强平均和最差群体性能，并可扩展到数千个候选特征。43州COVID-19现在预测研究表明，改变福利目标可以以很小的平均性能损失改善最差服务州，同时产生可解释的症状信号变化。

Conclusion: PopFS为跨异质人群的共享特征选择提供了一种可扩展且可解释的解决方案，在保护弱势群体和保持总体性能之间实现了有效平衡，同时保持了各人群独立建模的灵活性。

Abstract: Choosing which features to collect is a deployment decision: the same limited questionnaire, test panel, or sensor set may need to serve several heterogeneous populations. Standard feature-selection methods typically optimize for one large population, while existing robust approaches tend to learn one shared model for every population. We introduce PopFS, a method for learning one shared, deployable feature set that is robust to population differences while letting each pop- ulation train its own model. PopFS uses a tunable welfare objective that lets practitioners balance overall predictive ben- efit against stronger protection of the populations that benefit least. To make this objective practical at scale, PopFS first uses multitask sparse learning to reduce the candidate pool, then searches directly over hard feature sets by ranking promising additions and swaps and fully refitting only a shortlist. Across eight population splits from six prediction tasks drawn from five tabular and public-health datasets, PopFS consistently achieves strong average and worst-population performance while scaling to thousands of candidate features. A 43-state COVID-19 nowcasting study further shows that changing the welfare objective can improve the least-served states with lit- tle change in average performance and yields an interpretable change in the selected symptom signals. Our code is available at https://github.com/Rachel-Lyu/PopFS.

</details>


### [154] [Robust Counterfactual Policy Optimisation via Nondeterministic Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02893)
*Jessica Lally,Milad Kazemi,Nicola Paoletti,David Watson,Sander Beckers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将反事实策略优化形式化扩展到概率非确定性因果模型（即随机MDP），在敏感性分析框架下识别鲁棒反事实策略，并在败血症治疗模拟器上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实推理方法通常假设确定性因果模型，将所有随机性归因于潜变量，但马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）本质上是随机的。这导致反事实策略优化在真实随机环境中缺乏理论支撑与实际可行的优化方案。

Method: 形式化概率非确定性因果模型下的反事实策略优化问题，将潜在混淆与不可约随机性明确分离，并提出首个在敏感性分析框架下识别鲁棒反事实策略的实际优化问题。

Result: 在败血症治疗模拟器上验证了方法有效性，其中糖尿病状态作为隐藏的全局混淆因子。

Conclusion: 通过将反事实策略优化推广至随机MDP情境，并采用敏感性分析保证鲁棒性，为在真实随机序列决策环境中应用反事实推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Counterfactual inference approaches for sequential decision-making typically assume deterministic causal models, where all randomness stems from latent variables. However, Markov Decision Processes (MDPs) are inherently stochastic. We address this by formalising counterfactual policy optimisation under probabilistic nondeterministic causal models, which properly separates latent confounding from irreducible stochasticity, and here propose a first practical optimisation problem for identifying robust counterfactual policies under a sensitivity analysis framework. We validate our approach on a sepsis treatment simulator, where diabetes status acts as a hidden global confounder.

</details>


### [155] [AnchorKV: Anchor-Residual KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02901)
*Malik Khalaf,Yara Shamshoum,Nitzan Hodos,Yuval Sieradzki,Assaf Schuster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AnchorKV是一种KV缓存压缩方案，通过锚点表示所有token，实现20倍压缩率而不丢弃任何token，在70B规模模型上保留99%的完整缓存准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文LLM推理中，KV缓存是主要内存瓶颈。现有的压缩方法（逐出法和量化法）各有缺陷：逐出法永久丢弃token，可能导致关键信息丢失损害性能；量化法保留所有token但压缩率有限。需要一个既能大幅压缩又不损失信息的方法。

Method: AnchorKV采用锚点（anchor）机制：用一小部分精确存储的锚点表示整个缓存，每个其他token通过其最相似的锚点来表达（近似），仅对最能影响模型输出的近似token进行精化（refinement）。这种方法实现了20倍压缩且不丢弃任何token。

Result: 在多个模型和数据集上，AnchorKV一致地保持了准确率，在70B规模模型上保留了99%的完整缓存得分，同时将整个上下文的成本降至原来的很小一部分。

Conclusion: AnchorKV通过"保留所有token但以锚点近似表示"的策略，在不损失精度的情况下实现了远超量化方法的大幅压缩，为长上下文LLM推理的KV缓存压缩提供了一种有效的折中方案。

Abstract: The key-value (KV) cache is the primary memory bottleneck in long-context LLM inference. Existing approaches attack it from opposite ends: eviction methods permanently discard tokens, degrading performance whenever a discarded token later proves essential, while quantization methods retain all tokens at low precision but offer limited compression. We propose AnchorKV, a compression scheme that shrinks the cache by $20\times$ without discarding a single token. AnchorKV represents the cache using a small set of anchors stored exactly, expresses every other token through its most similar anchor, and refines only those whose approximation most affects the model's output. AnchorKV consistently preserves accuracy across models and datasets, retaining 99% of the full-cache score at the 70B scale, while keeping the entire context at a fraction of its cost.

</details>


### [156] [Bayesian Data Reweighting Improves Multimodal Retrieval for Knowledge-Based Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02907)
*Jingchen Sun,Shaobo Han,Ruiyi Zhang,Naresh Kumar Devulapally,Ming Liu,Yitao Long,Vishnu Suresh Lokhande,Changyou Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出贝叶斯数据加权（Bayesian Data Reweighting）框架，通过将查询-文档重要性建模为潜在变量，自适应推断后验权重来降低假阴性样本的影响，从而提升多模态检索器在知识型视觉问答中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态检索器对比训练将所有不匹配的查询-文档对视为同等信息量的负样本，但许多不匹配的文档可能具有语义相关性或部分有用性，作为假阴性样本会影响训练效果。

Method: 采用概率框架将查询-文档重要性建模为潜在变量，在共轭先验下推导闭式后验更新，并结合随机EM（期望最大化）优化来自适应推断后验权重，从而降低假阴性样本的权重。

Result: 在三种检索器和七个知识型VQA基准上一致提升了检索准确率。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯数据加权方法有效解决了对比训练中假阴性样本的干扰问题，在不同检索架构和多个基准上均表现出稳定且一致的性能提升。

Abstract: Multimodal retrievers are essential for knowledge-based visual question answering, where they retrieve external evidence for image-question pairs. However, existing contrastive training methods typically treat all unmatched query-document pairs as equally informative negatives, which is problematic because many unmatched documents may still be semantically relevant or partially useful. We propose Bayesian Data Reweighting, a probabilistic framework that models query-document importance as latent variables and adaptively infers posterior weights to downweight likely false negatives. With closed-form posterior updates under conjugate priors and stochastic EM optimization, our method consistently improves retrieval accuracy across three retrievers and seven knowledge-based VQA benchmarks.

</details>


### [157] [Forecasting Revenue with its Customer-Base Drivers: When and Why Coordination Helps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02911)
*Kyeongbin Kim,Daniel McCarthy,Dokyun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出客户基础多任务Transformer（CBMT），利用966家公司的周度交易面板数据，学习客户基础各原始预测分量（获取、重复购买、每单消费）的共享结构并协调其与下游总收入的匹配。CBMT的总销售额误差比最强基准低30%，在74.3%的企业上优于单任务预测，并在23/24项结果对比中源误差更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 收入预测指导收购预算、需求规划和基于客户的估值，但聚合预测无法揭示变化是源于获取、重复购买、每单支出还是抵消性运动。现有模型仅预测总量而丢失了这些组成部分的语义与诊断信息，因此需要一种能够同时生成协调的、可解释的、且保持各分量准确性的预测方法。

Method: 提出Customer-Based Multi-task Transformer（CBMT）：（1）通过Transformer学习客户基础各原始预测分量之间的共享结构；（2）保留每个分量的独立预测；（3）通过额外层将分量组合并对齐到下游总收入。在966家公司的周度交易面板（25个行业）上进行训练与验证。

Result: CBMT的总销售额平均误差比最强的代表性客户基准低30%；比直接预测总销售额的Transformer低2.65%（配对差异不显著，p=.222）；在74.3%的企业上优于分别估计的单任务预测。源MAE在23/24项基准对比中更低，剩余一项差异与零无显著区别。客户基础动态高度波动时所有模型准确性下降，CBMT的优势收窄；校准期路由规则未能改善平均准确性。

Conclusion: 协调的客户基础预测能够支持收入规划，但需要谨慎：当客户基础动态高度波动时其优势减弱，并且联合预测的收益与企业原始分量共动强度相关。研究结果同时说明了共享表示与收入对齐带来的增益属于诊断性而非因果证据。

Abstract: Revenue forecasts guide acquisition budgets, demand planning, and customer-based valuations, yet an aggregate forecast does not show whether change reflects acquisition, repeat purchasing, spending per order, or offsetting movements. Using weekly transaction panels for 966 companies in 25 industries, the authors develop the Customer-Based Multi-task Transformer (CBMT), which learns shared structure, retains separate primitive forecasts, and aligns their combination with downstream revenue. CBMT's mean total-sales error is 30% below the strongest representative established customer-base benchmark. It is also 2.65% below a Transformer that forecasts total sales directly, although the paired difference is not statistically significant (p=.222), and it beats separately estimated single-task forecasts for 74.3% of firms. CBMT's source MAE is lower in 23 of 24 benchmark-by-outcome comparisons, with the remaining difference not statistically distinguishable from zero. Firms whose primitives co-move more strongly are more likely to benefit from joint forecasting; selected-family scenario-3 comparisons are consistent with gains from shared representation and revenue alignment but remain diagnostic rather than causal. Accuracy deteriorates for all models when customer-base dynamics are highly volatile, and CBMT's advantage narrows there. Calibration-period routing rules do not improve average accuracy over always deploying CBMT. The results show how coordinated customer-base forecasts support revenue planning and when they warrant greater caution.

</details>


### [158] [When Should Graph Attention Be Sparse? Learning a Per-Edge Tsallis Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02938)
*Kleyton da Costa,Bernardo Modenesi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LTGA（可学习Tsallis图注意力），通过学习Tsallis熵指数q在重尾、softmax和紧支撑注意力之间连续插值，在不同粒度上灵活调整注意力形状以适应同质/异质图。实验表明LTGA-Edge平均排名最佳，但统计上并未显著优于基线；学习的q主要带来单次运行免网格搜索的优势，并具有可解释的剪枝机制（剪除42%的错误边，恢复它们损失7.1点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准图注意力（GAT）用softmax归一化邻域分数，这是Shannon统计下的最大熵选择，但同质图和异质图需要不同形状的注意力分布，单一固定归一化无法兼顾两者。

Method: 提出LTGA层，将Tsallis熵指数q作为可与网络权重联合学习的参数，在全局标量到逐边指数四种粒度上连续插值不同形状的注意力；采用有界重参数化，使模型从GAT基线初始化出发。

Result: 在8个基准、10个种子下，LTGA-Edge取得最佳平均排名（2.75），但omnibus检验p=0.199未达显著；学习q未胜过搜索q（验证集冻结网格61.4%、调优α-entmax 62.2%、容量匹配q≡1对照62.0%，LTGA-Edge为61.7%）。learned q带来单次运行免网格搜索，且q偏离1时剪除42%的注意力系数到精确零，剪除的是选择性错误的边，恢复它们损失7.1点，而随机剪除同比例损失13.0点。

Conclusion: LTGA的核心价值不在于显著提升精度，而在于（1）以单次训练替代网格搜索q，（2）提供可解释机制——学习的q通过选择性剪枝错误边来工作，揭示了可学习的图注意力形状所带来的可解释洞察。

Abstract: Graph attention normalizes neighborhood scores with softmax, the maximum-entropy choice under Shannon statistics. But homophilic and heterophilic graphs want different attention shapes, and one fixed normalization cannot serve both. We propose \textbf{LTGA} (\textbf{L}earnable \textbf{T}sallis \textbf{G}raph \textbf{A}ttention), a graph attention layer whose Tsallis entropic index $q$ is learned jointly with the weights, interpolating continuously between heavy-tailed ($q\!<\!1$), softmax ($q\!=\!1$) and compact-support ($q\!>\!1$) attention at four granularities from a global scalar to a per-edge index, under a bounded reparameterization that starts every model at the GAT baseline. Across eight benchmarks at ten seeds, LTGA-Edge takes the best average rank ($2.75$), but the omnibus test does not reject ($p\!=\!0.199$) and learning $q$ does not beat searching it: a validation-tuned frozen grid reaches $61.4\%$, tuned $α$-entmax $62.2\%$ and a capacity-matched $q\!\equiv\!1$ control $62.0\%$, against $61.7\%$ for LTGA-Edge. What the learned index buys is one run instead of a grid, and an interpretable mechanism: where $q$ leaves $1$, it prunes $42\%$ of attention coefficients to exactly zero, and those edges are selectively the wrong ones, restoring them costs $7.1$ points, while random pruning at the same rate costs $13.0$ more. Project page: https://kleyt0n.github.io/ltga

</details>


### [159] [Federated generative event models for tokenized electronic health records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02939)
*Michael C. Burkhart,Luke Solo,Inhyeok Lee,S'Khaja Charles,Zewei "Whiskey" Liao,Kaveri Chhikara,Dema Therese,Wan-Ting Liao,Catherine A. Gao,William F. Parker,Brett K. Beaulieu-Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究评估了跨三个独立医疗系统的122,251例ICU住院数据上，联邦训练的分词化生成事件模型（GEMs）的性能。GEMs在院内和跨院预测任务上均优于传统监督模型，联邦学习（FedAvg/FedAvgM）接近集中式训练的性能，且大多数收益在5-10轮通信内获得。然而，集中式多站点训练的增益有限，多站点模型仅在本地数据有限时更有用。主要开放挑战是学习可迁移的表征以将更大但异质的多系统数据转化为可靠的目标站点收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录基础模型受限于机构数据孤岛和跨站点迁移时显著的性能退化。本文旨在探索联邦训练是否能在不共享原始数据的情况下，缓解数据孤岛问题并提升模型的跨站点可迁移性，从而验证联邦GEM训练的技术可行性。

Method: 使用统一为Common Longitudinal ICU Data Format的三个独立医疗系统的122,251例ICU住院数据，训练分词化生成事件模型（GEMs）。在12个入院后24小时以上的临床预测任务上，采用院内、跨院、集中式和联邦（FedAvg和FedAvgM）四种训练配置进行评估，并与LightGBM等传统监督模型进行对比。

Result: GEMs在院内和跨院平均ROC-AUC上表现最佳，跨站点性能损失（ROC-AUC 0.025、PR-AUC 0.027）远小于LightGBM（0.079和0.089）。联邦学习接近集中式GEM训练的性能，大部分收益在5-10轮通信内获得。但集中式多站点训练的改进有限，多站点模型仅在本地训练数据不足时才有明显优势。

Conclusion: 联邦GEM训练在技术上可行且能保留大部分集中式训练性能，但当前的核心挑战在于学习可迁移的表征，以便将来自多个医疗系统的更大但异质的数据转化为可靠的目标站点收益。多站点建模的价值具有条件性，取决于目标站点的本地数据规模。

Abstract: Electronic health record foundation models are limited by institutionally siloed data and substantial performance degradation under cross-site transfer. We evaluated federated training of tokenized generative event models (GEMs) across 122,251 intensive care hospitalizations from three independent health systems harmonized to the Common Longitudinal ICU Data Format. Models were assessed on 12 post-24-hour clinical prediction tasks using within-site, cross-site, centralized, and federated training configurations. GEMs achieved the highest mean within-site and cross-site ROC-AUC and were substantially more transportable than conventional supervised models: their average cross-site penalties were 0.025 ROC-AUC and 0.027 PR-AUC, compared with 0.079 and 0.089 for LightGBM. Federated Learning (FedAvg and FedAvgM) approached the performance of centralized GEM training, with most gains obtained within 5-10 communication rounds. However, centralized multi-site training provided only modest improvements over complete local training. Multi-site models were most useful when local training data were limited, with their advantage narrowing as institutional data accumulated. These findings show that federated GEM training is technically feasible and preserves most centralized performance, but that the main open challenge is learning transportable representations to translate larger, but heterogeneous data from multiple health systems into a reliable target-site benefit.

</details>


### [160] [Sedentary Behavior Classification for Wearable Sensors with a CNN-BiLSTM Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.02946)
*Yuliang Chen,Weiwei Shi,Jingjing Zou,Rong Zablocki,Animesh Kumar,Jordan A. Carlson,Sheri J. Hartman,Mikael Anne Greenwood-Hickman,Paul R. Hibbing,Marta Jankowska,Jay Yang,Arun Kumar,Loki Natarajan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将基于髋部佩戴加速度计数据训练的深度学习模型迁移到腕部佩戴加速度计数据上，用于久坐行为（坐与非坐）分类。使用CHAP模型（CNN-BiLSTM架构），在iWatch数据集上评估零样本性能和不同微调策略，发现髋部预训练模型在髋部数据上表现优异但腕部准确率下降，微调CHAP优于从头训练的Transformer模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 久坐行为与健康风险密切相关，但腕部佩戴传感器的姿态分类仍然具有挑战性。本研究旨在探索是否可以将髋部加速度计数据训练出的深度学习模型迁移到腕部数据，以解决传感器佩戴位置差异带来的分类性能下降问题。

Method: 使用CHAP模型（CNN-BiLSTM架构，最初为髋部加速度计设计），在iWatch数据集上评估两种策略：(1) 零样本迁移，直接将髋部训练模型应用于腕部数据；(2) 微调适应，使用不同数量的带标签腕部数据对模型进行微调。同时与从头训练的Transformer模型进行比较。

Result: 髋部训练模型在髋部数据上无需重新训练即可保持较强性能，但在腕部数据上因传感器放置位置变化导致准确率下降。对CHAP进行微调相较于从头训练的Transformer模型始终具有更优表现。

Conclusion: 髋部预训练为腕部部署提供了有用的起点，但需要进行腕部特定的适应以应对更高的信号变异性，从而确保模型在腕部佩戴场景下的准确分类。

Abstract: Accurate detection of sedentary behavior is important for studying health risks related to prolonged sitting, but posture-based classification remains challenging with wearable sensors, especially at the wrist.
